# Pulsix v0.2a Inventory Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Inventory groups with a typed variable schema builder, host assignment through schema-generated forms, an encrypted credential store with SSH key generation and public-key snippets, SSH jump hosts with inheritance, per-host SSH settings, and a masked Ansible inventory preview rendered by the same code the worker will use in v0.2b.

**Architecture:** Three domain packages follow the v0.1 layering (handler → service → Store interface → sqlc adapter): `internal/credentials` (sealed secrets, SSH keys), `internal/sshconf` (jump hosts, zone defaults, per-host SSH), `internal/inventory` (groups, schema, values, memberships, the renderer service). Three pure packages carry the logic spec §18 marks critical and are tested table-driven at ≥ 90 %: `internal/inventory/schema` (field model, schema check, value validation, defaults, secret markers), `internal/inventory/grouptree` (tree queries), `internal/inventory/render` (Ansible dynamic inventory, bastion resolution). The SPA renders every form from the same schema JSON the server validates against.

**Tech Stack:** as v0.1 (Go 1.27, chi v5, huma v2, pgx v5, sqlc, goose v3, testcontainers-go, React 19, TanStack Router/Query, Tailwind v4, Vitest, Playwright) plus `golang.org/x/crypto/ssh` for key handling.

## Where this plan sits: v0.2 is three plans

Milestone v0.2 "Inventory & Ansible" spans three independent subsystems. Each gets its own plan that ends in working, tested software:

| Plan | Backlog items | Delivers |
|------|---------------|----------|
| **v0.2a Inventory** (this plan) | 2.1–2.6, 2.7 (store, usage, expiry; rotation moves to v0.2b), 2.7a, 2.7b (key generation, visible public key, snippets) | Groups, schema builder, host assignment, credentials, jump hosts, host SSH settings, inventory preview |
| v0.2b Execution | 2.7b rest (bootstrap playbook, connection test, host-key TOFU + pinning, daily re-test), key rotation, 2.8–2.12a, 2.14 | Worker binary, internal CA with gRPC/mTLS, Postgres job queue, Git repos, editor playbooks, job templates, live runs, the Ansible E2E |
| v0.2c Automation | 2.13–2.13c | Schedules, webhook triggers, schedule notifications, calendar |

The user decided on 2026-09-11 to start v0.2 before the v0.1 release blockers (backlog 1.20–1.22); those stay open and are not part of this plan.

## Global Constraints

- Language: all code, comments, commit messages, docs in English. Chat with the user in German.
- Commits: Conventional Commits (`feat`, `fix`, `docs`, `refactor`, `test`, `build`, `ci`, `chore`), footer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Commit only what a task tells you to commit. Work happens on branch `feat/v0.2a-inventory`; history on `main` stays linear.
- Go 1.27, module path `github.com/noixdev/pulsix`. `gofmt` + `golangci-lint` clean. `go test -race ./...` green before every commit.
- IDs are UUIDv7 (`uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())`). Table and column names `snake_case`.
- Every new org-owned table has `org_id uuid NOT NULL`, `ENABLE` + `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`, and the policy pair in its final form: `org_isolation USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid)` and `org_isolation_bypass USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '')`.
- Migrations are additive only (new tables, new columns, new constraints). Numbers `00013`–`00015` belong to this plan.
- Every store is built on `*db.DB` (never its pool), so each statement runs inside the request's organization scope.
- References between org-owned rows use composite foreign keys `(org_id, x_id) REFERENCES t(org_id, id)`, so a row can never point into another organization.
- Secrets at rest use AES-256-GCM with the 32-byte `PULSIX_MASTER_KEY` through `crypto.Box.SealAAD`, with additional authenticated data that names the owning row. Secret values never appear in any API response, log line, audit summary, or query cache in the SPA.
- The server-side schema validator is the authority. The SPA renders forms from the same schema JSON and shows the server's problems at the field they name.
- **Layering (from `CLAUDE.md`):** handlers in `internal/api` only decode, call a service and encode. Business rules live in domain services that depend on a `Store` interface declared next to them. Unit tests use fakes; integration tests use the real store through `internal/db/testdb`; API tests go through `internal/api/apitest`. Constructors inject dependencies, including the clock and randomness. No package globals beyond compiled regexps and constants.
- Every mutating route carries `withAudit(operationID, objectType)`; `TestEveryMutatingRouteIsAuditedOrAllowlisted` must stay green without new allowlist entries.
- Coverage gates: Go packages ≥ 80 %; `internal/auth`, `internal/tenancy`, `internal/db`, `internal/inventory/schema`, `internal/inventory/grouptree`, `internal/inventory/render`, `internal/credentials` ≥ 90 %; SPA ≥ 70 %.
- Status colours (`ok #34D399`, `warn #FBBF24`, `crit #F87171`, `unknown #6B7280`) are reserved for states. The accent `#00E5FF` is for actions and selection. Hostnames, variable names and values use JetBrains Mono.
- **Dependencies are current, never stale.** Every dependency uses the newest stable release, or the active LTS line where one exists. Versions in this plan are a floor. Verify at implementation time (`go list -m -versions`, `npm view <pkg> version`), use the newest, and record in your report what you pinned. Never introduce a deprecated API. The TypeScript 5.9 pin in `docs/dependency-exceptions.md` still applies.
- End-user documentation goes to `~/_dev/noix-docs` on branch `pulsix`, committed locally and **never pushed** (user decision). Public texts never name the tech stack.

## Decisions taken in this plan

- **Column `vars`, not `values`.** `VALUES` is an SQL keyword. `inventory_groups.vars` holds group-scoped values; `inventory_memberships.vars` holds host-scoped values and overrides. The API and the spec keep the word "values" for the concept.
- **Collections count with `min_items`/`max_items`.** `min`/`max` mean a number range for `int`/`float` and a length in characters for `string`. The spec §6 example's `"min": 1` on a repeater becomes `"min_items": 1` (Task 12 amends the spec).
- **Names.** Field names match `^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]{0,63}$` and top-level names must not start with `ansible_` or `pulsix_`, so a schema can never shadow a connection variable or the facts. Group names match `^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]{0,62}$` (valid Ansible group names) and must not be `all`, `ungrouped` or `_meta` (the last added by the final review: it collides with the dynamic inventory's `_meta.hostvars`). Group names are unique per organization because Ansible's group namespace is flat.
- **Host-scoped fields are asked for direct members only.** A host assigned to `webserver` answers `webserver`'s host fields. It inherits the group-scoped values of `webserver`'s ancestors through Ansible's own group inheritance, but it does not answer an ancestor's host fields unless it is assigned to that ancestor too.
- **Secret wire format.** Stored inside a vars document as `{"$sealed": "<base64 ciphertext>"}`. Returned on reads as `{"$secret": "set"}`. A write sends a plaintext string for a new value, or `{"$secret": "keep", "from": "<path>"}` to keep the value stored at `<path>` when the form was loaded, so a repeater item that moves keeps its secret. A client can never write `$sealed` directly. The AAD names the row (`inventory-group:<org>:<group>` or `inventory-member:<org>:<group>:<host>`), not the path, so a moved value still opens and can never be replayed into another row.
- **Paths** use one format everywhere, server and SPA: `vhosts[1].basic_auth.password`. Schema problems use index paths into the field list: `fields[0].fields[2].name`.
- **Orphaned values.** A schema edit never rewrites stored values. Reads list the orphaned top-level keys. The renderer exports only declared fields. The next save of that form sends only declared fields, which drops the orphans. Nothing is destroyed silently.
- **Hosts in several groups.** Membership values merge in order of group depth (shallow first), then group name. The later group wins, and a key set to different values by two groups produces a preview warning.
- **Jump host resolution.** Host `explicit` or `none` wins. Otherwise, for each group the host is a direct member of, walk up the tree to the nearest group with a jump host; the candidate whose defining group is deepest wins, ties broken by group name, and tied candidates that disagree produce a warning. Otherwise the zone default. Otherwise none. Chains are at most 4 hops, cycles are rejected on write, and a chain renders as `ansible_ssh_common_args: -o ProxyJump=u1@a1:p1,u2@a2:p2` (first hop first).
- **Only managed hosts** can be assigned to inventory groups, and only managed hosts are rendered. A membership whose host lost the capability stays stored and is skipped with a warning whenever that host would otherwise be in the rendered scope (whole organization, a group scope that contains the membership's group, and/or a host list that names the host).
- **Optimistic concurrency (first step of backlog 1.31).** Groups carry `revision`. `PATCH`, `PUT …/schema` and `PUT …/vars` require `If-Match: "<revision>"`: missing is 428, stale is 412. Every group response carries `ETag`. Membership writes accept an optional `If-Match`.
- **Permissions.** New entries in the fixed matrix: `inventory.read` (viewer), `inventory.write` (operator), `credentials.read` (operator), `credentials.write` (admin). Jump host objects are written with `credentials.write`. Assigning a jump host to a zone, group or host is `inventory.write`.
- **Credential kinds for v0.2a:** `ssh_key`, `ssh_password`, `git_key`, `git_token`. Channel secrets arrive with v0.4. Generated keys are ed25519. Imports accept ed25519, ECDSA and RSA ≥ 2048 bits; DSA is rejected. A passphrase-protected import is decrypted once and stored sealed without the passphrase. Private keys, passwords and tokens are never returned. The public key is stored without a comment; the API composes the comment `pulsix:<org-slug>:<credential-name>` on read, so renaming a credential updates it.
- **Forms are controlled components**, not react-hook-form. The schema is only known at runtime and nests to arbitrary depth; one recursive renderer over a plain value tree is simpler to test than nested `useFieldArray` hooks. This replaces the react-hook-form mention in spec §15 for schema-driven forms (Task 12 amends the spec).
- **Renderer scope.** `render.Scope{GroupID, HostIDs}`: both empty renders every managed host; `GroupID` selects the members of that group and its descendants; `HostIDs` selects those hosts; both together intersect. A rendered group appears only if one of its selected hosts is in its subtree, plus its ancestors. v0.2b's job snapshot uses the same function with secrets revealed.

### Task 1: Variable schema package

The field model from spec §6, the check that a schema is well-formed, strict and lenient value validation, defaults, and the secret markers. Pure Go, no I/O. This is the package every later task builds on, so its exported names below are binding.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/inventory/schema/schema.go`
- Create: `internal/inventory/schema/validate.go`
- Create: `internal/inventory/schema/secrets.go`
- Create: `internal/inventory/schema/resolve.go`
- Test: `internal/inventory/schema/schema_test.go`, `validate_test.go`, `secrets_test.go`, `resolve_test.go`
- Modify: `scripts/coverage-gate.sh` (the 90 % floor list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (package `schema`):
  - `type Type string` with `TypeString, TypeInt, TypeFloat, TypeBool, TypeEnum, TypeSecret, TypeList, TypeRepeater, TypeObject`
  - `type Scope string` with `ScopeGroup, ScopeHost`
  - `type Field struct{ Name, Label, Description string; Type Type; Scope Scope; Required, Overridable bool; Default any; Options []string; Min, Max *float64; Pattern string; MinItems, MaxItems *int; Item *Field; Fields []Field }` (JSON tags below are the wire contract)
  - `type Schema struct{ Fields []Field }`; methods `Check() error`, `GroupFields() []Field`, `HostFields() []Field`, `MembershipFields() []Field`; `Field.EffectiveScope() Scope`
  - `type Problem struct{ Path, Message string }`, `type ValidationError struct{ Problems []Problem }`
  - `Validate(fields []Field, values map[string]any) error` (strict), `ValidateStored(fields []Field, values map[string]any) error` (ignores undeclared keys)
  - `Sealed(ciphertext []byte) map[string]any`, `SealedBytes(v any) ([]byte, bool)`, `Mask() map[string]any`, `KeepFrom(v any) (string, bool)`, `MaskVars(values map[string]any) map[string]any`
  - `type SecretFunc func(path string, v any) (any, error)`, `MapSecrets(fields []Field, values map[string]any, fn SecretFunc) (map[string]any, error)`
  - `Lookup(values map[string]any, path string) (any, bool)`
  - `Resolve(fields []Field, values map[string]any) map[string]any`, `Orphans(fields []Field, values map[string]any) []string`
  - Limits `MaxDepth = 4`, `MaxFields = 100`, `MaxOptions = 100`, `MaxPatternLen = 512`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests for the schema check**

`internal/inventory/schema/schema_test.go`:

```go
package schema_test

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

func ptr[T any](v T) *T { return &v }

// specExample is spec §6's example schema (with min_items instead of the
// example's min on a repeater) plus two group-scoped fields.
func specExample() schema.Schema {
	return schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{
		{
			Name: "vhosts", Label: "Virtual hosts", Type: schema.TypeRepeater, Scope: schema.ScopeHost,
			Required: true, MinItems: ptr(1), Description: "Virtual hosts served by this host",
			Fields: []schema.Field{
				{Name: "domain", Type: schema.TypeString, Required: true, Pattern: `^[a-z0-9.-]+$`},
				{Name: "php_version", Type: schema.TypeEnum, Options: []string{"8.2", "8.3", "8.4"}},
				{Name: "ssl", Type: schema.TypeBool, Default: true},
				{Name: "aliases", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeString}},
				{Name: "basic_auth", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{
					{Name: "user", Type: schema.TypeString},
					{Name: "password", Type: schema.TypeSecret},
				}},
			},
		},
		{Name: "php_default", Type: schema.TypeEnum, Options: []string{"8.3", "8.4"}, Default: "8.3", Overridable: true},
		{Name: "max_upload_mb", Type: schema.TypeInt, Min: ptr(1.0), Max: ptr(2048.0), Default: 64.0},
	}}
}

// nest returns an object field with levels object levels, the innermost
// holding one string field.
func nest(levels int) schema.Field {
	f := schema.Field{Name: "leaf", Type: schema.TypeString}
	for i := 0; i < levels; i++ {
		f = schema.Field{Name: fmt.Sprintf("o%d", i), Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{f}}
	}
	return f
}

func problemsOf(t *testing.T, err error) []schema.Problem {
	t.Helper()
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	if !errors.As(err, &ve) {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want *schema.ValidationError", err)
	}
	return ve.Problems
}

func hasProblem(ps []schema.Problem, path, substr string) bool {
	for _, p := range ps {
		if p.Path == path && strings.Contains(p.Message, substr) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func TestCheckAcceptsSpecExample(t *testing.T) {
	if err := specExample().Check(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Check: %v", err)
	}
	if err := (schema.Schema{}).Check(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("empty schema: %v", err)
	}
	if err := (schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{nest(4)}}).Check(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("four levels: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCheckRejects(t *testing.T) {
	str := schema.Field{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString}
	cases := []struct {
		name   string
		fields []schema.Field
		path   string
		msg    string
	}{
		{"bad name", []schema.Field{{Name: "Bad-Name", Type: schema.TypeString}}, "fields[0].name", "lowercase"},
		{"reserved prefix", []schema.Field{{Name: "ansible_user", Type: schema.TypeString}}, "fields[0].name", "ansible_"},
		{"duplicate", []schema.Field{str, str}, "fields[1].name", "duplicate"},
		{"unknown type", []schema.Field{{Name: "x", Type: "date"}}, "fields[0].type", "unknown type"},
		{"bad scope", []schema.Field{{Name: "x", Type: schema.TypeString, Scope: "zone"}}, "fields[0].scope", "group or host"},
		{"overridable host field", []schema.Field{{Name: "x", Type: schema.TypeString, Scope: schema.ScopeHost, Overridable: true}}, "fields[0].overridable", "group-scoped"},
		{"nested scope", []schema.Field{{Name: "o", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "x", Type: schema.TypeString, Scope: schema.ScopeHost}}}}, "fields[0].fields[0].scope", "top-level"},
		{"nested overridable", []schema.Field{{Name: "o", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "x", Type: schema.TypeString, Overridable: true}}}}, "fields[0].fields[0].overridable", "top-level"},
		{"enum without options", []schema.Field{{Name: "e", Type: schema.TypeEnum}}, "fields[0].options", "at least one option"},
		{"enum duplicate option", []schema.Field{{Name: "e", Type: schema.TypeEnum, Options: []string{"a", "a"}}}, "fields[0].options", "duplicate"},
		{"enum empty option", []schema.Field{{Name: "e", Type: schema.TypeEnum, Options: []string{""}}}, "fields[0].options", "empty"},
		{"options on string", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Options: []string{"a"}}}, "fields[0].options", "enum fields only"},
		{"min above max", []schema.Field{{Name: "n", Type: schema.TypeInt, Min: ptr(5.0), Max: ptr(1.0)}}, "fields[0].max", "less than min"},
		{"min on list", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeString}, Min: ptr(1.0)}}, "fields[0].min", "min_items"},
		{"fractional string length", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Min: ptr(1.5)}}, "fields[0].min", "whole numbers"},
		{"bad pattern", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Pattern: "("}}, "fields[0].pattern", "invalid regular expression"},
		{"long pattern", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Pattern: strings.Repeat("a", schema.MaxPatternLen+1)}}, "fields[0].pattern", "at most"},
		{"pattern on int", []schema.Field{{Name: "n", Type: schema.TypeInt, Pattern: "^1$"}}, "fields[0].pattern", "string fields only"},
		{"min_items on string", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, MinItems: ptr(1)}}, "fields[0].min_items", "list and repeater"},
		{"negative min_items", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeInt}, MinItems: ptr(-1)}}, "fields[0].min_items", "≥ 0"},
		{"min_items above max_items", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeInt}, MinItems: ptr(3), MaxItems: ptr(1)}}, "fields[0].max_items", "less than min_items"},
		{"list without item", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList}}, "fields[0].item", "item type"},
		{"item on string", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeString}}}, "fields[0].item", "list fields only"},
		{"list of objects", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeObject}}}, "fields[0].item.type", "must be string"},
		{"named list item", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Name: "x", Type: schema.TypeString}}}, "fields[0].item.name", "no name"},
		{"required list item", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeString, Required: true}}}, "fields[0].item", "only type and validation"},
		{"enum item without options", []schema.Field{{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeEnum}}}, "fields[0].item.options", "at least one option"},
		{"repeater without fields", []schema.Field{{Name: "r", Type: schema.TypeRepeater}}, "fields[0].fields", "at least one sub-field"},
		{"fields on string", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Fields: []schema.Field{str}}}, "fields[0].fields", "repeater and object"},
		{"nested duplicate", []schema.Field{{Name: "o", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{str, str}}}, "fields[0].fields[1].name", "duplicate"},
		{"secret default", []schema.Field{{Name: "p", Type: schema.TypeSecret, Default: "x"}}, "fields[0].default", "cannot have a default"},
		{"object default", []schema.Field{{Name: "o", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{str}, Default: map[string]any{}}}, "fields[0].default", "cannot have a default"},
		{"default of wrong type", []schema.Field{{Name: "n", Type: schema.TypeInt, Default: "ten"}}, "fields[0].default", "whole number"},
		{"too deep", []schema.Field{nest(5)}, "fields[0].fields[0].fields[0].fields[0].fields[0].fields", "levels deep"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			ps := problemsOf(t, schema.Schema{Fields: c.fields}.Check())
			if !hasProblem(ps, c.path, c.msg) {
				t.Fatalf("problems = %+v, want %q at %q", ps, c.msg, c.path)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCheckRejectsTooManyFields(t *testing.T) {
	fields := make([]schema.Field, schema.MaxFields+1)
	for i := range fields {
		fields[i] = schema.Field{Name: fmt.Sprintf("f%d", i), Type: schema.TypeString}
	}
	ps := problemsOf(t, schema.Schema{Fields: fields}.Check())
	if !hasProblem(ps, "", "at most 100 fields") {
		t.Fatalf("problems = %+v", ps)
	}
}

func TestScopeHelpers(t *testing.T) {
	s := specExample()
	names := func(fs []schema.Field) string {
		out := make([]string, len(fs))
		for i, f := range fs {
			out[i] = f.Name
		}
		return strings.Join(out, ",")
	}
	if got := names(s.GroupFields()); got != "php_default,max_upload_mb" {
		t.Errorf("GroupFields = %s", got)
	}
	if got := names(s.HostFields()); got != "vhosts" {
		t.Errorf("HostFields = %s", got)
	}
	m := s.MembershipFields()
	if got := names(m); got != "vhosts,php_default" {
		t.Fatalf("MembershipFields = %s", got)
	}
	if m[1].Required || m[1].Default != nil {
		t.Errorf("an override must be optional with no schema default: %+v", m[1])
	}
	if s.Fields[1].Default != "8.3" {
		t.Errorf("MembershipFields mutated the schema: %+v", s.Fields[1])
	}
	if (schema.Field{}).EffectiveScope() != schema.ScopeGroup {
		t.Error("an unset scope must mean group")
	}
}
```

```go
package schema_test

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"testing"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

func vhost(domain string, extra map[string]any) map[string]any {
	m := map[string]any{"domain": domain}
	for k, v := range extra {
		m[k] = v
	}
	return m
}

func TestValidate(t *testing.T) {
	host := specExample().HostFields()
	group := specExample().GroupFields()
	str := schema.Field{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString}
	list := schema.Field{Name: "l", Type: schema.TypeList, Item: &schema.Field{Type: schema.TypeString}, MaxItems: ptr(1)}
	cases := []struct {
		name     string
		fields   []schema.Field
		values   map[string]any
		problems map[string]string // path -> message substring; nil means valid
	}{
		{"valid", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("acme.io", map[string]any{
			"php_version": "8.3", "aliases": []any{"www.acme.io"},
			"basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u", "password": "p"},
		})}}, nil},
		{"required missing", host, map[string]any{}, map[string]string{"vhosts": "is required"}},
		{"null counts as missing", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": nil}, map[string]string{"vhosts": "is required"}},
		{"too few items", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{}}, map[string]string{"vhosts": "at least 1"}},
		{"nested required and pattern", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("acme.io", nil), vhost("Bad Domain", nil), map[string]any{}}},
			map[string]string{"vhosts[1].domain": "must match", "vhosts[2].domain": "is required"}},
		{"unknown nested key", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"extra": 1.0})}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0].extra": "unknown field"}},
		{"unknown top key", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", nil)}, "nope": true}, map[string]string{"nope": "unknown field"}},
		{"enum", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"php_version": "7.4"})}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0].php_version": "one of 8.2, 8.3, 8.4"}},
		{"list item type", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"aliases": []any{"ok", 3.0}})}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0].aliases[1]": "must be a string"}},
		{"null list item", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"aliases": []any{nil}})}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0].aliases[0]": "must not be empty"}},
		{"repeater item not an object", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{"a.io"}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0]": "entry"}},
		{"repeater must be a list", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": "a.io"}, map[string]string{"vhosts": "list of entries"}},
		{"secret empty", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"basic_auth": map[string]any{"password": ""}})}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0].basic_auth.password": "must not be empty"}},
		{"secret wrong type", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"basic_auth": map[string]any{"password": 5.0}})}}, map[string]string{"vhosts[0].basic_auth.password": "must be a string"}},
		{"sealed secret accepted", host, map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"basic_auth": map[string]any{"password": schema.Sealed([]byte{1, 2, 3})}})}}, nil},
		{"int fractional", group, map[string]any{"max_upload_mb": 1.5}, map[string]string{"max_upload_mb": "whole number"}},
		{"int range", group, map[string]any{"max_upload_mb": 4096.0}, map[string]string{"max_upload_mb": "at most 2048"}},
		{"int below min", group, map[string]any{"max_upload_mb": 0.0}, map[string]string{"max_upload_mb": "at least 1"}},
		{"int from json.Number", group, map[string]any{"max_upload_mb": json.Number("10")}, nil},
		{"int from Go int", group, map[string]any{"max_upload_mb": 10}, nil},
		{"int must be a number", group, map[string]any{"max_upload_mb": "10"}, map[string]string{"max_upload_mb": "whole number"}},
		{"bool", []schema.Field{{Name: "b", Type: schema.TypeBool}}, map[string]any{"b": "yes"}, map[string]string{"b": "true or false"}},
		{"float", []schema.Field{{Name: "f", Type: schema.TypeFloat, Min: ptr(0.5)}}, map[string]any{"f": 0.25}, map[string]string{"f": "at least 0.5"}},
		{"float above max", []schema.Field{{Name: "f", Type: schema.TypeFloat, Max: ptr(1.0)}}, map[string]any{"f": 1.5}, map[string]string{"f": "at most 1"}},
		{"float must be a number", []schema.Field{{Name: "f", Type: schema.TypeFloat}}, map[string]any{"f": true}, map[string]string{"f": "must be a number"}},
		{"string length counts characters", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Max: ptr(3.0)}}, map[string]any{"s": "äöü"}, nil},
		{"string too long", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Max: ptr(3.0)}}, map[string]any{"s": "äöüß"}, map[string]string{"s": "at most 3 characters"}},
		{"string too short", []schema.Field{{Name: "s", Type: schema.TypeString, Min: ptr(2.0)}}, map[string]any{"s": "a"}, map[string]string{"s": "at least 2 characters"}},
		{"string wrong type", []schema.Field{str}, map[string]any{"s": 1.0}, map[string]string{"s": "must be a string"}},
		{"object must be an object", []schema.Field{{Name: "o", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{str}}}, map[string]any{"o": "x"}, map[string]string{"o": "must be an object"}},
		{"required with default is satisfied", []schema.Field{{Name: "d", Type: schema.TypeString, Required: true, Default: "x"}}, map[string]any{}, nil},
		{"list must be a list", []schema.Field{list}, map[string]any{"l": "x"}, map[string]string{"l": "must be a list"}},
		{"max items", []schema.Field{list}, map[string]any{"l": []any{"a", "b"}}, map[string]string{"l": "at most 1"}},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			err := schema.Validate(c.fields, c.values)
			if c.problems == nil {
				if err != nil {
					t.Fatalf("Validate: %v", err)
				}
				return
			}
			ps := problemsOf(t, err)
			if len(ps) != len(c.problems) {
				t.Fatalf("problems = %+v, want %d", ps, len(c.problems))
			}
			for path, msg := range c.problems {
				if !hasProblem(ps, path, msg) {
					t.Errorf("missing %q at %q in %+v", msg, path, ps)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestValidateStoredIgnoresUndeclaredKeys(t *testing.T) {
	host := specExample().HostFields()
	values := map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"removed_sub_field": 1.0})}, "removed_field": "x"}
	if err := schema.ValidateStored(host, values); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ValidateStored: %v", err)
	}
	if err := schema.Validate(host, values); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("Validate must reject the same values")
	}
	if err := schema.ValidateStored(host, map[string]any{}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ValidateStored must still report missing required values")
	}
}

func TestValidationErrorMessage(t *testing.T) {
	e := &schema.ValidationError{Problems: []schema.Problem{{Path: "", Message: "x"}, {Path: "a", Message: "y"}}}
	if got := e.Error(); got != "invalid: (root): x; a: y" {
		t.Fatalf("Error() = %q", got)
	}
}
```

`internal/inventory/schema/secrets_test.go`:

```go
package schema_test

import (
	"errors"
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"testing"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

func TestMapSecretsTransformsEverySecretAtItsPath(t *testing.T) {
	fields := specExample().HostFields()
	in := map[string]any{
		"vhosts": []any{
			vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u", "password": "one"}}),
			vhost("b.io", map[string]any{"basic_auth": map[string]any{"password": "two"}}),
			"not an entry",
		},
		"orphan": map[string]any{"password": "untouched"},
	}
	var seen []string
	out, err := schema.MapSecrets(fields, in, func(path string, v any) (any, error) {
		seen = append(seen, path+"="+v.(string))
		return "X", nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	want := []string{"vhosts[0].basic_auth.password=one", "vhosts[1].basic_auth.password=two"}
	if !slices.Equal(seen, want) {
		t.Fatalf("visited %v, want %v", seen, want)
	}
	first := out["vhosts"].([]any)[0].(map[string]any)["basic_auth"].(map[string]any)
	if first["password"] != "X" || first["user"] != "u" {
		t.Fatalf("transformed entry = %v", first)
	}
	if out["vhosts"].([]any)[2] != "not an entry" {
		t.Fatal("a malformed entry must be copied unchanged")
	}
	if out["orphan"].(map[string]any)["password"] != "untouched" {
		t.Fatal("undeclared keys must be copied unchanged")
	}
	if in["vhosts"].([]any)[0].(map[string]any)["basic_auth"].(map[string]any)["password"] != "one" {
		t.Fatal("MapSecrets must not mutate its input")
	}
	empty, err := schema.MapSecrets(fields, nil, nil)
	if err != nil || len(empty) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("nil values = %v, %v", empty, err)
	}
}

func TestMapSecretsPropagatesError(t *testing.T) {
	boom := errors.New("boom")
	fields := []schema.Field{{Name: "o", Type: schema.TypeObject, Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "p", Type: schema.TypeSecret}}}}
	_, err := schema.MapSecrets(fields, map[string]any{"o": map[string]any{"p": "x"}}, func(string, any) (any, error) { return nil, boom })
	if !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	rep := []schema.Field{{Name: "r", Type: schema.TypeRepeater, Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "p", Type: schema.TypeSecret}}}}
	_, err = schema.MapSecrets(rep, map[string]any{"r": []any{map[string]any{"p": "x"}}}, func(string, any) (any, error) { return nil, boom })
	if !errors.Is(err, boom) {
		t.Fatalf("repeater err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestSecretMarkers(t *testing.T) {
	sealed := schema.Sealed([]byte("ciphertext"))
	if b, ok := schema.SealedBytes(sealed); !ok || string(b) != "ciphertext" {
		t.Fatalf("SealedBytes = %q, %v", b, ok)
	}
	for _, v := range []any{
		"plain", map[string]any{"$sealed": "!!not base64!!"},
		map[string]any{"$sealed": "YQ==", "extra": 1}, map[string]any{"$sealed": 1},
	} {
		if _, ok := schema.SealedBytes(v); ok {
			t.Errorf("SealedBytes(%v) accepted", v)
		}
	}
	if from, ok := schema.KeepFrom(map[string]any{"$secret": "keep", "from": "a[0].b"}); !ok || from != "a[0].b" {
		t.Fatalf("KeepFrom = %q, %v", from, ok)
	}
	for _, v := range []any{
		map[string]any{"$secret": "keep"}, map[string]any{"$secret": "set", "from": "a"},
		map[string]any{"$secret": "keep", "from": "a", "x": 1}, "keep",
	} {
		if _, ok := schema.KeepFrom(v); ok {
			t.Errorf("KeepFrom(%v) accepted", v)
		}
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(schema.Mask(), map[string]any{"$secret": "set"}) {
		t.Fatalf("Mask = %v", schema.Mask())
	}
}

func TestMaskVarsReplacesSealedValuesAtAnyDepth(t *testing.T) {
	in := map[string]any{
		"top":    schema.Sealed([]byte{1}),
		"list":   []any{schema.Sealed([]byte{2}), "plain"},
		"nested": map[string]any{"deep": schema.Sealed([]byte{3}), "n": 1.0},
	}
	got := schema.MaskVars(in)
	want := map[string]any{
		"top":    schema.Mask(),
		"list":   []any{schema.Mask(), "plain"},
		"nested": map[string]any{"deep": schema.Mask(), "n": 1.0},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("MaskVars = %v", got)
	}
	if _, ok := schema.SealedBytes(in["top"]); !ok {
		t.Fatal("MaskVars must not mutate its input")
	}
}

func TestLookup(t *testing.T) {
	doc := map[string]any{
		"vhosts":  []any{vhost("a.io", nil), vhost("b.io", map[string]any{"basic_auth": map[string]any{"password": "p"}})},
		"aliases": []any{"x", "y"},
		"grid":    []any{[]any{"r0c0"}},
	}
	cases := []struct {
		path string
		want any
		ok   bool
	}{
		{"vhosts[1].basic_auth.password", "p", true},
		{"vhosts[0].domain", "a.io", true},
		{"aliases[1]", "y", true},
		{"grid[0][0]", "r0c0", true},
		{"vhosts[5].domain", nil, false},
		{"vhosts.domain", nil, false},
		{"aliases[0].x", nil, false},
		{"missing", nil, false},
		{"vhosts[x]", nil, false},
		{"vhosts[-1]", nil, false},
		{"vhosts[0", nil, false},
		{"vhosts[0]x", nil, false},
		{"[0]", nil, false},
		{"", nil, false},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		got, ok := schema.Lookup(doc, c.path)
		if ok != c.ok || (ok && got != c.want) {
			t.Errorf("Lookup(%q) = %v, %v; want %v, %v", c.path, got, ok, c.want, c.ok)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
package schema_test

import (
	"reflect"
	"slices"
	"testing"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

func TestResolveFillsDefaultsAndDropsUndeclaredKeys(t *testing.T) {
	s := specExample()
	got := schema.Resolve(s.HostFields(), map[string]any{
		"vhosts": []any{
			vhost("a.io", map[string]any{"stale": 1.0, "basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u", "old": true}}),
			"malformed",
		},
		"gone": true,
	})
	want := map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{
		map[string]any{"domain": "a.io", "ssl": true, "basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u"}},
		"malformed",
	}}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(got, want) {
		t.Fatalf("Resolve(host) = %#v", got)
	}
	if got := schema.Resolve(s.GroupFields(), nil); !reflect.DeepEqual(got, map[string]any{"php_default": "8.3", "max_upload_mb": 64.0}) {
		t.Fatalf("Resolve(group) = %#v", got)
	}
	if got := schema.Resolve(s.GroupFields(), map[string]any{"php_default": "8.4", "max_upload_mb": nil}); got["php_default"] != "8.4" || got["max_upload_mb"] != 64.0 {
		t.Fatalf("explicit value and null: %#v", got)
	}
}

func TestOrphans(t *testing.T) {
	fields := specExample().GroupFields()
	if got := schema.Orphans(fields, map[string]any{"b": 1, "a": 2, "php_default": "8.3"}); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"a", "b"}) {
		t.Fatalf("Orphans = %v", got)
	}
	if got := schema.Orphans(fields, map[string]any{"php_default": "8.3"}); got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Orphans = %v, want nil", got)
	}
}
```

`internal/inventory/schema/schema.go`:

```go
// Package schema is the typed variable schema an inventory group owns (spec
// §6): the field model, the check that a schema is well-formed, validation of
// values against it, schema defaults, and the secret markers that let sealed
// values travel through the API without ever being revealed. It is pure: no
// I/O, no database, no HTTP.
package schema

import (
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
)

// Type is a field type.
type Type string

// Field types (spec §6). list holds scalars, repeater holds objects with a
// sub-schema, object is a single sub-schema.
const (
	TypeString   Type = "string"
	TypeInt      Type = "int"
	TypeFloat    Type = "float"
	TypeBool     Type = "bool"
	TypeEnum     Type = "enum"
	TypeSecret   Type = "secret"
	TypeList     Type = "list"
	TypeRepeater Type = "repeater"
	TypeObject   Type = "object"
)

// Scope says where a top-level field's value lives: on the group, set once,
// or on each membership, asked when a host is assigned.
type Scope string

// Scopes.
const (
	ScopeGroup Scope = "group"
	ScopeHost  Scope = "host"
)

// Limits that keep a schema small enough to render as a form.
const (
	MaxDepth      = 4
	MaxFields     = 100
	MaxOptions    = 100
	MaxPatternLen = 512
)

var fieldNamePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]{0,63}$`)

// reservedPrefixes protect Ansible's connection variables and the facts
// Pulsix renders under pulsix_facts from being shadowed by a schema field.
var reservedPrefixes = []string{"ansible_", "pulsix_"}

// Field is one schema field. The JSON tags are the wire contract of spec §6:
// the SPA renders its forms from exactly this JSON.
type Field struct {
	Name        string   `json:"name"`
	Label       string   `json:"label,omitempty"`
	Description string   `json:"description,omitempty"`
	Type        Type     `json:"type"`
	Scope       Scope    `json:"scope,omitempty"`
	Required    bool     `json:"required,omitempty"`
	Overridable bool     `json:"overridable,omitempty"`
	Default     any      `json:"default,omitempty"`
	Options     []string `json:"options,omitempty"`
	Min         *float64 `json:"min,omitempty"`
	Max         *float64 `json:"max,omitempty"`
	Pattern     string   `json:"pattern,omitempty"`
	MinItems    *int     `json:"min_items,omitempty"`
	MaxItems    *int     `json:"max_items,omitempty"`
	Item        *Field   `json:"item,omitempty"`
	Fields      []Field  `json:"fields,omitempty"`
}

// Schema is the ordered field list a group owns.
type Schema struct {
	Fields []Field `json:"fields"`
}

// EffectiveScope is the field's scope, group when unset.
func (f Field) EffectiveScope() Scope {
	if f.Scope == "" {
		return ScopeGroup
	}
	return f.Scope
}

// GroupFields returns the top-level fields whose values live on the group.
func (s Schema) GroupFields() []Field {
	var out []Field
	for _, f := range s.Fields {
		if f.EffectiveScope() == ScopeGroup {
			out = append(out, f)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// HostFields returns the top-level fields asked when a host is assigned.
func (s Schema) HostFields() []Field {
	var out []Field
	for _, f := range s.Fields {
		if f.EffectiveScope() == ScopeHost {
			out = append(out, f)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// MembershipFields returns what a membership may carry: every host-scoped
// field, plus every overridable group-scoped field as an optional override
// without a schema default (an absent override means "use the group value").
func (s Schema) MembershipFields() []Field {
	var out []Field
	for _, f := range s.Fields {
		switch {
		case f.EffectiveScope() == ScopeHost:
			out = append(out, f)
		case f.Overridable:
			o := f
			o.Required = false
			o.Default = nil
			out = append(out, o)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// Check reports every problem with the schema itself, or nil. Problem paths
// index into the field list: fields[0].fields[2].name.
func (s Schema) Check() error {
	var p problems
	checkFields(&p, "", s.Fields, 0, true)
	return p.err()
}

func checkFields(p *problems, prefix string, fields []Field, depth int, top bool) {
	if len(fields) > MaxFields {
		p.add(prefix, fmt.Sprintf("at most %d fields are allowed", MaxFields))
	}
	seen := make(map[string]bool, len(fields))
	for i, f := range fields {
		path := join(prefix, fmt.Sprintf("fields[%d]", i))
		switch {
		case !fieldNamePattern.MatchString(f.Name):
			p.add(path+".name", "must start with a lowercase letter or underscore and contain only lowercase letters, digits and underscores (at most 64)")
		case top && reserved(f.Name):
			p.add(path+".name", "must not start with ansible_ or pulsix_")
		case seen[f.Name]:
			p.add(path+".name", "duplicate field name "+f.Name)
		}
		seen[f.Name] = true
		checkField(p, path, f, depth, top)
	}
}

func checkField(p *problems, path string, f Field, depth int, top bool) {
	if !knownType(f.Type) {
		p.add(path+".type", "unknown type "+string(f.Type))
		return
	}
	checkScope(p, path, f, top)
	checkAttributes(p, path, f)
	switch f.Type {
	case TypeEnum:
		checkOptions(p, path, f.Options)
	case TypeList:
		checkItem(p, path, f.Item)
	case TypeRepeater, TypeObject:
		switch {
		case len(f.Fields) == 0:
			p.add(path+".fields", "a "+string(f.Type)+" needs at least one sub-field")
		case depth+1 > MaxDepth:
			p.add(path+".fields", fmt.Sprintf("schemas nest at most %d levels deep", MaxDepth))
		default:
			checkFields(p, path, f.Fields, depth+1, false)
		}
	}
	checkDefault(p, path, f)
}

func checkScope(p *problems, path string, f Field, top bool) {
	if !top {
		if f.Scope != "" {
			p.add(path+".scope", "only top-level fields have a scope")
		}
		if f.Overridable {
			p.add(path+".overridable", "only top-level fields can be overridable")
		}
		return
	}
	if f.Scope != "" && f.Scope != ScopeGroup && f.Scope != ScopeHost {
		p.add(path+".scope", "must be group or host")
	}
	if f.Overridable && f.EffectiveScope() != ScopeGroup {
		p.add(path+".overridable", "only group-scoped fields can be overridden per host")
	}
}

func checkAttributes(p *problems, path string, f Field) {
	numeric := f.Type == TypeInt || f.Type == TypeFloat
	if (f.Min != nil || f.Max != nil) && !numeric && f.Type != TypeString {
		p.add(path+".min", "min and max apply to int, float and string fields; use min_items and max_items for lists")
	}
	if f.Min != nil && f.Max != nil && *f.Min > *f.Max {
		p.add(path+".max", "must not be less than min")
	}
	if f.Type == TypeString {
		for _, b := range []*float64{f.Min, f.Max} {
			if b != nil && (*b < 0 || *b != math.Trunc(*b)) {
				p.add(path+".min", "string lengths must be whole numbers ≥ 0")
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if f.Pattern != "" {
		switch {
		case f.Type != TypeString:
			p.add(path+".pattern", "pattern applies to string fields only")
		case len(f.Pattern) > MaxPatternLen:
			p.add(path+".pattern", fmt.Sprintf("must be at most %d characters", MaxPatternLen))
		default:
			if _, err := regexp.Compile(f.Pattern); err != nil {
				p.add(path+".pattern", "invalid regular expression: "+err.Error())
			}
		}
	}
	if (f.MinItems != nil || f.MaxItems != nil) && f.Type != TypeList && f.Type != TypeRepeater {
		p.add(path+".min_items", "min_items and max_items apply to list and repeater fields only")
	}
	if f.MinItems != nil && *f.MinItems < 0 {
		p.add(path+".min_items", "must be ≥ 0")
	}
	if f.MinItems != nil && f.MaxItems != nil && *f.MinItems > *f.MaxItems {
		p.add(path+".max_items", "must not be less than min_items")
	}
	if len(f.Options) > 0 && f.Type != TypeEnum {
		p.add(path+".options", "options apply to enum fields only")
	}
	if f.Item != nil && f.Type != TypeList {
		p.add(path+".item", "item applies to list fields only")
	}
	if len(f.Fields) > 0 && f.Type != TypeRepeater && f.Type != TypeObject {
		p.add(path+".fields", "sub-fields apply to repeater and object fields only")
	}
}

func checkOptions(p *problems, path string, options []string) {
	if len(options) == 0 {
		p.add(path+".options", "an enum needs at least one option")
		return
	}
	if len(options) > MaxOptions {
		p.add(path+".options", fmt.Sprintf("at most %d options are allowed", MaxOptions))
	}
	seen := make(map[string]bool, len(options))
	for _, o := range options {
		switch {
		case o == "":
			p.add(path+".options", "options must not be empty")
		case seen[o]:
			p.add(path+".options", "duplicate option "+o)
		}
		seen[o] = true
	}
}

func checkItem(p *problems, path string, item *Field) {
	if item == nil {
		p.add(path+".item", "a list needs an item type")
		return
	}
	ip := path + ".item"
	if item.Name != "" {
		p.add(ip+".name", "list items have no name")
	}
	if !isScalar(item.Type) {
		p.add(ip+".type", "list items must be string, int, float, bool or enum")
		return
	}
	if item.Scope != "" || item.Overridable || item.Required || item.Default != nil {
		p.add(ip, "list items carry only type and validation attributes")
	}
	checkAttributes(p, ip, *item)
	if item.Type == TypeEnum {
		checkOptions(p, ip, item.Options)
	}
}

func checkDefault(p *problems, path string, f Field) {
	if f.Default == nil {
		return
	}
	switch f.Type {
	case TypeSecret, TypeRepeater, TypeObject:
		p.add(path+".default", string(f.Type)+" fields cannot have a default")
		return
	}
	var vp problems
	validateValue(&vp, "", f, f.Default, true)
	if len(vp.list) > 0 {
		p.add(path+".default", "does not satisfy the field: "+vp.list[0].Message)
	}
}

func knownType(t Type) bool {
	switch t {
	case TypeString, TypeInt, TypeFloat, TypeBool, TypeEnum, TypeSecret, TypeList, TypeRepeater, TypeObject:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func isScalar(t Type) bool {
	switch t {
	case TypeString, TypeInt, TypeFloat, TypeBool, TypeEnum:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func reserved(name string) bool {
	for _, prefix := range reservedPrefixes {
		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// join builds a dotted path; an empty prefix means the root.
func join(prefix, name string) string {
	if prefix == "" {
		return name
	}
	return prefix + "." + name
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement validation**

`internal/inventory/schema/validate.go`:

```go
package schema

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"math"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"
)

// maxSafeInt is the largest integer a JSON number carries without loss.
const maxSafeInt = 1 << 53

// Problem is one validation finding, located by path (vhosts[1].domain for
// values, fields[0].name for a schema).
type Problem struct {
	Path    string `json:"path"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

// ValidationError carries every problem found. Services return it unchanged
// and the API turns it into 422 with one detail per problem.
type ValidationError struct {
	Problems []Problem
}

func (e *ValidationError) Error() string {
	parts := make([]string, len(e.Problems))
	for i, pr := range e.Problems {
		path := pr.Path
		if path == "" {
			path = "(root)"
		}
		parts[i] = path + ": " + pr.Message
	}
	return "invalid: " + strings.Join(parts, "; ")
}

type problems struct{ list []Problem }

func (p *problems) add(path, msg string) { p.list = append(p.list, Problem{Path: path, Message: msg}) }

func (p *problems) err() error {
	if len(p.list) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	return &ValidationError{Problems: p.list}
}

// Validate checks a write strictly: an undeclared key at any depth is a
// problem, a missing or null value is a problem for a required field without
// a default, and every present value must satisfy its field. Secret fields
// accept a non-empty plaintext string or a sealed value; keep markers must be
// resolved before Validate runs.
func Validate(fields []Field, values map[string]any) error {
	var p problems
	validateObject(&p, "", fields, values, true)
	return p.err()
}

// ValidateStored checks stored values like Validate but ignores keys the
// schema no longer declares: a removed field keeps its old value until the
// next save, and that must not make a membership look incomplete.
func ValidateStored(fields []Field, values map[string]any) error {
	var p problems
	validateObject(&p, "", fields, values, false)
	return p.err()
}

func validateObject(p *problems, prefix string, fields []Field, values map[string]any, strict bool) {
	if strict {
		declared := make(map[string]bool, len(fields))
		for _, f := range fields {
			declared[f.Name] = true
		}
		var unknown []string
		for k := range values {
			if !declared[k] {
				unknown = append(unknown, k)
			}
		}
		slices.Sort(unknown)
		for _, k := range unknown {
			p.add(join(prefix, k), "unknown field")
		}
	}
	for _, f := range fields {
		v, ok := values[f.Name]
		if !ok || v == nil {
			if f.Required && f.Default == nil {
				p.add(join(prefix, f.Name), "is required")
			}
			continue
		}
		validateValue(p, join(prefix, f.Name), f, v, strict)
	}
}

func validateValue(p *problems, path string, f Field, v any, strict bool) {
	switch f.Type {
	case TypeString:
		s, ok := v.(string)
		if !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be a string")
			return
		}
		n := float64(utf8.RuneCountInString(s))
		if f.Min != nil && n < *f.Min {
			p.add(path, "must be at least "+num(*f.Min)+" characters")
		}
		if f.Max != nil && n > *f.Max {
			p.add(path, "must be at most "+num(*f.Max)+" characters")
		}
		if f.Pattern != "" && !matchPattern(f.Pattern, s) {
			p.add(path, "must match "+f.Pattern)
		}
	case TypeInt:
		n, ok := number(v)
		if !ok || n != math.Trunc(n) || math.Abs(n) > maxSafeInt {
			p.add(path, "must be a whole number")
			return
		}
		checkRange(p, path, f, n)
	case TypeFloat:
		n, ok := number(v)
		if !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be a number")
			return
		}
		checkRange(p, path, f, n)
	case TypeBool:
		if _, ok := v.(bool); !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be true or false")
		}
	case TypeEnum:
		s, ok := v.(string)
		if !ok || !slices.Contains(f.Options, s) {
			p.add(path, "must be one of "+strings.Join(f.Options, ", "))
		}
	case TypeSecret:
		if _, ok := SealedBytes(v); ok {
			return
		}
		s, ok := v.(string)
		if !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be a string")
			return
		}
		if s == "" {
			p.add(path, "must not be empty")
		}
	case TypeList:
		items, ok := v.([]any)
		if !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be a list")
			return
		}
		checkCount(p, path, f, len(items))
		if f.Item == nil {
			return
		}
		for i, it := range items {
			ip := fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", path, i)
			if it == nil {
				p.add(ip, "must not be empty")
				continue
			}
			validateValue(p, ip, *f.Item, it, strict)
		}
	case TypeRepeater:
		items, ok := v.([]any)
		if !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be a list of entries")
			return
		}
		checkCount(p, path, f, len(items))
		for i, it := range items {
			ip := fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", path, i)
			obj, ok := it.(map[string]any)
			if !ok {
				p.add(ip, "must be an entry with fields")
				continue
			}
			validateObject(p, ip, f.Fields, obj, strict)
		}
	case TypeObject:
		obj, ok := v.(map[string]any)
		if !ok {
			p.add(path, "must be an object")
			return
		}
		validateObject(p, path, f.Fields, obj, strict)
	}
}

func checkRange(p *problems, path string, f Field, n float64) {
	if f.Min != nil && n < *f.Min {
		p.add(path, "must be at least "+num(*f.Min))
	}
	if f.Max != nil && n > *f.Max {
		p.add(path, "must be at most "+num(*f.Max))
	}
}

func checkCount(p *problems, path string, f Field, n int) {
	if f.MinItems != nil && n < *f.MinItems {
		p.add(path, fmt.Sprintf("needs at least %d entries", *f.MinItems))
	}
	if f.MaxItems != nil && n > *f.MaxItems {
		p.add(path, fmt.Sprintf("allows at most %d entries", *f.MaxItems))
	}
}

// number accepts the numeric shapes a decoded JSON document or a Go caller
// can produce.
func number(v any) (float64, bool) {
	switch n := v.(type) {
	case float64:
		return n, true
	case float32:
		return float64(n), true
	case int:
		return float64(n), true
	case int64:
		return float64(n), true
	case json.Number:
		f, err := n.Float64()
		return f, err == nil
	}
	return 0, false
}

func num(f float64) string { return strconv.FormatFloat(f, 'f', -1, 64) }

// matchPattern reports whether s matches pattern. A pattern that does not
// compile never fails a value: Check reports the pattern itself.
func matchPattern(pattern, s string) bool {
	re, err := regexp.Compile(pattern)
	if err != nil {
		return true
	}
	return re.MatchString(s)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the secret markers and path lookup**

`internal/inventory/schema/secrets.go`:

```go
package schema

import (
	"encoding/base64"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// Secret markers inside a vars document. A stored secret is
// {"$sealed": "<base64>"}; a read returns {"$secret": "set"} in its place; a
// write keeps a stored secret with {"$secret": "keep", "from": "<path>"}.
const (
	markerSealed = "$sealed"
	markerSecret = "$secret"
	markerFrom   = "from"
	secretSet    = "set"
	secretKeep   = "keep"
)

// Sealed wraps ciphertext the way it is stored inside a vars document.
func Sealed(ciphertext []byte) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{markerSealed: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(ciphertext)}
}

// SealedBytes returns the ciphertext when v is a stored sealed value.
func SealedBytes(v any) ([]byte, bool) {
	m, ok := v.(map[string]any)
	if !ok || len(m) != 1 {
		return nil, false
	}
	s, ok := m[markerSealed].(string)
	if !ok {
		return nil, false
	}
	b, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(s)
	return b, err == nil
}

// Mask is what a read returns in place of a stored secret.
func Mask() map[string]any { return map[string]any{markerSecret: secretSet} }

// KeepFrom returns the path a keep marker points at.
func KeepFrom(v any) (string, bool) {
	m, ok := v.(map[string]any)
	if !ok || len(m) != 2 || m[markerSecret] != secretKeep {
		return "", false
	}
	from, ok := m[markerFrom].(string)
	return from, ok
}

// MaskVars returns a deep copy of values with every sealed value, at any
// depth and whether or not a schema still declares it, replaced by Mask().
// Reads use it so no ciphertext ever leaves the manager.
func MaskVars(values map[string]any) map[string]any {
	out := make(map[string]any, len(values))
	for k, v := range values {
		out[k] = maskValue(v)
	}
	return out
}

func maskValue(v any) any {
	switch t := v.(type) {
	case map[string]any:
		if _, ok := SealedBytes(t); ok {
			return Mask()
		}
		return MaskVars(t)
	case []any:
		out := make([]any, len(t))
		for i, x := range t {
			out[i] = maskValue(x)
		}
		return out
	default:
		return v
	}
}

// SecretFunc transforms the value of one secret-typed field found at path.
type SecretFunc func(path string, v any) (any, error)

// MapSecrets returns a deep copy of values in which fn replaced every
// non-null value of a secret-typed field, at any depth. Undeclared keys and
// values whose shape does not match their field are copied unchanged;
// Validate reports those.
func MapSecrets(fields []Field, values map[string]any, fn SecretFunc) (map[string]any, error) {
	return mapObject("", fields, values, fn)
}

func mapObject(prefix string, fields []Field, values map[string]any, fn SecretFunc) (map[string]any, error) {
	byName := make(map[string]Field, len(fields))
	for _, f := range fields {
		byName[f.Name] = f
	}
	out := make(map[string]any, len(values))
	for k, v := range values {
		f, declared := byName[k]
		if !declared || v == nil {
			out[k] = copyValue(v)
			continue
		}
		mv, err := mapValue(join(prefix, k), f, v, fn)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out[k] = mv
	}
	return out, nil
}

func mapValue(path string, f Field, v any, fn SecretFunc) (any, error) {
	switch f.Type {
	case TypeSecret:
		return fn(path, v)
	case TypeObject:
		if obj, ok := v.(map[string]any); ok {
			return mapObject(path, f.Fields, obj, fn)
		}
	case TypeRepeater:
		if items, ok := v.([]any); ok {
			out := make([]any, len(items))
			for i, it := range items {
				obj, isObj := it.(map[string]any)
				if !isObj {
					out[i] = copyValue(it)
					continue
				}
				m, err := mapObject(fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", path, i), f.Fields, obj, fn)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				out[i] = m
			}
			return out, nil
		}
	}
	return copyValue(v), nil
}

func copyValue(v any) any {
	switch t := v.(type) {
	case map[string]any:
		out := make(map[string]any, len(t))
		for k, x := range t {
			out[k] = copyValue(x)
		}
		return out
	case []any:
		out := make([]any, len(t))
		for i, x := range t {
			out[i] = copyValue(x)
		}
		return out
	default:
		return v
	}
}

// Lookup returns the value at path ("vhosts[1].basic_auth.password").
func Lookup(values map[string]any, path string) (any, bool) {
	var cur any = values
	for _, seg := range strings.Split(path, ".") {
		name, idxs, ok := splitSegment(seg)
		if !ok {
			return nil, false
		}
		m, isMap := cur.(map[string]any)
		if !isMap {
			return nil, false
		}
		if cur, ok = m[name]; !ok {
			return nil, false
		}
		for _, i := range idxs {
			arr, isArr := cur.([]any)
			if !isArr || i >= len(arr) {
				return nil, false
			}
			cur = arr[i]
		}
	}
	return cur, true
}

// splitSegment splits "vhosts[2][0]" into "vhosts" and [2 0].
func splitSegment(seg string) (string, []int, bool) {
	name, rest, hasIndex := strings.Cut(seg, "[")
	if name == "" {
		return "", nil, false
	}
	if !hasIndex {
		return name, nil, true
	}
	rest = "[" + rest
	var idxs []int
	for rest != "" {
		if !strings.HasPrefix(rest, "[") {
			return "", nil, false
		}
		end := strings.IndexByte(rest, ']')
		if end < 0 {
			return "", nil, false
		}
		i, err := strconv.Atoi(rest[1:end])
		if err != nil || i < 0 {
			return "", nil, false
		}
		idxs = append(idxs, i)
		rest = rest[end+1:]
	}
	return name, idxs, true
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement resolution and orphans**

`internal/inventory/schema/resolve.go`:

```go
package schema

import "slices"

// Resolve returns the values Ansible sees for fields: declared keys only at
// every depth, schema defaults filled in for absent or null optional values.
// Values whose shape does not match their field are passed through; stored
// values were validated when they were written.
func Resolve(fields []Field, values map[string]any) map[string]any {
	out := make(map[string]any, len(fields))
	for _, f := range fields {
		v, ok := values[f.Name]
		if !ok || v == nil {
			if f.Default != nil {
				out[f.Name] = f.Default
			}
			continue
		}
		out[f.Name] = resolveValue(f, v)
	}
	return out
}

func resolveValue(f Field, v any) any {
	switch f.Type {
	case TypeObject:
		if obj, ok := v.(map[string]any); ok {
			return Resolve(f.Fields, obj)
		}
	case TypeRepeater:
		if items, ok := v.([]any); ok {
			out := make([]any, len(items))
			for i, it := range items {
				if obj, isObj := it.(map[string]any); isObj {
					out[i] = Resolve(f.Fields, obj)
				} else {
					out[i] = it
				}
			}
			return out
		}
	}
	return v
}

// Orphans returns the sorted top-level keys of values that fields does not
// declare, or nil when there are none.
func Orphans(fields []Field, values map[string]any) []string {
	declared := make(map[string]bool, len(fields))
	for _, f := range fields {
		declared[f.Name] = true
	}
	var out []string
	for k := range values {
		if !declared[k] {
			out = append(out, k)
		}
	}
	slices.Sort(out)
	return out
}
```

### Task 2: Sealing with additional data, permissions, and the credential store

The encrypted secret store of backlog 2.7 without its API: `crypto.Box` learns to bind ciphertext to a row, the role matrix learns the four new permissions, and `internal/credentials` stores SSH keys, SSH passwords, Git deploy keys and Git tokens. SSH keys are generated (ed25519) or imported; the public key and fingerprint are kept in clear, the private material only sealed.

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/crypto/secretbox.go`, `internal/crypto/secretbox_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/tenancy/roles.go`, `internal/tenancy/roles_test.go`
- Create: `internal/db/convert.go`, `internal/db/convert_test.go`
- Create: `internal/db/rls_catalog_test.go`
- Create: `internal/db/migrations/00013_credentials.sql`
- Create: `internal/db/queries/credentials.sql` (then `sqlc generate`, which writes `internal/db/gen/credentials.sql.go` and updates `models.go`)
- Create: `internal/credentials/service.go`, `internal/credentials/keys.go`, `internal/credentials/snippets.go`, `internal/credentials/store_sql.go`
- Test: `internal/credentials/service_test.go`, `internal/credentials/keys_test.go`, `internal/credentials/store_sql_test.go`
- Modify: `go.mod`/`go.sum` only if `golang.org/x/crypto/ssh` needs it (it is part of the existing `golang.org/x/crypto` module; run `go mod tidy`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `crypto.Box`, `db.DB`, `testdb.Open`.
- Produces:
  - `(*crypto.Box).SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)`, `(*crypto.Box).OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)`
  - `tenancy.PermInventoryRead`, `tenancy.PermInventoryWrite`, `tenancy.PermCredentialsRead`, `tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite`
  - `db.NullUUID(*uuid.UUID) pgtype.UUID`, `db.UUIDPtr(pgtype.UUID) *uuid.UUID`, `db.NullTime(*time.Time) pgtype.Timestamptz`, `db.TimePtr(pgtype.Timestamptz) *time.Time`, `db.IsUniqueViolation(error) bool`, `db.IsForeignKeyViolation(error) bool`
  - package `credentials`: `Kind` (`KindSSHKey`, `KindSSHPassword`, `KindGitKey`, `KindGitToken`) with `Valid()`, `IsKey()`, `IsSSH()`; `Credential{ID, OrgID uuid.UUID; Name string; Kind Kind; Username, PublicKey, Fingerprint, KeyType string; ExpiresAt *time.Time; CreatedBy *uuid.UUID; CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time}` with `Expired(now) bool`, `ExpiresSoon(now) bool`; `Secret{PrivateKey, Password, Token string}`; `Material{Generate bool; PrivateKey, Passphrase, Password, Token string}`; `CreateInput{Name, Username string; Kind Kind; ExpiresAt *time.Time; CreatedBy *uuid.UUID; Material Material}`; `UpdateInput{Name, Username string; ExpiresAt *time.Time; Material Material}`; `Sealer` interface; `Store` interface; `NewService(store Store, box Sealer, now func() time.Time, rand io.Reader) *Service`; methods `Create`, `Get`, `List`, `Update`, `Delete`; `AAD(orgID, id uuid.UUID) []byte`; `AuthorizedKey(c Credential, orgSlug string) string`; `Snippets{AuthorizedKeysCommand, CloudInit, Terraform string}`; `SnippetsFor(c Credential, orgSlug string) Snippets`; `DefaultLoginUser = "ansible"`; `ExpiryWarning = 30 * 24 * time.Hour`; errors `ErrInvalidInput`, `ErrNotFound`, `ErrNameTaken`, `ErrInUse`; `NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Add SealAAD/OpenAAD with tests**

Append to `internal/crypto/secretbox_test.go`:

```go
func TestSealAADBindsCiphertextToItsData(t *testing.T) {
	box := crypto.NewBox(testKey())
	sealed, err := box.SealAAD([]byte("hunter2"), []byte("credential:org-a:1"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	plain, err := box.OpenAAD(sealed, []byte("credential:org-a:1"))
	if err != nil || string(plain) != "hunter2" {
		t.Fatalf("OpenAAD = %q, %v", plain, err)
	}
	if _, err := box.OpenAAD(sealed, []byte("credential:org-b:1")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("OpenAAD with different data must fail")
	}
	if _, err := box.Open(sealed); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("Open without the data must fail")
	}
	if _, err := box.OpenAAD([]byte{1, 2}, nil); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("OpenAAD of a too-short value must fail")
	}
	legacy, err := box.Seal([]byte("old"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if plain, err := box.OpenAAD(legacy, nil); err != nil || string(plain) != "old" {
		t.Fatalf("Seal output must open with nil data: %q, %v", plain, err)
	}
}
```

Replace the `Seal`/`Open` methods in `internal/crypto/secretbox.go` with:

```go
// Seal returns nonce || ciphertext.
func (b *Box) Seal(plain []byte) ([]byte, error) { return b.SealAAD(plain, nil) }

// Open reverses Seal and fails on any tampering.
func (b *Box) Open(sealed []byte) ([]byte, error) { return b.OpenAAD(sealed, nil) }

// SealAAD is Seal with additional authenticated data: the result only opens
// with the same aad. Callers pass a string naming the owning row (for example
// "credential:<org_id>:<id>"), so a ciphertext copied into another row or
// organization no longer opens.
func (b *Box) SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	nonce := make([]byte, b.aead.NonceSize())
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(rand.Reader, nonce); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return b.aead.Seal(nonce, nonce, plain, aad), nil
}

// OpenAAD reverses SealAAD with the same aad and fails on any tampering.
func (b *Box) OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	n := b.aead.NonceSize()
	if len(sealed) < n {
		return nil, errors.New("sealed value too short")
	}
	return b.aead.Open(nil, sealed[:n], sealed[n:], aad)
}
```

Run: `go test -race ./internal/crypto/...` → PASS.

- [ ] **Step 3: Shared null and error helpers for stores**

`internal/db/convert_test.go`:

```go
package db_test

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
)

func TestNullConversions(t *testing.T) {
	id := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	if got := db.UUIDPtr(db.NullUUID(&id)); got == nil || *got != id {
		t.Fatalf("uuid round trip = %v", got)
	}
	if db.NullUUID(nil).Valid || db.UUIDPtr(db.NullUUID(nil)) != nil {
		t.Fatal("nil uuid must stay NULL")
	}
	now := time.Now().UTC().Truncate(time.Microsecond)
	if got := db.TimePtr(db.NullTime(&now)); got == nil || !got.Equal(now) {
		t.Fatalf("time round trip = %v", got)
	}
	if db.NullTime(nil).Valid || db.TimePtr(db.NullTime(nil)) != nil {
		t.Fatal("nil time must stay NULL")
	}
}

func TestPgErrorClassification(t *testing.T) {
	unique := fmt.Errorf("wrapped: %w", &pgconn.PgError{Code: "23505"})
	fk := &pgconn.PgError{Code: "23503"}
	if !db.IsUniqueViolation(unique) || db.IsUniqueViolation(fk) {
		t.Fatal("IsUniqueViolation")
	}
	if !db.IsForeignKeyViolation(fk) || db.IsForeignKeyViolation(unique) {
		t.Fatal("IsForeignKeyViolation")
	}
	if db.IsUniqueViolation(errors.New("plain")) || db.IsForeignKeyViolation(nil) {
		t.Fatal("non-pg errors must not match")
	}
}
```

`internal/db/convert.go`:

```go
package db

import (
	"errors"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"
)

// NullUUID converts an optional id to the type sqlc generates for a nullable
// uuid column.
func NullUUID(id *uuid.UUID) pgtype.UUID {
	if id == nil {
		return pgtype.UUID{}
	}
	return pgtype.UUID{Bytes: *id, Valid: true}
}

// UUIDPtr reverses NullUUID.
func UUIDPtr(v pgtype.UUID) *uuid.UUID {
	if !v.Valid {
		return nil
	}
	id := uuid.UUID(v.Bytes)
	return &id
}

// NullTime converts an optional time to a nullable timestamptz.
func NullTime(t *time.Time) pgtype.Timestamptz {
	if t == nil {
		return pgtype.Timestamptz{}
	}
	return pgtype.Timestamptz{Time: *t, Valid: true}
}

// TimePtr reverses NullTime.
func TimePtr(v pgtype.Timestamptz) *time.Time {
	if !v.Valid {
		return nil
	}
	t := v.Time
	return &t
}

// IsUniqueViolation reports a unique constraint violation (SQLSTATE 23505).
func IsUniqueViolation(err error) bool { return pgCode(err) == "23505" }

// IsForeignKeyViolation reports a foreign key violation (SQLSTATE 23503):
// a row that is still referenced, or a reference to a row that does not exist.
func IsForeignKeyViolation(err error) bool { return pgCode(err) == "23503" }

func pgCode(err error) string {
	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
	if errors.As(err, &pgErr) {
		return pgErr.Code
	}
	return ""
}
```
