- [ ] **Step 10: Implement the SQL store**

`internal/sshconf/store_sql.go`:

```go
package sshconf

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
)

// SQLStore implements Store on *db.DB. The composite foreign keys in
// migration 00014 make a reference into another organization fail as a
// foreign key violation, reported here as ErrInvalidInput; the service
// normally rejects it earlier with a clearer message.
type SQLStore struct{ q *gen.Queries }

// NewSQLStore builds the store.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{q: gen.New(d)} }

func (s *SQLStore) CreateBastion(ctx context.Context, b Bastion) (Bastion, error) {
	row, err := s.q.CreateBastion(ctx, gen.CreateBastionParams{
		ID: b.ID, OrgID: b.OrgID, Name: b.Name, Address: b.Address, Port: int32(b.Port), Username: b.Username,
		CredentialID: db.NullUUID(b.CredentialID), ViaBastionID: db.NullUUID(b.ViaBastionID),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Bastion{}, mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	return toBastion(row), nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) GetBastion(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Bastion, error) {
	row, err := s.q.GetBastion(ctx, gen.GetBastionParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return Bastion{}, mapErr(err)
	}
	return toBastion(row), nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListBastions(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Bastion, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListBastions(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Bastion, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = toBastion(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) UpdateBastion(ctx context.Context, b Bastion) (Bastion, error) {
	row, err := s.q.UpdateBastion(ctx, gen.UpdateBastionParams{
		OrgID: b.OrgID, ID: b.ID, Name: b.Name, Address: b.Address, Port: int32(b.Port), Username: b.Username,
		CredentialID: db.NullUUID(b.CredentialID), ViaBastionID: db.NullUUID(b.ViaBastionID),
	})
	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return Bastion{}, ErrNotFound
		}
		return Bastion{}, mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	return toBastion(row), nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) DeleteBastion(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := s.q.DeleteBastion(ctx, gen.DeleteBastionParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		if db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err) {
			return ErrInUse
		}
		return err
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) SetZoneBastion(ctx context.Context, orgID, zoneID uuid.UUID, bastionID *uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := s.q.SetZoneBastion(ctx, gen.SetZoneBastionParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: zoneID, BastionID: db.NullUUID(bastionID)})
	if err != nil {
		return mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ZoneBastions(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListZoneBastions(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make(map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID, len(rows))
	for _, r := range rows {
		if id := db.UUIDPtr(r.BastionID); id != nil {
			out[r.ID] = *id
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) GetHostSSH(ctx context.Context, orgID, hostID uuid.UUID) (HostSSH, error) {
	row, err := s.q.GetHostSSH(ctx, gen.GetHostSSHParams{OrgID: orgID, HostID: hostID})
	if err != nil {
		return HostSSH{}, mapErr(err)
	}
	return toHostSSH(row), nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) PutHostSSH(ctx context.Context, h HostSSH) (HostSSH, error) {
	row, err := s.q.UpsertHostSSH(ctx, gen.UpsertHostSSHParams{
		HostID: h.HostID, OrgID: h.OrgID, Username: h.Username, Port: int32(h.Port),
		CredentialID: db.NullUUID(h.CredentialID), BastionMode: string(h.BastionMode), BastionID: db.NullUUID(h.BastionID),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return HostSSH{}, mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	return toHostSSH(row), nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListHostSSH(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]HostSSH, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListHostSSH(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]HostSSH, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		out[i] = toHostSSH(r)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func mapErr(err error) error {
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return err
}

func mapWriteErr(err error) error {
	switch {
	case db.IsUniqueViolation(err):
		return ErrNameTaken
	case db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err):
		return ErrInvalidInput
	default:
		return err
	}
}

func toBastion(r gen.Bastion) Bastion {
	return Bastion{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, Name: r.Name, Address: r.Address, Port: int(r.Port), Username: r.Username,
		CredentialID: db.UUIDPtr(r.CredentialID), ViaBastionID: db.UUIDPtr(r.ViaBastionID),
		CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt,
	}
}

func toHostSSH(r gen.HostSsh) HostSSH {
	return HostSSH{
		HostID: r.HostID, OrgID: r.OrgID, Username: r.Username, Port: int(r.Port),
		CredentialID: db.UUIDPtr(r.CredentialID), BastionMode: BastionMode(r.BastionMode), BastionID: db.UUIDPtr(r.BastionID),
	}
}
```

sqlc names the `host_ssh` model `HostSsh`; if your sqlc version names it differently, use the generated name.

### Task 4: Inventory groups, schema, values and memberships

Backlog 2.1–2.5: a group tree per organization, one schema per group, group-scoped values on the group, host-scoped values and overrides on each membership, server-side validation on every write, secrets sealed per row, and optimistic concurrency through `revision`. A pure `grouptree` package answers the structural questions for this service and for the renderer in Task 5.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/inventory/grouptree/tree.go`, test `internal/inventory/grouptree/tree_test.go`
- Create: `internal/db/migrations/00015_inventory.sql`
- Create: `internal/db/queries/inventory.sql`, then `sqlc generate`
- Create: `internal/inventory/service.go`, `internal/inventory/members.go`, `internal/inventory/secrets.go`, `internal/inventory/store_sql.go`
- Test: `internal/inventory/fake_test.go`, `internal/inventory/service_test.go`, `internal/inventory/members_test.go`, `internal/inventory/store_sql_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: package `schema` (Task 1), `sshconf.Bastion`, `sshconf.ErrNotFound`, `hosts.Host`, `hosts.ErrNotFound`, `crypto.Box` (as `Sealer`), `db` helpers (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - package `grouptree`: `Node{ID uuid.UUID; ParentID *uuid.UUID; Name string}`; `New(nodes []Node) Tree`; methods `Has(id) bool`, `Node(id) (Node, bool)`, `Roots() []uuid.UUID`, `Children(id) []uuid.UUID`, `Ancestors(id) []uuid.UUID` (nearest first), `Depth(id) int` (root 0), `Descendants(id) []uuid.UUID`, `IsDescendant(id, of uuid.UUID) bool`, `Height(id) int` (leaf 0). Children and roots are ordered by name.
  - package `inventory`: errors `ErrInvalidInput`, `ErrNotFound`, `ErrNameTaken`, `ErrHasChildren`, `ErrRevisionMismatch`, `ErrNotManaged`; `MaxGroupDepth = 10`; `Group{ID, OrgID uuid.UUID; ParentID *uuid.UUID; Name, Description string; Schema schema.Schema; Vars map[string]any; BastionID *uuid.UUID; Revision int64; CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time}`; `Membership{GroupID, HostID, OrgID uuid.UUID; HostName, GroupName string; Vars map[string]any; Revision int64; CreatedAt, UpdatedAt time.Time}`; `GroupInput{Name, Description string; ParentID *uuid.UUID}`; `GroupPatch{Name, Description string; ParentID, BastionID *uuid.UUID}`; `GroupView{Group; Problems []schema.Problem; Orphaned []string}`; `GroupSummary{GroupView; Members int}`; `MemberView{Membership; Complete bool; Problems []schema.Problem; Orphaned []string}`; `Store`, `HostLookup`, `BastionLookup`, `Sealer` interfaces; `NewService(store Store, hostLookup HostLookup, bastions BastionLookup, box Sealer) *Service` with `CreateGroup`, `GetGroup`, `ListGroups`, `UpdateGroup`, `DeleteGroup`, `SetSchema`, `SetVars`, `Assign`, `Unassign`, `Members`, `HostGroups`; `NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore`. Views always carry masked vars; `Group.Vars` inside the store carries sealed values.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tree tests**

`internal/inventory/grouptree/tree_test.go`:

```go
package grouptree_test

import (
	"slices"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/grouptree"
)

// build returns a tree and a name->id map for:
//
//	production
//	├── dbserver
//	└── webserver
//	    └── php
//	staging
func build() (grouptree.Tree, map[string]uuid.UUID) {
	ids := map[string]uuid.UUID{}
	for _, n := range []string{"production", "webserver", "php", "dbserver", "staging"} {
		ids[n] = uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	}
	p := func(n string) *uuid.UUID { id := ids[n]; return &id }
	return grouptree.New([]grouptree.Node{
		{ID: ids["php"], ParentID: p("webserver"), Name: "php"},
		{ID: ids["webserver"], ParentID: p("production"), Name: "webserver"},
		{ID: ids["production"], Name: "production"},
		{ID: ids["dbserver"], ParentID: p("production"), Name: "dbserver"},
		{ID: ids["staging"], Name: "staging"},
	}), ids
}

func names(t grouptree.Tree, ids []uuid.UUID) []string {
	out := make([]string, len(ids))
	for i, id := range ids {
		n, _ := t.Node(id)
		out[i] = n.Name
	}
	return out
}

func TestTreeStructure(t *testing.T) {
	tr, ids := build()
	if got := names(tr, tr.Roots()); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"production", "staging"}) {
		t.Errorf("Roots = %v", got)
	}
	if got := names(tr, tr.Children(ids["production"])); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"dbserver", "webserver"}) {
		t.Errorf("Children = %v", got)
	}
	if got := names(tr, tr.Ancestors(ids["php"])); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"webserver", "production"}) {
		t.Errorf("Ancestors = %v", got)
	}
	if got := names(tr, tr.Descendants(ids["production"])); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"dbserver", "webserver", "php"}) {
		t.Errorf("Descendants = %v", got)
	}
	for name, depth := range map[string]int{"production": 0, "webserver": 1, "php": 2, "staging": 0} {
		if got := tr.Depth(ids[name]); got != depth {
			t.Errorf("Depth(%s) = %d, want %d", name, got, depth)
		}
	}
	for name, height := range map[string]int{"production": 2, "webserver": 1, "php": 0, "staging": 0} {
		if got := tr.Height(ids[name]); got != height {
			t.Errorf("Height(%s) = %d, want %d", name, got, height)
		}
	}
	if !tr.IsDescendant(ids["php"], ids["production"]) || tr.IsDescendant(ids["production"], ids["php"]) || tr.IsDescendant(ids["php"], ids["php"]) {
		t.Error("IsDescendant")
	}
	if tr.IsDescendant(ids["staging"], ids["production"]) {
		t.Error("siblings of different roots are not descendants")
	}
	if !tr.Has(ids["php"]) || tr.Has(uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())) {
		t.Error("Has")
	}
	if _, ok := tr.Node(uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); ok {
		t.Error("Node of an unknown id")
	}
}

func TestTreeToleratesDanglingParentsAndCycles(t *testing.T) {
	a, b, orphan := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	missing := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	tr := grouptree.New([]grouptree.Node{
		{ID: a, ParentID: &b, Name: "a"},
		{ID: b, ParentID: &a, Name: "b"},
		{ID: orphan, ParentID: &missing, Name: "orphan"},
	})
	if got := names(tr, tr.Roots()); !slices.Equal(got, []string{"orphan"}) {
		t.Errorf("a node whose parent is missing is a root: %v", got)
	}
	if got := len(tr.Ancestors(a)); got != 1 {
		t.Errorf("Ancestors must stop at a cycle, got %d", got)
	}
	if tr.Depth(a) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("Depth in a cycle = %d", tr.Depth(a))
	}
	if tr.Height(a) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("Height in a cycle = %d", tr.Height(a))
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the tree**

`internal/inventory/grouptree/tree.go`:

```go
// Package grouptree answers structural questions about an organization's
// inventory group tree: roots, children, ancestors, depth and subtree
// height. It is pure and shared by the inventory service (moves, depth
// limits) and the renderer. It never loops forever: a cycle or a dangling
// parent in its input (which the service's own checks prevent) is
// tolerated, not trusted.
package grouptree

import (
	"cmp"
	"slices"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// Node is one group as the tree sees it.
type Node struct {
	ID       uuid.UUID
	ParentID *uuid.UUID
	Name     string
}

// Tree indexes nodes by id and by parent.
type Tree struct {
	nodes    map[uuid.UUID]Node
	children map[uuid.UUID][]uuid.UUID
	roots    []uuid.UUID
}

// New builds a tree. A node whose parent is not among nodes is a root.
func New(nodes []Node) Tree {
	t := Tree{nodes: make(map[uuid.UUID]Node, len(nodes)), children: map[uuid.UUID][]uuid.UUID{}}
	for _, n := range nodes {
		t.nodes[n.ID] = n
	}
	for _, n := range nodes {
		if n.ParentID != nil {
			if _, ok := t.nodes[*n.ParentID]; ok {
				t.children[*n.ParentID] = append(t.children[*n.ParentID], n.ID)
				continue
			}
		}
		t.roots = append(t.roots, n.ID)
	}
	byName := func(a, b uuid.UUID) int { return cmp.Compare(t.nodes[a].Name, t.nodes[b].Name) }
	slices.SortFunc(t.roots, byName)
	for _, c := range t.children {
		slices.SortFunc(c, byName)
	}
	return t
}

// Has reports whether id is in the tree.
func (t Tree) Has(id uuid.UUID) bool { _, ok := t.nodes[id]; return ok }

// Node returns the node with id.
func (t Tree) Node(id uuid.UUID) (Node, bool) { n, ok := t.nodes[id]; return n, ok }

// Roots returns the root ids ordered by name.
func (t Tree) Roots() []uuid.UUID { return slices.Clone(t.roots) }

// Children returns the direct children of id ordered by name.
func (t Tree) Children(id uuid.UUID) []uuid.UUID { return slices.Clone(t.children[id]) }

// Ancestors returns the ancestors of id, nearest first, without id itself.
func (t Tree) Ancestors(id uuid.UUID) []uuid.UUID {
	var out []uuid.UUID
	seen := map[uuid.UUID]bool{id: true}
	n, ok := t.nodes[id]
	for ok && n.ParentID != nil {
		p := *n.ParentID
		if seen[p] || !t.Has(p) {
			break
		}
		seen[p] = true
		out = append(out, p)
		n, ok = t.nodes[p]
	}
	return out
}

// Depth is the number of ancestors: 0 for a root.
func (t Tree) Depth(id uuid.UUID) int { return len(t.Ancestors(id)) }

// Descendants returns every node below id, depth first, children by name.
func (t Tree) Descendants(id uuid.UUID) []uuid.UUID {
	var out []uuid.UUID
	seen := map[uuid.UUID]bool{id: true}
	var walk func(uuid.UUID)
	walk = func(n uuid.UUID) {
		for _, c := range t.children[n] {
			if seen[c] {
				continue
			}
			seen[c] = true
			out = append(out, c)
			walk(c)
		}
	}
	walk(id)
	return out
}

// IsDescendant reports whether id lies strictly below of.
func (t Tree) IsDescendant(id, of uuid.UUID) bool {
	return slices.Contains(t.Ancestors(id), of)
}

// Height is the length of the longest path from id down to a leaf: 0 for a
// leaf.
func (t Tree) Height(id uuid.UUID) int {
	seen := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	var h func(uuid.UUID) int
	h = func(n uuid.UUID) int {
		if seen[n] {
			return -1
		}
		seen[n] = true
		best := 0
		for _, c := range t.children[n] {
			if d := h(c) + 1; d > best {
				best = d
			}
		}
		return best
	}
	return h(id)
}
```

Check the cycle case in the test against this code: `a` and `b` are each other's parent, so both have a parent in the set and neither is a root; `Ancestors(a)` returns `[b]` (stops when it would revisit `a`), `Depth(a)` is 1, and `Height(a)` walks `a → b → a` where the revisit contributes `-1 + 1 = 0`, so `b` has height 0 and `a` height 1.

Run: `go test -race -cover ./internal/inventory/grouptree/...` → PASS, ≥ 90 %.

- [ ] **Step 3: Migration and queries**

`internal/db/migrations/00015_inventory.sql`:

```sql
-- +goose Up
-- Inventory groups and memberships (spec §6, backlog 2.1-2.5).
--
-- schema is the group's variable schema (internal/inventory/schema JSON);
-- vars holds the group-scoped values ("values" is an SQL keyword). Secret
-- values inside vars are stored as {"$sealed": "<base64>"}, sealed with
-- "inventory-group:<org>:<group>" as additional data. revision backs the
-- If-Match concurrency check on every group write.
--
-- Group names are unique per organization because Ansible's group namespace
-- is flat. A group with children cannot be deleted (the parent foreign key
-- has no cascade); deleting a group removes its memberships.
CREATE TABLE inventory_groups (
  id          uuid PRIMARY KEY,
  org_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  parent_id   uuid,
  name        text NOT NULL,
  description text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  schema      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{"fields": []}',
  vars        jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  bastion_id  uuid,
  revision    bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (org_id, name),
  UNIQUE (org_id, id),
  CHECK (parent_id IS DISTINCT FROM id),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, parent_id) REFERENCES inventory_groups (org_id, id),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, bastion_id) REFERENCES bastions (org_id, id)
);
CREATE INDEX inventory_groups_parent ON inventory_groups (org_id, parent_id);

-- vars holds the host-scoped values and the overrides of overridable
-- group-scoped fields; secrets are sealed with
-- "inventory-member:<org>:<group>:<host>". Deleting the group or the host
-- removes the membership.
CREATE TABLE inventory_memberships (
  group_id   uuid NOT NULL,
  host_id    uuid NOT NULL,
  org_id     uuid NOT NULL,
  vars       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  revision   bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (group_id, host_id),
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, group_id) REFERENCES inventory_groups (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  FOREIGN KEY (org_id, host_id) REFERENCES hosts (org_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE INDEX inventory_memberships_host ON inventory_memberships (org_id, host_id);

ALTER TABLE inventory_groups ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE inventory_groups FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON inventory_groups
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON inventory_groups
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');
ALTER TABLE inventory_memberships ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE inventory_memberships FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY org_isolation ON inventory_memberships
  USING (org_id = NULLIF(current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true), '')::uuid);
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_bypass ON inventory_memberships
  USING (current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) IS NULL OR current_setting('pulsix.org_id', true) = '');

-- +goose Down
DROP TABLE inventory_memberships;
DROP TABLE inventory_groups;
```

`internal/db/queries/inventory.sql`:

```sql
-- name: CreateInventoryGroup :one
INSERT INTO inventory_groups (id, org_id, parent_id, name, description, schema, vars, bastion_id)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8)
RETURNING *;

-- name: GetInventoryGroup :one
SELECT * FROM inventory_groups WHERE org_id = $1 AND id = $2;

-- name: ListInventoryGroups :many
SELECT * FROM inventory_groups WHERE org_id = $1 ORDER BY name;

-- name: UpdateInventoryGroup :one
-- Writes only when the caller's revision is still current; no row back means
-- a stale revision or an unknown group (the store tells the two apart).
UPDATE inventory_groups
SET parent_id = @parent_id, name = @name, description = @description, schema = @schema,
    vars = @vars, bastion_id = @bastion_id, revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND id = @id AND revision = @revision
RETURNING *;

-- name: DeleteInventoryGroup :execrows
DELETE FROM inventory_groups WHERE org_id = $1 AND id = $2;

-- name: CountInventoryMembers :many
SELECT group_id, count(*)::int AS members
FROM inventory_memberships WHERE org_id = $1 GROUP BY group_id;

-- name: UpsertInventoryMembership :one
INSERT INTO inventory_memberships (group_id, host_id, org_id, vars)
VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
ON CONFLICT (group_id, host_id) DO UPDATE
SET vars = EXCLUDED.vars, revision = inventory_memberships.revision + 1, updated_at = now()
RETURNING *;

-- name: UpdateInventoryMembership :one
UPDATE inventory_memberships
SET vars = @vars, revision = revision + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE org_id = @org_id AND group_id = @group_id AND host_id = @host_id AND revision = @revision
RETURNING *;

-- name: GetInventoryMembership :one
SELECT * FROM inventory_memberships WHERE org_id = $1 AND group_id = $2 AND host_id = $3;

-- name: DeleteInventoryMembership :execrows
DELETE FROM inventory_memberships WHERE org_id = $1 AND group_id = $2 AND host_id = $3;

-- name: ListInventoryMemberships :many
SELECT * FROM inventory_memberships WHERE org_id = $1 ORDER BY group_id, host_id;

-- name: ListGroupMembers :many
SELECT m.*, h.name AS host_name
FROM inventory_memberships m
JOIN hosts h ON h.org_id = m.org_id AND h.id = m.host_id
WHERE m.org_id = $1 AND m.group_id = $2
ORDER BY h.name;

-- name: ListHostMemberships :many
SELECT m.*, g.name AS group_name
FROM inventory_memberships m
JOIN inventory_groups g ON g.org_id = m.org_id AND g.id = m.group_id
WHERE m.org_id = $1 AND m.host_id = $2
ORDER BY g.name;
```

Run: `sqlc generate && go build ./...` → build passes; `gen.InventoryGroup` has `Schema []byte`, `Vars []byte`, `ParentID pgtype.UUID`, `BastionID pgtype.UUID`, `Revision int64`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Test fakes for the service**

`internal/inventory/fake_test.go`:

```go
package inventory_test

import (
	"context"
	"sort"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/sshconf"
)

var ctx = context.Background()

type memberKey struct{ group, host uuid.UUID }

// fakeStore mirrors the SQL store's contract: revision checks, name
// uniqueness, the children guard on delete, and cascading memberships.
type fakeStore struct {
	groups  map[uuid.UUID]inventory.Group
	members map[memberKey]inventory.Membership
	hosts   fakeHosts
}

func newFakeStore(h fakeHosts) *fakeStore {
	return &fakeStore{groups: map[uuid.UUID]inventory.Group{}, members: map[memberKey]inventory.Membership{}, hosts: h}
}

func (f *fakeStore) CreateGroup(_ context.Context, g inventory.Group) (inventory.Group, error) {
	for _, x := range f.groups {
		if x.OrgID == g.OrgID && x.Name == g.Name {
			return inventory.Group{}, inventory.ErrNameTaken
		}
	}
	g.Revision = 1
	f.groups[g.ID] = g
	return g, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) GetGroup(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (inventory.Group, error) {
	g, ok := f.groups[id]
	if !ok || g.OrgID != orgID {
		return inventory.Group{}, inventory.ErrNotFound
	}
	return g, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) ListGroups(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]inventory.Group, error) {
	var out []inventory.Group
	for _, g := range f.groups {
		if g.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, g)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Name < out[j].Name })
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) UpdateGroup(_ context.Context, g inventory.Group) (inventory.Group, error) {
	cur, ok := f.groups[g.ID]
	if !ok || cur.OrgID != g.OrgID {
		return inventory.Group{}, inventory.ErrNotFound
	}
	if cur.Revision != g.Revision {
		return inventory.Group{}, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	for _, x := range f.groups {
		if x.ID != g.ID && x.OrgID == g.OrgID && x.Name == g.Name {
			return inventory.Group{}, inventory.ErrNameTaken
		}
	}
	g.Revision++
	f.groups[g.ID] = g
	return g, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) DeleteGroup(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	g, ok := f.groups[id]
	if !ok || g.OrgID != orgID {
		return inventory.ErrNotFound
	}
	for _, x := range f.groups {
		if x.ParentID != nil && *x.ParentID == id {
			return inventory.ErrHasChildren
		}
	}
	delete(f.groups, id)
	for k := range f.members {
		if k.group == id {
			delete(f.members, k)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) MemberCounts(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]int, error) {
	out := map[uuid.UUID]int{}
	for _, m := range f.members {
		if m.OrgID == orgID {
			out[m.GroupID]++
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) PutMembership(_ context.Context, m inventory.Membership) (inventory.Membership, error) {
	k := memberKey{m.GroupID, m.HostID}
	m.Revision = f.members[k].Revision + 1
	f.members[k] = m
	return m, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) UpdateMembership(_ context.Context, m inventory.Membership) (inventory.Membership, error) {
	k := memberKey{m.GroupID, m.HostID}
	cur, ok := f.members[k]
	if !ok || cur.OrgID != m.OrgID {
		return inventory.Membership{}, inventory.ErrNotFound
	}
	if cur.Revision != m.Revision {
		return inventory.Membership{}, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	m.Revision++
	f.members[k] = m
	return m, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) GetMembership(_ context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) (inventory.Membership, error) {
	m, ok := f.members[memberKey{groupID, hostID}]
	if !ok || m.OrgID != orgID {
		return inventory.Membership{}, inventory.ErrNotFound
	}
	return m, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) DeleteMembership(_ context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) error {
	k := memberKey{groupID, hostID}
	if m, ok := f.members[k]; !ok || m.OrgID != orgID {
		return inventory.ErrNotFound
	}
	delete(f.members, k)
	return nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) ListMemberships(_ context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]inventory.Membership, error) {
	var out []inventory.Membership
	for _, m := range f.members {
		if m.OrgID == orgID {
			out = append(out, m)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) ListGroupMembers(_ context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]inventory.Membership, error) {
	var out []inventory.Membership
	for _, m := range f.members {
		if m.OrgID == orgID && m.GroupID == groupID {
			m.HostName = f.hosts[m.HostID].Name
			out = append(out, m)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].HostName < out[j].HostName })
	return out, nil
}

func (f *fakeStore) ListHostMemberships(_ context.Context, orgID, hostID uuid.UUID) ([]inventory.Membership, error) {
	var out []inventory.Membership
	for _, m := range f.members {
		if m.OrgID == orgID && m.HostID == hostID {
			m.GroupName = f.groups[m.GroupID].Name
			out = append(out, m)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].GroupName < out[j].GroupName })
	return out, nil
}

type fakeHosts map[uuid.UUID]hosts.Host

func (f fakeHosts) GetHost(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (hosts.Host, error) {
	h, ok := f[id]
	if !ok || h.OrgID != orgID {
		return hosts.Host{}, hosts.ErrNotFound
	}
	return h, nil
}

type fakeBastions map[uuid.UUID]sshconf.Bastion

func (f fakeBastions) GetBastion(_ context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (sshconf.Bastion, error) {
	b, ok := f[id]
	if !ok || b.OrgID != orgID {
		return sshconf.Bastion{}, sshconf.ErrNotFound
	}
	return b, nil
}
```

`internal/inventory/service_test.go`:

```go
package inventory_test

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/crypto"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

func testBox() *crypto.Box {
	var key [32]byte
	copy(key[:], "inventory-test-key-0123456789abc")
	return crypto.NewBox(key)
}

type fixture struct {
	svc       *inventory.Service
	store     *fakeStore
	org       uuid.UUID
	web01     uuid.UUID // managed
	switch01  uuid.UUID // not managed
	bastionID uuid.UUID
}

func newFixture() fixture {
	f := fixture{org: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), web01: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), switch01: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), bastionID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())}
	hs := fakeHosts{
		f.web01:    {ID: f.web01, OrgID: f.org, Name: "web-01", Managed: true},
		f.switch01: {ID: f.switch01, OrgID: f.org, Name: "switch-01", Monitored: true},
	}
	f.store = newFakeStore(hs)
	bastions := fakeBastions{f.bastionID: {ID: f.bastionID, OrgID: f.org, Name: "bastion"}}
	f.svc = inventory.NewService(f.store, hs, bastions, testBox())
	return f
}

func (f fixture) group(t *testing.T, name string, parent *uuid.UUID) inventory.GroupView {
	t.Helper()
	g, err := f.svc.CreateGroup(ctx, f.org, inventory.GroupInput{Name: name, ParentID: parent})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("CreateGroup(%s): %v", name, err)
	}
	return g
}

func problemAt(t *testing.T, err error, path, substr string) {
	t.Helper()
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	if !errors.As(err, &ve) {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want *schema.ValidationError", err)
	}
	for _, p := range ve.Problems {
		if p.Path == path && strings.Contains(p.Message, substr) {
			return
		}
	}
	t.Fatalf("problems %+v lack %q at %q", ve.Problems, substr, path)
}

func TestCreateGroup(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	prod := f.group(t, "production", nil)
	if prod.Revision != 1 || len(prod.Schema.Fields) != 0 || len(prod.Vars) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("new group = %+v", prod)
	}
	web := f.group(t, "webserver", &prod.ID)
	if web.ParentID == nil || *web.ParentID != prod.ID {
		t.Fatalf("parent = %v", web.ParentID)
	}
	unknown := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		in   inventory.GroupInput
		want error
		msg  string
	}{
		{"reserved all", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "all"}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "reserved"},
		{"reserved ungrouped", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "ungrouped"}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "reserved"},
		{"dash", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "web-servers"}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "letters, digits and underscores"},
		{"uppercase", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "Web"}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "letters, digits and underscores"},
		{"long description", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "x", Description: strings.Repeat("d", 1025)}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "description"},
		{"unknown parent", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "x", ParentID: &unknown}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "parent"},
		{"duplicate", inventory.GroupInput{Name: "webserver"}, inventory.ErrNameTaken, ""},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := f.svc.CreateGroup(ctx, f.org, c.in)
			if !errors.Is(err, c.want) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), c.msg) {
				t.Fatalf("err = %v, want %v mentioning %q", err, c.want, c.msg)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCreateGroupDepthLimit(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	var parent *uuid.UUID
	for i := 0; i < inventory.MaxGroupDepth; i++ {
		g := f.group(t, fmt.Sprintf("level_%d", i), parent)
		id := g.ID
		parent = &id
	}
	_, err := f.svc.CreateGroup(ctx, f.org, inventory.GroupInput{Name: "too_deep", ParentID: parent})
	if !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrInvalidInput) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "levels") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestUpdateGroup(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	prod := f.group(t, "production", nil)
	web := f.group(t, "webserver", &prod.ID)
	php := f.group(t, "php", &web.ID)
	staging := f.group(t, "staging", nil)
	unknown := uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())

	cases := []struct {
		name  string
		id    uuid.UUID
		rev   int64
		patch inventory.GroupPatch
		want  error
		msg   string
	}{
		{"below itself", web.ID, web.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "webserver", ParentID: &web.ID}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "below itself"},
		{"below own descendant", prod.ID, prod.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "production", ParentID: &php.ID}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "below itself"},
		{"unknown parent", web.ID, web.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "webserver", ParentID: &unknown}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "parent"},
		{"unknown bastion", web.ID, web.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "webserver", ParentID: &prod.ID, BastionID: &unknown}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "jump host"},
		{"stale revision", web.ID, web.Revision + 5, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "webserver", ParentID: &prod.ID}, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch, ""},
		{"unknown group", unknown, 1, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "x"}, inventory.ErrNotFound, ""},
		{"bad name", web.ID, web.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "all"}, inventory.ErrInvalidInput, "reserved"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := f.svc.UpdateGroup(ctx, f.org, c.id, c.rev, c.patch)
			if !errors.Is(err, c.want) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), c.msg) {
				t.Fatalf("err = %v, want %v mentioning %q", err, c.want, c.msg)
			}
		})
	}

	moved, err := f.svc.UpdateGroup(ctx, f.org, web.ID, web.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{
		Name: "webserver", Description: "Web tier", ParentID: &staging.ID, BastionID: &f.bastionID,
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("move: %v", err)
	}
	if *moved.ParentID != staging.ID || moved.Revision != web.Revision+1 || *moved.BastionID != f.bastionID || moved.Description != "Web tier" {
		t.Fatalf("moved = %+v", moved)
	}
	root, err := f.svc.UpdateGroup(ctx, f.org, web.ID, moved.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "webserver"})
	if err != nil || root.ParentID != nil || root.BastionID != nil {
		t.Fatalf("to root = %+v, %v", root, err)
	}
}

func TestUpdateGroupDepthCountsTheSubtree(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	// A chain of MaxGroupDepth-1 levels, and a separate group with one child.
	var parent *uuid.UUID
	for i := 0; i < inventory.MaxGroupDepth-1; i++ {
		g := f.group(t, fmt.Sprintf("level_%d", i), parent)
		id := g.ID
		parent = &id
	}
	sub := f.group(t, "sub", nil)
	f.group(t, "sub_child", &sub.ID)
	_, err := f.svc.UpdateGroup(ctx, f.org, sub.ID, sub.Revision, inventory.GroupPatch{Name: "sub", ParentID: parent})
	if !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrInvalidInput) || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "levels") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestDeleteGroup(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	prod := f.group(t, "production", nil)
	web := f.group(t, "webserver", &prod.ID)
	if err := f.svc.DeleteGroup(ctx, f.org, prod.ID); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrHasChildren) {
		t.Fatalf("delete parent: %v", err)
	}
	if err := f.svc.DeleteGroup(ctx, f.org, web.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := f.svc.GetGroup(ctx, f.org, web.ID); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("after delete: %v", err)
	}
}

func webSchema() schema.Schema {
	return schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{
		{Name: "env", Type: schema.TypeEnum, Options: []string{"prod", "staging"}, Required: true},
		{Name: "db_password", Type: schema.TypeSecret},
		{Name: "php_default", Type: schema.TypeEnum, Options: []string{"8.3", "8.4"}, Default: "8.3", Overridable: true},
		{Name: "vhosts", Type: schema.TypeRepeater, Scope: schema.ScopeHost, Required: true, Fields: []schema.Field{
			{Name: "domain", Type: schema.TypeString, Required: true},
			{Name: "password", Type: schema.TypeSecret},
		}},
	}}
}

func TestSetSchema(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	bad := schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "ansible_host", Type: schema.TypeString}}}
	_, _, err := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, g.Revision, bad)
	problemAt(t, err, "fields[0].name", "ansible_")

	view, incomplete, err := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, g.Revision, webSchema())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if incomplete != 0 || view.Revision != g.Revision+1 || len(view.Schema.Fields) != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("view = %+v, incomplete = %d", view, incomplete)
	}
	if len(view.Problems) != 1 || view.Problems[0].Path != "env" {
		t.Fatalf("a new required group field must show as a problem: %+v", view.Problems)
	}
	if _, _, err := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, g.Revision, webSchema()); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale revision: %v", err)
	}
	empty, _, err := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, view.Revision, schema.Schema{})
	if err != nil || empty.Schema.Fields == nil {
		t.Fatalf("an empty schema is stored with an empty, non-nil field list: %+v, %v", empty.Schema, err)
	}
}

func TestSetVarsSealsKeepsAndMasks(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	v, _, err := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, g.Revision, webSchema())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	view, err := f.svc.SetVars(ctx, f.org, g.ID, v.Revision, map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": "hunter2"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SetVars: %v", err)
	}
	if view.Vars["db_password"].(map[string]any)["$secret"] != "set" || view.Vars["env"] != "prod" || len(view.Problems) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("view = %+v", view)
	}
	stored := f.store.groups[g.ID].Vars
	raw, _ := json.Marshal(stored)
	if strings.Contains(string(raw), "hunter2") {
		t.Fatal("plaintext secret reached the store")
	}
	ct, ok := schema.SealedBytes(stored["db_password"])
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("stored secret = %v", stored["db_password"])
	}
	plain, err := testBox().OpenAAD(ct, inventory.GroupAAD(f.org, g.ID))
	if err != nil || string(plain) != "hunter2" {
		t.Fatalf("sealed value = %q, %v", plain, err)
	}

	kept, err := f.svc.SetVars(ctx, f.org, g.ID, view.Revision, map[string]any{"env": "staging", "db_password": map[string]any{"$secret": "keep", "from": "db_password"}})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("keep: %v", err)
	}
	if ct2, _ := schema.SealedBytes(f.store.groups[g.ID].Vars["db_password"]); string(ct2) != string(ct) {
		t.Fatal("a keep marker must keep the stored ciphertext unchanged")
	}

	cases := []struct {
		name string
		vars map[string]any
		path string
		msg  string
	}{
		{"dangling keep", map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": map[string]any{"$secret": "keep", "from": "nope"}}, "db_password", "enter it again"},
		{"client-sealed value", map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": schema.Sealed([]byte("forged"))}, "db_password", "cannot be written"},
		{"unknown field", map[string]any{"env": "prod", "extra": 1.0}, "extra", "unknown field"},
		{"host field on the group", map[string]any{"env": "prod", "vhosts": []any{}}, "vhosts", "unknown field"},
		{"required missing", map[string]any{}, "env", "is required"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := f.svc.SetVars(ctx, f.org, g.ID, kept.Revision, c.vars)
			problemAt(t, err, c.path, c.msg)
		})
	}
	if _, err := f.svc.SetVars(ctx, f.org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), 1, nil); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown group: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestSchemaChangeKeepsValuesAndReportsOrphans(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	v, _, _ := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, g.Revision, webSchema())
	v, err := f.svc.SetVars(ctx, f.org, g.ID, v.Revision, map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": "x"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	reduced := webSchema()
	reduced.Fields = reduced.Fields[:1] // keep env only
	view, _, err := f.svc.SetSchema(ctx, f.org, g.ID, v.Revision, reduced)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(view.Orphaned) != 1 || view.Orphaned[0] != "db_password" {
		t.Fatalf("Orphaned = %v", view.Orphaned)
	}
	if _, ok := schema.SealedBytes(f.store.groups[g.ID].Vars["db_password"]); !ok {
		t.Fatal("a schema change must not rewrite stored values")
	}
	if view.Vars["db_password"].(map[string]any)["$secret"] != "set" {
		t.Fatal("an orphaned secret must still be masked on read")
	}
}

func TestListGroupsCountsMembers(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFixture()
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	f.group(t, "empty", nil)
	if _, err := f.svc.Assign(ctx, f.org, g.ID, f.web01, map[string]any{}, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	list, err := f.svc.ListGroups(ctx, f.org)
	if err != nil || len(list) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("ListGroups = %v, %v", list, err)
	}
	counts := map[string]int{}
	for _, s := range list {
		counts[s.Name] = s.Members
	}
	if counts["webserver"] != 1 || counts["empty"] != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("counts = %v", counts)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the service**

`internal/inventory/service.go`:

```go
// Package inventory implements Ansible inventory groups (spec §6, backlog
// 2.1-2.6): a group tree per organization, a typed variable schema per
// group, group-scoped values on the group, host-scoped values and overrides
// on each membership, and the renderer service that turns all of it into an
// Ansible dynamic inventory. Validation is internal/inventory/schema's;
// structure is internal/inventory/grouptree's; rendering is
// internal/inventory/render's. This package wires them to storage and seals
// secret values per row.
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"regexp"
	"time"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/grouptree"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/sshconf"
)

// Sentinel errors mapped to HTTP status by the transport layer. Invalid
// values come back as *schema.ValidationError instead, so the API can point
// at each field.
var (
	ErrInvalidInput     = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrNotFound         = errors.New("not found")
	ErrNameTaken        = errors.New("name already in use")
	ErrHasChildren      = errors.New("group has child groups")
	ErrRevisionMismatch = errors.New("revision does not match")
	ErrNotManaged       = errors.New("host is not managed")
)

// MaxGroupDepth is the number of levels a group tree may have.
const MaxGroupDepth = 10

const maxDescription = 1024

var groupNamePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[a-z_][a-z0-9_]{0,62}$`)

// reservedGroupNames are Ansible's implicit groups.
var reservedGroupNames = map[string]bool{"all": true, "ungrouped": true}

// Group is an inventory group. Inside the store, Vars carries sealed
// secrets; every value this package returns to callers has them masked.
type Group struct {
	ID          uuid.UUID
	OrgID       uuid.UUID
	ParentID    *uuid.UUID
	Name        string
	Description string
	Schema      schema.Schema
	Vars        map[string]any
	BastionID   *uuid.UUID
	Revision    int64
	CreatedAt   time.Time
	UpdatedAt   time.Time
}

// Membership assigns a managed host to a group with its host-scoped values
// and overrides. HostName and GroupName are filled by the list queries.
type Membership struct {
	GroupID   uuid.UUID
	HostID    uuid.UUID
	OrgID     uuid.UUID
	HostName  string
	GroupName string
	Vars      map[string]any
	Revision  int64
	CreatedAt time.Time
	UpdatedAt time.Time
}

// GroupInput creates a group.
type GroupInput struct {
	Name        string
	Description string
	ParentID    *uuid.UUID
}

// GroupPatch fully replaces a group's name, description, parent and jump
// host. A nil ParentID makes it a root; a nil BastionID clears the jump host.
type GroupPatch struct {
	Name        string
	Description string
	ParentID    *uuid.UUID
	BastionID   *uuid.UUID
}

// GroupView is a group as a reader sees it: vars masked, plus what is wrong
// with the stored values against the current schema.
type GroupView struct {
	Group
	Problems []schema.Problem
	Orphaned []string
}

// GroupSummary is a list entry.
type GroupSummary struct {
	GroupView
	Members int
}

// MemberView is a membership as a reader sees it.
type MemberView struct {
	Membership
	Complete bool
	Problems []schema.Problem
	Orphaned []string
}

// Store persists groups and memberships. Lookups that find nothing return
// ErrNotFound; UpdateGroup and UpdateMembership write only when the given
// Revision is current and return ErrRevisionMismatch otherwise.
type Store interface {
	CreateGroup(ctx context.Context, g Group) (Group, error)
	GetGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Group, error)
	ListGroups(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Group, error)
	UpdateGroup(ctx context.Context, g Group) (Group, error)
	// DeleteGroup returns ErrHasChildren while child groups exist.
	DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error
	MemberCounts(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]int, error)
	// PutMembership creates the membership or replaces its vars.
	PutMembership(ctx context.Context, m Membership) (Membership, error)
	UpdateMembership(ctx context.Context, m Membership) (Membership, error)
	GetMembership(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) (Membership, error)
	DeleteMembership(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) error
	ListMemberships(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Membership, error)
	ListGroupMembers(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]Membership, error)
	ListHostMemberships(ctx context.Context, orgID, hostID uuid.UUID) ([]Membership, error)
}

// HostLookup is the slice of hosts.Service this package needs.
type HostLookup interface {
	GetHost(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (hosts.Host, error)
}

// BastionLookup is the slice of sshconf.Service this package needs.
type BastionLookup interface {
	GetBastion(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (sshconf.Bastion, error)
}

// Sealer is the slice of crypto.Box this package needs.
type Sealer interface {
	SealAAD(plain, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)
	OpenAAD(sealed, aad []byte) ([]byte, error)
}

// Service implements inventory groups and memberships.
type Service struct {
	store    Store
	hosts    HostLookup
	bastions BastionLookup
	box      Sealer
}

// NewService wires the store, the lookups and the sealing box.
func NewService(store Store, hostLookup HostLookup, bastions BastionLookup, box Sealer) *Service {
	return &Service{store: store, hosts: hostLookup, bastions: bastions, box: box}
}

// CreateGroup creates an empty group, optionally below a parent.
func (s *Service) CreateGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, in GroupInput) (GroupView, error) {
	if err := validateGroupMeta(in.Name, in.Description); err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	if in.ParentID != nil {
		t, err := s.tree(ctx, orgID)
		if err != nil {
			return GroupView{}, err
		}
		if !t.Has(*in.ParentID) {
			return GroupView{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: parent group does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		if t.Depth(*in.ParentID)+2 > MaxGroupDepth {
			return GroupView{}, errTooDeep()
		}
	}
	g := Group{
		ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: orgID, ParentID: in.ParentID, Name: in.Name, Description: in.Description,
		Schema: schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{}}, Vars: map[string]any{},
	}
	created, err := s.store.CreateGroup(ctx, g)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	return groupView(created), nil
}

// GetGroup returns one group with masked vars and its value problems.
func (s *Service) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (GroupView, error) {
	g, err := s.store.GetGroup(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	return groupView(g), nil
}

// ListGroups returns every group, ordered by name, with its member count.
func (s *Service) ListGroups(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]GroupSummary, error) {
	groups, err := s.store.ListGroups(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	counts, err := s.store.MemberCounts(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]GroupSummary, len(groups))
	for i, g := range groups {
		out[i] = GroupSummary{GroupView: groupView(g), Members: counts[g.ID]}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// UpdateGroup renames, moves, describes and sets the jump host of a group.
// A move may not place a group below itself and may not make the tree
// deeper than MaxGroupDepth.
func (s *Service) UpdateGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, in GroupPatch) (GroupView, error) {
	if err := validateGroupMeta(in.Name, in.Description); err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	cur, err := s.store.GetGroup(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	t, err := s.tree(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	levels := 1 + t.Height(id)
	if in.ParentID != nil {
		p := *in.ParentID
		switch {
		case p == id || t.IsDescendant(p, id):
			return GroupView{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: a group cannot move below itself", ErrInvalidInput)
		case !t.Has(p):
			return GroupView{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: parent group does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
		}
		levels += t.Depth(p) + 1
	}
	if levels > MaxGroupDepth {
		return GroupView{}, errTooDeep()
	}
	if in.BastionID != nil {
		if _, err := s.bastions.GetBastion(ctx, orgID, *in.BastionID); errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrNotFound) {
			return GroupView{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: jump host does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
		} else if err != nil {
			return GroupView{}, err
		}
	}
	next := cur
	next.Name, next.Description, next.ParentID, next.BastionID, next.Revision = in.Name, in.Description, in.ParentID, in.BastionID, revision
	updated, err := s.store.UpdateGroup(ctx, next)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	return groupView(updated), nil
}

// DeleteGroup removes a group without children, and its memberships.
func (s *Service) DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.store.DeleteGroup(ctx, orgID, id)
}

// SetSchema replaces a group's schema. Stored values are never rewritten;
// the second result counts the members whose values no longer satisfy the
// new schema.
func (s *Service) SetSchema(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, sc schema.Schema) (GroupView, int, error) {
	if sc.Fields == nil {
		sc.Fields = []schema.Field{}
	}
	if err := sc.Check(); err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, 0, err
	}
	cur, err := s.store.GetGroup(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, 0, err
	}
	next := cur
	next.Schema, next.Revision = sc, revision
	updated, err := s.store.UpdateGroup(ctx, next)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, 0, err
	}
	members, err := s.store.ListGroupMembers(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, 0, err
	}
	incomplete := 0
	for _, m := range members {
		if schema.ValidateStored(sc.MembershipFields(), m.Vars) != nil {
			incomplete++
		}
	}
	return groupView(updated), incomplete, nil
}

// SetVars replaces a group's group-scoped values after strict validation.
// Plaintext secrets are sealed; keep markers keep the stored secret.
func (s *Service) SetVars(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID, revision int64, vars map[string]any) (GroupView, error) {
	cur, err := s.store.GetGroup(ctx, orgID, id)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	sealed, err := s.prepareVars(GroupAAD(orgID, id), cur.Schema.GroupFields(), vars, cur.Vars)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	next := cur
	next.Vars, next.Revision = sealed, revision
	updated, err := s.store.UpdateGroup(ctx, next)
	if err != nil {
		return GroupView{}, err
	}
	return groupView(updated), nil
}

func (s *Service) tree(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (grouptree.Tree, error) {
	groups, err := s.store.ListGroups(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return grouptree.Tree{}, err
	}
	return treeOf(groups), nil
}

func treeOf(groups []Group) grouptree.Tree {
	nodes := make([]grouptree.Node, len(groups))
	for i, g := range groups {
		nodes[i] = grouptree.Node{ID: g.ID, ParentID: g.ParentID, Name: g.Name}
	}
	return grouptree.New(nodes)
}

func validateGroupMeta(name, description string) error {
	if reservedGroupNames[name] {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %q is reserved by Ansible", ErrInvalidInput, name)
	}
	if !groupNamePattern.MatchString(name) {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: group names start with a lowercase letter or underscore and contain only lowercase letters, digits and underscores (at most 63)", ErrInvalidInput)
	}
	if utf8.RuneCountInString(description) > maxDescription {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: description must be at most %d characters", ErrInvalidInput, maxDescription)
	}
	return nil
}

func errTooDeep() error {
	return fmt.Errorf("%w: group trees have at most %d levels", ErrInvalidInput, MaxGroupDepth)
}

func groupView(g Group) GroupView {
	fields := g.Schema.GroupFields()
	v := GroupView{Group: g, Orphaned: schema.Orphans(fields, g.Vars)}
	v.Vars = schema.MaskVars(g.Vars)
	if err := schema.ValidateStored(fields, g.Vars); err != nil {
		var ve *schema.ValidationError
		if errors.As(err, &ve) {
			v.Problems = ve.Problems
		}
	}
	return v
}
```

`internal/inventory/members.go`:

```go
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

// Assign creates or replaces a host's membership in a group. The host must
// be managed. vars may carry the group's host-scoped fields and overrides of
// its overridable group-scoped fields. A non-nil revision makes the write
// conditional on the stored membership's revision.
func (s *Service) Assign(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID, vars map[string]any, revision *int64) (MemberView, error) {
	g, err := s.store.GetGroup(ctx, orgID, groupID)
	if err != nil {
		return MemberView{}, err
	}
	h, err := s.hosts.GetHost(ctx, orgID, hostID)
	if errors.Is(err, hosts.ErrNotFound) {
		return MemberView{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return MemberView{}, err
	}
	if !h.Managed {
		return MemberView{}, ErrNotManaged
	}
	prev, err := s.store.GetMembership(ctx, orgID, groupID, hostID)
	exists := err == nil
	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ErrNotFound) {
		return MemberView{}, err
	}
	if revision != nil && (!exists || prev.Revision != *revision) {
		return MemberView{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	sealed, err := s.prepareVars(MemberAAD(orgID, groupID, hostID), g.Schema.MembershipFields(), vars, prev.Vars)
	if err != nil {
		return MemberView{}, err
	}
	m := Membership{GroupID: groupID, HostID: hostID, OrgID: orgID, Vars: sealed}
	var saved Membership
	if revision != nil {
		m.Revision = *revision
		saved, err = s.store.UpdateMembership(ctx, m)
	} else {
		saved, err = s.store.PutMembership(ctx, m)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return MemberView{}, err
	}
	saved.HostName, saved.GroupName = h.Name, g.Name
	return memberView(g, saved), nil
}

// Unassign removes a host from a group.
func (s *Service) Unassign(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) error {
	return s.store.DeleteMembership(ctx, orgID, groupID, hostID)
}

// Members lists a group's members by host name, each with its completeness
// against the current schema.
func (s *Service) Members(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]MemberView, error) {
	g, err := s.store.GetGroup(ctx, orgID, groupID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ms, err := s.store.ListGroupMembers(ctx, orgID, groupID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]MemberView, len(ms))
	for i, m := range ms {
		m.GroupName = g.Name
		out[i] = memberView(g, m)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// HostGroups lists the groups a host is a direct member of, by group name.
func (s *Service) HostGroups(ctx context.Context, orgID, hostID uuid.UUID) ([]MemberView, error) {
	if _, err := s.hosts.GetHost(ctx, orgID, hostID); errors.Is(err, hosts.ErrNotFound) {
		return nil, ErrNotFound
	} else if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ms, err := s.store.ListHostMemberships(ctx, orgID, hostID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	groups, err := s.store.ListGroups(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	byID := make(map[uuid.UUID]Group, len(groups))
	for _, g := range groups {
		byID[g.ID] = g
	}
	out := make([]MemberView, len(ms))
	for i, m := range ms {
		out[i] = memberView(byID[m.GroupID], m)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func memberView(g Group, m Membership) MemberView {
	fields := g.Schema.MembershipFields()
	v := MemberView{Membership: m, Complete: true, Orphaned: schema.Orphans(fields, m.Vars)}
	v.Vars = schema.MaskVars(m.Vars)
	if err := schema.ValidateStored(fields, m.Vars); err != nil {
		var ve *schema.ValidationError
		if errors.As(err, &ve) {
			v.Complete, v.Problems = false, ve.Problems
		}
	}
	return v
}
```

`internal/inventory/secrets.go`:

```go
package inventory

import (
	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

// GroupAAD is the additional data group-scoped secrets are sealed with.
func GroupAAD(orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) []byte {
	return []byte("inventory-group:" + orgID.String() + ":" + groupID.String())
}

// MemberAAD is the additional data membership secrets are sealed with.
func MemberAAD(orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) []byte {
	return []byte("inventory-member:" + orgID.String() + ":" + groupID.String() + ":" + hostID.String())
}

// prepareVars turns a write into what is stored: plaintext secrets sealed
// with aad, keep markers replaced by the sealed value they point at in
// stored, and a client-supplied sealed value refused. It then validates the
// result strictly against fields. Every problem comes back as one
// *schema.ValidationError.
func (s *Service) prepareVars(aad []byte, fields []schema.Field, incoming, stored map[string]any) (map[string]any, error) {
	var problems []schema.Problem
	out, err := schema.MapSecrets(fields, incoming, func(path string, v any) (any, error) {
		if from, ok := schema.KeepFrom(v); ok {
			prev, found := schema.Lookup(stored, from)
			if _, sealed := schema.SealedBytes(prev); !found || !sealed {
				problems = append(problems, schema.Problem{Path: path, Message: "the stored secret this refers to no longer exists; enter it again"})
				return nil, nil
			}
			return prev, nil
		}
		if _, ok := schema.SealedBytes(v); ok {
			problems = append(problems, schema.Problem{Path: path, Message: "sealed values cannot be written directly"})
			return nil, nil
		}
		if plain, ok := v.(string); ok && plain != "" {
			ct, err := s.box.SealAAD([]byte(plain), aad)
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			return schema.Sealed(ct), nil
		}
		return v, nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(problems) > 0 {
		return nil, &schema.ValidationError{Problems: problems}
	}
	if err := schema.Validate(fields, out); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
package inventory_test

func TestSQLStore(t *testing.T) {

			t.Fatal(err)
		}

	}

	sc := schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "env", Type: schema.TypeString}}}
	prod, err := store.CreateGroup(ctx, inventory.Group{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Name: "production", Schema: sc, Vars: map[string]any{"env": "prod"}})

		t.Fatalf("duplicate: %v", err)

		t.Fatalf("parent in another org: %v", err)

	if err != nil {

	}

	// Revision handling.

	if err != nil || updated.Revision != 2 || updated.Vars["env"] != "production" {
		t.Fatalf("UpdateGroup = %+v, %v", updated, err)
	}
	if _, err := store.UpdateGroup(ctx, prod); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch) {
		t.Fatalf("stale revision: %v", err)

	ghost := prod

	if list, err := store.ListGroups(ctx, org); err != nil || len(list) != 2 || list[0].Name != "production" {

	// Memberships.
	m, err := store.PutMembership(ctx, inventory.Membership{GroupID: web.ID, HostID: webA, OrgID: org, Vars: map[string]any{"env": "a"}})
	if err != nil || m.Revision != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("PutMembership = %+v, %v", m, err)

	m2, err := store.PutMembership(ctx, inventory.Membership{GroupID: web.ID, HostID: webA, OrgID: org, Vars: map[string]any{"env": "b"}})
	if err != nil || m2.Revision != 2 || m2.Vars["env"] != "b" {
		t.Fatalf("replace = %+v, %v", m2, err)

	m2.Vars = map[string]any{"env": "c"}
	if m3, err := store.UpdateMembership(ctx, m2); err != nil || m3.Revision != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("UpdateMembership = %+v, %v", m3, err)
	}
	if _, err := store.UpdateMembership(ctx, m2); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch) {

	}
	missing := inventory.Membership{GroupID: prod.ID, HostID: webA, OrgID: org, Revision: 1}
	if _, err := store.UpdateMembership(ctx, missing); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {

	if _, err := store.PutMembership(ctx, inventory.Membership{GroupID: web.ID, HostID: webB, OrgID: org, Vars: map[string]any{}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := store.PutMembership(ctx, inventory.Membership{GroupID: prod.ID, HostID: webA, OrgID: org, Vars: map[string]any{}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	members, err := store.ListGroupMembers(ctx, org, web.ID)
	if err != nil || len(members) != 2 || members[0].HostName != "web-a" || members[1].HostName != "web-b" {
		t.Fatalf("ListGroupMembers = %+v, %v", members, err)

	hostGroups, err := store.ListHostMemberships(ctx, org, webA)

		t.Fatalf("ListHostMemberships = %+v, %v", hostGroups, err)
	}
	counts, err := store.MemberCounts(ctx, org)
	if err != nil || counts[web.ID] != 2 || counts[prod.ID] != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("MemberCounts = %v, %v", counts, err)
	}
	if all, err := store.ListMemberships(ctx, org); err != nil || len(all) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("ListMemberships = %v, %v", all, err)
	}
	if got, err := store.GetMembership(ctx, org, web.ID, webA); err != nil || got.Vars["env"] != "c" {
		t.Fatalf("GetMembership = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}

	// A group's jump host blocks deleting the jump host.
	b, err := sshStore.CreateBastion(ctx, sshconf.Bastion{ID: uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()), OrgID: org, Name: "bastion", Address: "b.example", Port: 22, Username: "ops"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cur, _ := store.GetGroup(ctx, org, web.ID)
	cur.BastionID = &b.ID
	if _, err := store.UpdateGroup(ctx, cur); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := sshStore.DeleteBastion(ctx, org, b.ID); !errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrInUse) {
		t.Fatalf("delete referenced bastion: %v", err)
	}

	// Deletes.
	if err := store.DeleteGroup(ctx, org, prod.ID); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrHasChildren) {
		t.Fatalf("delete parent: %v", err)
	}
	if err := hostStore.DeleteHost(ctx, org, webB); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if members, _ := store.ListGroupMembers(ctx, org, web.ID); len(members) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("deleting a host must remove its memberships: %v", members)
	}
	if err := store.DeleteMembership(ctx, org, prod.ID, webA); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := store.DeleteMembership(ctx, org, prod.ID, webA); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second DeleteMembership: %v", err)
	}
	if err := store.DeleteGroup(ctx, org, web.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if all, _ := store.ListMemberships(ctx, org); len(all) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("deleting a group must remove its memberships: %v", all)
	}
	if err := store.DeleteGroup(ctx, org, web.ID); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("second DeleteGroup: %v", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement the SQL store**

`internal/inventory/store_sql.go`:

```go
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/db/gen"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
)

// SQLStore implements Store on *db.DB, so every statement runs inside the
// request's organization scope. Schemas and vars are stored as jsonb.
type SQLStore struct{ q *gen.Queries }

// NewSQLStore builds the store.
func NewSQLStore(d *db.DB) *SQLStore { return &SQLStore{q: gen.New(d)} }

func (s *SQLStore) CreateGroup(ctx context.Context, g Group) (Group, error) {
	sc, vars, err := encodeGroup(g)
	if err != nil {
		return Group{}, err
	}
	row, err := s.q.CreateInventoryGroup(ctx, gen.CreateInventoryGroupParams{
		ID: g.ID, OrgID: g.OrgID, ParentID: db.NullUUID(g.ParentID), Name: g.Name, Description: g.Description,
		Schema: sc, Vars: vars, BastionID: db.NullUUID(g.BastionID),
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Group{}, mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	return toGroup(row)
}

func (s *SQLStore) GetGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) (Group, error) {
	row, err := s.q.GetInventoryGroup(ctx, gen.GetInventoryGroupParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		return Group{}, mapErr(err)
	}
	return toGroup(row)
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListGroups(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Group, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListInventoryGroups(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Group, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		if out[i], err = toGroup(r); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) UpdateGroup(ctx context.Context, g Group) (Group, error) {
	sc, vars, err := encodeGroup(g)
	if err != nil {
		return Group{}, err
	}
	row, err := s.q.UpdateInventoryGroup(ctx, gen.UpdateInventoryGroupParams{
		ParentID: db.NullUUID(g.ParentID), Name: g.Name, Description: g.Description, Schema: sc, Vars: vars,
		BastionID: db.NullUUID(g.BastionID), OrgID: g.OrgID, ID: g.ID, Revision: g.Revision,
	})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		if _, getErr := s.q.GetInventoryGroup(ctx, gen.GetInventoryGroupParams{OrgID: g.OrgID, ID: g.ID}); getErr != nil {
			return Group{}, mapErr(getErr)
		}
		return Group{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Group{}, mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	return toGroup(row)
}

func (s *SQLStore) DeleteGroup(ctx context.Context, orgID, id uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := s.q.DeleteInventoryGroup(ctx, gen.DeleteInventoryGroupParams{OrgID: orgID, ID: id})
	if err != nil {
		if db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err) {
			return ErrHasChildren
		}
		return err
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) MemberCounts(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]int, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.CountInventoryMembers(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make(map[uuid.UUID]int, len(rows))
	for _, r := range rows {
		out[r.GroupID] = int(r.Members)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) PutMembership(ctx context.Context, m Membership) (Membership, error) {
	vars, err := json.Marshal(m.Vars)
	if err != nil {
		return Membership{}, fmt.Errorf("encode membership vars: %w", err)
	}
	row, err := s.q.UpsertInventoryMembership(ctx, gen.UpsertInventoryMembershipParams{GroupID: m.GroupID, HostID: m.HostID, OrgID: m.OrgID, Vars: vars})
	if err != nil {
		return Membership{}, mapWriteErr(err)
	}
	return toMembership(row.GroupID, row.HostID, row.OrgID, row.Vars, row.Revision, row.CreatedAt, row.UpdatedAt, "", "")
}

func (s *SQLStore) UpdateMembership(ctx context.Context, m Membership) (Membership, error) {
	vars, err := json.Marshal(m.Vars)
	if err != nil {
		return Membership{}, fmt.Errorf("encode membership vars: %w", err)
	}
	row, err := s.q.UpdateInventoryMembership(ctx, gen.UpdateInventoryMembershipParams{Vars: vars, OrgID: m.OrgID, GroupID: m.GroupID, HostID: m.HostID, Revision: m.Revision})
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		if _, getErr := s.GetMembership(ctx, m.OrgID, m.GroupID, m.HostID); getErr != nil {
			return Membership{}, getErr
		}
		return Membership{}, ErrRevisionMismatch
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Membership{}, err
	}
	return toMembership(row.GroupID, row.HostID, row.OrgID, row.Vars, row.Revision, row.CreatedAt, row.UpdatedAt, "", "")
}

func (s *SQLStore) GetMembership(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) (Membership, error) {
	row, err := s.q.GetInventoryMembership(ctx, gen.GetInventoryMembershipParams{OrgID: orgID, GroupID: groupID, HostID: hostID})
	if err != nil {
		return Membership{}, mapErr(err)
	}
	return toMembership(row.GroupID, row.HostID, row.OrgID, row.Vars, row.Revision, row.CreatedAt, row.UpdatedAt, "", "")
}

func (s *SQLStore) DeleteMembership(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID, hostID uuid.UUID) error {
	n, err := s.q.DeleteInventoryMembership(ctx, gen.DeleteInventoryMembershipParams{OrgID: orgID, GroupID: groupID, HostID: hostID})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListMemberships(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]Membership, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListInventoryMemberships(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Membership, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		if out[i], err = toMembership(r.GroupID, r.HostID, r.OrgID, r.Vars, r.Revision, r.CreatedAt, r.UpdatedAt, "", ""); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListGroupMembers(ctx context.Context, orgID, groupID uuid.UUID) ([]Membership, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListGroupMembers(ctx, gen.ListGroupMembersParams{OrgID: orgID, GroupID: groupID})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Membership, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		if out[i], err = toMembership(r.GroupID, r.HostID, r.OrgID, r.Vars, r.Revision, r.CreatedAt, r.UpdatedAt, r.HostName, ""); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *SQLStore) ListHostMemberships(ctx context.Context, orgID, hostID uuid.UUID) ([]Membership, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListHostMemberships(ctx, gen.ListHostMembershipsParams{OrgID: orgID, HostID: hostID})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Membership, len(rows))
	for i, r := range rows {
		if out[i], err = toMembership(r.GroupID, r.HostID, r.OrgID, r.Vars, r.Revision, r.CreatedAt, r.UpdatedAt, "", r.GroupName); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

func mapErr(err error) error {
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return ErrNotFound
	}
	return err
}

func mapWriteErr(err error) error {
	switch {
	case db.IsUniqueViolation(err):
		return ErrNameTaken
	case db.IsForeignKeyViolation(err):
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: a referenced group, host or jump host does not exist", ErrInvalidInput)
	default:
		return err
	}
}

func encodeGroup(g Group) ([]byte, []byte, error) {
	sc, err := json.Marshal(g.Schema)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("encode group schema: %w", err)
	}
	vars := g.Vars
	if vars == nil {
		vars = map[string]any{}
	}
	v, err := json.Marshal(vars)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("encode group vars: %w", err)
	}
	return sc, v, nil
}

func toGroup(r gen.InventoryGroup) (Group, error) {
	g := Group{
		ID: r.ID, OrgID: r.OrgID, ParentID: db.UUIDPtr(r.ParentID), Name: r.Name, Description: r.Description,
		BastionID: db.UUIDPtr(r.BastionID), Revision: r.Revision, CreatedAt: r.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: r.UpdatedAt,
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(r.Schema, &g.Schema); err != nil {
		return Group{}, fmt.Errorf("decode schema of group %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	if g.Schema.Fields == nil {
		g.Schema.Fields = []schema.Field{}
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(r.Vars, &g.Vars); err != nil {
		return Group{}, fmt.Errorf("decode vars of group %s: %w", r.ID, err)
	}
	if g.Vars == nil {
		g.Vars = map[string]any{}
	}
	return g, nil
}

func toMembership(groupID, hostID, orgID uuid.UUID, rawVars []byte, revision int64, created, updated time.Time, hostName, groupName string) (Membership, error) {
	m := Membership{GroupID: groupID, HostID: hostID, OrgID: orgID, Revision: revision, CreatedAt: created, UpdatedAt: updated, HostName: hostName, GroupName: groupName}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawVars, &m.Vars); err != nil {
		return Membership{}, fmt.Errorf("decode membership vars: %w", err)
	}
	if m.Vars == nil {
		m.Vars = map[string]any{}
	}
	return m, nil
}
```

sqlc generates one row type per query for the joined lists (`gen.ListGroupMembersRow` with `HostName`, `gen.ListHostMembershipsRow` with `GroupName`) and `gen.InventoryMembership` for the others; `toMembership` takes plain fields so it serves all of them. If sqlc names a parameter field differently from this code, use its name.

```go
func hostvars(t *testing.T, inv render.Inventory, host string) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	hv, ok := inv.Doc["_meta"].(map[string]any)["hostvars"].(map[string]any)[host].(map[string]any)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("no hostvars for %s in %v", host, inv.Doc["_meta"])
	}
	return hv
}

func group(t *testing.T, inv render.Inventory, name string) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	g, ok := inv.Doc[name].(map[string]any)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("no group %s in %v", name, inv.Doc)
	}
	return g
}

func hasWarning(inv render.Inventory, host, substr string) bool {
	for _, w := range inv.Warnings {
		if w.Host == host && strings.Contains(w.Message, substr) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func TestRenderWholeOrganization(t *testing.T) {
	inv, err := render.Render(fixture())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := group(t, inv, "all")["children"]; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"dbserver", "dmz", "edge", "production", "ungrouped"}) {
		t.Errorf("all.children = %v", got)
	}
	if got := group(t, inv, "production"); !reflect.DeepEqual(got["children"], []string{"webserver"}) || !reflect.DeepEqual(got["vars"], map[string]any{"env": "prod"}) {
		t.Errorf("production = %v", got)
	}
	web := group(t, inv, "webserver")
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(web["hosts"], []string{"web-01", "web-02"}) {
		t.Errorf("webserver.hosts = %v", web["hosts"])
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(web["vars"], map[string]any{"php_default": "8.3", "max_upload_mb": 64.0}) {
		t.Errorf("webserver.vars must hold defaults and drop undeclared keys: %v", web["vars"])
	}
	if got := group(t, inv, "dbserver")["hosts"]; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"db-01", "db-02"}) {
		t.Errorf("dbserver.hosts must skip the unmanaged host: %v", got)
	}
	if got := group(t, inv, "ungrouped")["hosts"]; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"lonely"}) {
		t.Errorf("ungrouped = %v", got)
	}
	if _, ok := inv.Doc["dmz"].(map[string]any)["vars"]; ok {
		t.Error("a group without vars must not carry an empty vars key")
	}

	w1 := hostvars(t, inv, "web-01")
	want := map[string]any{
		"ansible_host": "10.0.0.11", "ansible_user": "deploy", "ansible_port": 2222,
		"ansible_ssh_common_args": "-o ProxyJump=ops@bastion-a.example:22",
		"php_default":             "8.4",
		"vhosts":                  []any{map[string]any{"domain": "acme.io", "ssl": true, "basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u", "password": "********"}}},
		"pulsix_facts":            map[string]any{"os": "debian"},
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(w1, want) {
		t.Errorf("web-01 hostvars =\n%#v\nwant\n%#v", w1, want)
	}
	if c := inv.Connections["web-01"]; c.BastionSource != "group:production" || !slices.Equal(c.Bastions, []uuid.UUID{bastionA}) || *c.CredentialID != cred1 {
		t.Errorf("web-01 connection = %+v", c)
	}

	w2 := hostvars(t, inv, "web-02")
	if w2["ansible_host"] != "web-02" || w2["ansible_ssh_common_args"] != nil || inv.Connections["web-02"].BastionSource != render.SourceNone {
		t.Errorf("web-02 (mode none, no address) = %v %+v", w2, inv.Connections["web-02"])
	}
	if _, ok := w2["ansible_port"]; ok {
		t.Error("port 22 must not be rendered")
	}

	if d1 := hostvars(t, inv, "db-01"); d1["ansible_ssh_common_args"] != "-o ProxyJump=jump@zone-jump.example:22" || inv.Connections["db-01"].BastionSource != render.SourceZone {
		t.Errorf("db-01 must use the zone default: %v", d1)
	}

	d2 := hostvars(t, inv, "db-02")
	if d2["ansible_ssh_common_args"] != "-o ProxyJump=ops@bastion-a.example:22,ops@bastion-b.example:2222" || inv.Connections["db-02"].BastionSource != "group:edge" {
		t.Errorf("db-02 must use the chained bastion of edge: %v", d2)
	}
	if d2["db_role"] != "witness" || !hasWarning(inv, "db-02", "db_role is set by groups dbserver and edge; edge wins") {
		t.Errorf("db-02 conflict: db_role=%v warnings=%+v", d2["db_role"], inv.Warnings)
	}

	w3 := hostvars(t, inv, "web-03")
	if w3["ansible_ssh_common_args"] != "-o ProxyJump=jump@zone-jump.example:22" || inv.Connections["web-03"].BastionSource != "group:dmz" {
		t.Errorf("web-03 must take dmz's jump host (tie broken by name): %v", w3)
	}
	if !hasWarning(inv, "web-03", "groups dmz and edge set different jump hosts; dmz wins") {
		t.Errorf("web-03 tie warning missing: %+v", inv.Warnings)
	}

	if hostvars(t, inv, "lonely")["ansible_user"] != "ubuntu" {
		t.Error("the credential's username applies when the host names none")
	}
	if !hasWarning(inv, "switch-01", "not managed") {
		t.Errorf("unmanaged member warning missing: %+v", inv.Warnings)
	}
	if _, ok := inv.Connections["switch-01"]; ok {
		t.Error("an unmanaged host must not be rendered")
	}
}

func TestRenderScopes(t *testing.T) {
	in := fixture()
	in.Scope = render.Scope{GroupID: ptr(gProduction)}
	inv, err := render.Render(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := group(t, inv, "all")["children"]; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"production"}) {
		t.Errorf("all.children = %v", got)
	}
	for _, name := range []string{"dbserver", "edge", "dmz", "ungrouped"} {
		if _, ok := inv.Doc[name]; ok {
			t.Errorf("%s must not be rendered for the production scope", name)
		}
	}
	if len(inv.Connections) != 2 || len(inv.Warnings) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("connections = %v warnings = %v", inv.Connections, inv.Warnings)
	}

	in = fixture()
	in.Scope = render.Scope{HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{hDb02}}
	inv, err = render.Render(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := group(t, inv, "all")["children"]; !reflect.DeepEqual(got, []string{"dbserver", "edge"}) {
		t.Errorf("host scope all.children = %v", got)
	}

	in = fixture()
	in.Scope = render.Scope{GroupID: ptr(gProduction), HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{hWeb02, hDb01}}
	inv, err = render.Render(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(inv.Connections) != 1 || inv.Connections["web-02"].HostID != hWeb02 {
		t.Errorf("group and host scope must intersect: %v", inv.Connections)
	}

	in = fixture()
	in.Scope = render.Scope{GroupID: ptr(uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))}
	if _, err := render.Render(in); !errors.Is(err, render.ErrUnknownGroup) {
		t.Errorf("unknown scope group: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRenderFailsClosedOnBrokenJumpHosts(t *testing.T) {
	in := fixture()
	in.SSH[hWeb02] = sshconf.HostSSH{HostID: hWeb02, Port: 22, BastionMode: sshconf.ModeExplicit, BastionID: ptr(uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))}
	if _, err := render.Render(in); !errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrInvalidInput) {
		t.Errorf("explicit unknown bastion: %v", err)
	}
	in = fixture()
	in.SSH[hWeb02] = sshconf.HostSSH{HostID: hWeb02, Port: 22, BastionMode: sshconf.ModeExplicit}
	if _, err := render.Render(in); err == nil {
		t.Error("explicit mode without a bastion must fail")
	}
}

func TestRenderGolden(t *testing.T) {
	inv, err := render.Render(fixture())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, err := json.MarshalIndent(struct {
		Inventory   map[string]any                `json:"inventory"`
		Connections map[string]render.Connection `json:"connections"`
		Warnings    []render.Warning              `json:"warnings"`
	}{inv.Doc, inv.Connections, inv.Warnings}, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got = append(got, '\n')
	const path = "testdata/inventory.golden.json"
	if *update {
		if err := os.WriteFile(path, got, 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	want, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("read golden file (run with -update once, then review it): %v", err)
	}
	if string(got) != string(want) {
		t.Errorf("inventory differs from %s; if the change is intended, run with -update and review the diff", path)
	}
}
```
