- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the renderer**

`internal/inventory/render/render.go`:

```go
// Package render turns an organization's inventory into an Ansible dynamic
// inventory (spec §6): groups with children, hosts and group vars; hostvars
// merged from each host's memberships; connection variables including the
// resolved jump host chain; host facts under pulsix_facts. It is pure: the
// caller passes every row in, decoded, with secrets already masked or
// revealed. The output is deterministic: every list is sorted.
package render

import (
	"cmp"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"slices"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/grouptree"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/sshconf"
)

// MaskedSecret replaces every secret value in a preview.
const MaskedSecret = "********"

// Where a host's jump host came from. A group source reads "group:<name>".
const (
	SourceHost = "host"
	SourceZone = "zone"
	SourceNone = "none"
)

// ErrUnknownGroup means Scope.GroupID names no group of the input.
var ErrUnknownGroup = errors.New("render: unknown group")

// Group is one inventory group, vars decoded.
type Group struct {
	ID        uuid.UUID
	ParentID  *uuid.UUID
	Name      string
	Schema    schema.Schema
	Vars      map[string]any
	BastionID *uuid.UUID
}

// Membership is one host in one group, vars decoded.
type Membership struct {
	GroupID uuid.UUID
	HostID  uuid.UUID
	Vars    map[string]any
}

// Host is one host.
type Host struct {
	ID      uuid.UUID
	ZoneID  uuid.UUID
	Name    string
	Address string
	Managed bool
	Facts   map[string]any
}

// Scope selects hosts: none set renders every managed host; GroupID selects
// the members of that group and its descendants; HostIDs selects those
// hosts; both together intersect.
type Scope struct {
	GroupID *uuid.UUID
	HostIDs []uuid.UUID
}

// Input is everything a render needs.
type Input struct {
	Groups          []Group
	Memberships     []Membership
	Hosts           []Host
	SSH             map[uuid.UUID]sshconf.HostSSH // by host id; absent means defaults
	Bastions        map[uuid.UUID]sshconf.Bastion
	ZoneBastions    map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID
	CredentialUsers map[uuid.UUID]string // login user per credential id
	Scope           Scope
}

// Connection is how the worker will reach one host; the credential and jump
// hosts are ids, never secrets.
type Connection struct {
	HostID        uuid.UUID   `json:"host_id"`
	CredentialID  *uuid.UUID  `json:"credential_id,omitempty"`
	Bastions      []uuid.UUID `json:"bastions,omitempty"`
	BastionSource string      `json:"bastion_source"`
}

// Warning is something an operator should look at; it never blocks a render.
type Warning struct {
	Host    string `json:"host,omitempty"`
	Group   string `json:"group,omitempty"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

// Inventory is a rendered inventory. Doc is the dynamic-inventory JSON
// document (groups by name, "all", "ungrouped", "_meta.hostvars").
type Inventory struct {
	Doc         map[string]any
	Connections map[string]Connection
	Warnings    []Warning
}

type renderer struct {
	in       Input
	tree     grouptree.Tree
	groups   map[uuid.UUID]Group
	hosts    map[uuid.UUID]Host
	warnings []Warning
}

// Render renders in. It fails, rather than guessing, when a host's jump host
// cannot be resolved: a job must never take a different route than shown.
func Render(in Input) (Inventory, error) {
	r := renderer{in: in, groups: make(map[uuid.UUID]Group, len(in.Groups)), hosts: make(map[uuid.UUID]Host, len(in.Hosts))}
	nodes := make([]grouptree.Node, len(in.Groups))
	for i, g := range in.Groups {
		nodes[i] = grouptree.Node{ID: g.ID, ParentID: g.ParentID, Name: g.Name}
		r.groups[g.ID] = g
	}
	r.tree = grouptree.New(nodes)
	for _, h := range in.Hosts {
		r.hosts[h.ID] = h
	}
	return r.run()
}

func (r *renderer) wholeOrg() bool { return r.in.Scope.GroupID == nil && len(r.in.Scope.HostIDs) == 0 }

func (r *renderer) run() (Inventory, error) {
	selected, err := r.selectHosts()
	if err != nil {
		return Inventory{}, err
	}
	byHost := r.membershipsByHost(selected)
	hostvars := map[string]any{}
	conns := map[string]Connection{}
	var ungrouped []string
	for _, h := range r.sortedHosts(selected) {
		ms := byHost[h.ID]
		if len(ms) == 0 {
			ungrouped = append(ungrouped, h.Name)
		}
		vars, conn, err := r.hostVars(h, ms)
		if err != nil {
			return Inventory{}, err
		}
		hostvars[h.Name] = vars
		conns[h.Name] = conn
	}

	included := r.includedGroups(byHost)
	doc := map[string]any{}
	var roots []string
	for _, g := range r.sortedGroups() {
		if !included[g.ID] {
			continue
		}
		entry := map[string]any{}
		if vars := schema.Resolve(g.Schema.GroupFields(), g.Vars); len(vars) > 0 {
			entry["vars"] = vars
		}
		var children []string
		for _, c := range r.tree.Children(g.ID) {
			if included[c] {
				children = append(children, r.groups[c].Name)
			}
		}
		if len(children) > 0 {
			entry["children"] = children
		}
		var members []string
		for _, h := range r.sortedHosts(selected) {
			for _, m := range byHost[h.ID] {
				if m.GroupID == g.ID {
					members = append(members, h.Name)
				}
			}
		}
		if len(members) > 0 {
			entry["hosts"] = members
		}
		doc[g.Name] = entry
		if len(r.tree.Ancestors(g.ID)) == 0 {
			roots = append(roots, g.Name)
		}
	}
	allChildren := roots
	if len(ungrouped) > 0 {
		allChildren = append(allChildren, "ungrouped")
		doc["ungrouped"] = map[string]any{"hosts": ungrouped}
	}
	all := map[string]any{}
	if len(allChildren) > 0 {
		all["children"] = allChildren
	}
	doc["all"] = all
	doc["_meta"] = map[string]any{"hostvars": hostvars}

	slices.SortFunc(r.warnings, func(a, b Warning) int {
		return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(a.Host, b.Host), cmp.Compare(a.Group, b.Group), cmp.Compare(a.Message, b.Message))
	})
	return Inventory{Doc: doc, Connections: conns, Warnings: r.warnings}, nil
}

func (r *renderer) selectHosts() (map[uuid.UUID]bool, error) {
	sel := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	for _, h := range r.in.Hosts {
		if h.Managed {
			sel[h.ID] = true
		}
	}
	if g := r.in.Scope.GroupID; g != nil {
		if !r.tree.Has(*g) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrUnknownGroup, *g)
		}
		subtree := map[uuid.UUID]bool{*g: true}
		for _, d := range r.tree.Descendants(*g) {
			subtree[d] = true
		}
		inSubtree := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
		for _, m := range r.in.Memberships {
			if subtree[m.GroupID] {
				inSubtree[m.HostID] = true
			}
		}
		for id := range sel {
			if !inSubtree[id] {
				delete(sel, id)
			}
		}
	}
	if len(r.in.Scope.HostIDs) > 0 {
		want := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
		for _, id := range r.in.Scope.HostIDs {
			want[id] = true
		}
		for id := range sel {
			if !want[id] {
				delete(sel, id)
			}
		}
	}
	return sel, nil
}

// membershipsByHost returns each selected host's memberships ordered by group
// depth (shallow first), then group name: the merge order of hostvars.
func (r *renderer) membershipsByHost(sel map[uuid.UUID]bool) map[uuid.UUID][]Membership {
	out := map[uuid.UUID][]Membership{}
	warned := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	for _, m := range r.in.Memberships {
		h, ok := r.hosts[m.HostID]
		if _, known := r.groups[m.GroupID]; !ok || !known {
			continue
		}
		if !h.Managed {
			if r.wholeOrg() && !warned[h.ID] {
				warned[h.ID] = true
				r.warnings = append(r.warnings, Warning{Host: h.Name, Message: "is a member of inventory groups but not managed; skipped"})
			}
			continue
		}
		if sel[m.HostID] {
			out[m.HostID] = append(out[m.HostID], m)
		}
	}
	for _, ms := range out {
		slices.SortFunc(ms, func(a, b Membership) int {
			return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(r.tree.Depth(a.GroupID), r.tree.Depth(b.GroupID)), cmp.Compare(r.groups[a.GroupID].Name, r.groups[b.GroupID].Name))
		})
	}
	return out
}

func (r *renderer) includedGroups(byHost map[uuid.UUID][]Membership) map[uuid.UUID]bool {
	inc := map[uuid.UUID]bool{}
	if r.wholeOrg() {
		for id := range r.groups {
			inc[id] = true
		}
		return inc
	}
	for _, ms := range byHost {
		for _, m := range ms {
			inc[m.GroupID] = true
			for _, a := range r.tree.Ancestors(m.GroupID) {
				inc[a] = true
			}
		}
	}
	return inc
}

func (r *renderer) hostVars(h Host, ms []Membership) (map[string]any, Connection, error) {
	vars := map[string]any{}
	origin := map[string]string{}
	for _, m := range ms {
		g := r.groups[m.GroupID]
		resolved := schema.Resolve(g.Schema.MembershipFields(), m.Vars)
		keys := make([]string, 0, len(resolved))
		for k := range resolved {
			keys = append(keys, k)
		}
		slices.Sort(keys)
		for _, k := range keys {
			v := resolved[k]
			if prev, ok := vars[k]; ok && !reflect.DeepEqual(prev, v) {
				r.warnings = append(r.warnings, Warning{Host: h.Name, Group: g.Name,
					Message: fmt.Sprintf("%s is set by groups %s and %s; %s wins", k, origin[k], g.Name, g.Name)})
			}
			vars[k], origin[k] = v, g.Name
		}
	}

	ssh := r.sshFor(h.ID)
	host := h.Address
	if host == "" {
		host = h.Name
	}
	vars["ansible_host"] = host
	user := ssh.Username
	if user == "" && ssh.CredentialID != nil {
		user = r.in.CredentialUsers[*ssh.CredentialID]
	}
	if user != "" {
		vars["ansible_user"] = user
	}
	if ssh.Port != 0 && ssh.Port != sshconf.DefaultPort {
		vars["ansible_port"] = ssh.Port
	}
	hops, source, err := r.resolveBastion(h, ssh, ms)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, Connection{}, fmt.Errorf("host %s: %w", h.Name, err)
	}
	conn := Connection{HostID: h.ID, CredentialID: ssh.CredentialID, BastionSource: source}
	if len(hops) > 0 {
		vars["ansible_ssh_common_args"] = "-o ProxyJump=" + sshconf.ProxyJump(hops)
		for _, b := range hops {
			conn.Bastions = append(conn.Bastions, b.ID)
		}
	}
	if len(h.Facts) > 0 {
		vars["pulsix_facts"] = h.Facts
	}
	return vars, conn, nil
}

func (r *renderer) sshFor(hostID uuid.UUID) sshconf.HostSSH {
	if s, ok := r.in.SSH[hostID]; ok {
		return s
	}
	return sshconf.HostSSH{HostID: hostID, Port: sshconf.DefaultPort, BastionMode: sshconf.ModeInherit}
}

func (r *renderer) sortedHosts(sel map[uuid.UUID]bool) []Host {
	out := make([]Host, 0, len(sel))
	for id := range sel {
		out = append(out, r.hosts[id])
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b Host) int { return cmp.Compare(a.Name, b.Name) })
	return out
}

func (r *renderer) sortedGroups() []Group {
	out := slices.Clone(r.in.Groups)
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b Group) int { return cmp.Compare(a.Name, b.Name) })
	return out
}
```

`internal/inventory/render/bastion.go`:

```go
package render

import (
	"cmp"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"slices"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/sshconf"
)

type bastionCandidate struct {
	bastion uuid.UUID
	group   Group
	depth   int
}

// resolveBastion applies spec §5's order: the host's own setting, then the
// nearest group jump host (deepest defining group wins, ties by group name),
// then the zone default, then none.
func (r *renderer) resolveBastion(h Host, ssh sshconf.HostSSH, ms []Membership) ([]sshconf.Bastion, string, error) {
	switch ssh.BastionMode {
	case sshconf.ModeNone:
		return nil, SourceNone, nil
	case sshconf.ModeExplicit:
		if ssh.BastionID == nil {
			return nil, "", errors.New("explicit jump host mode without a jump host")
		}
		hops, err := sshconf.Chain(r.in.Bastions, *ssh.BastionID)
		return hops, SourceHost, err
	}
	var cands []bastionCandidate
	for _, m := range ms {
		for _, gid := range append([]uuid.UUID{m.GroupID}, r.tree.Ancestors(m.GroupID)...) {
			if g := r.groups[gid]; g.BastionID != nil {
				cands = append(cands, bastionCandidate{bastion: *g.BastionID, group: g, depth: r.tree.Depth(gid)})
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if len(cands) > 0 {
		slices.SortFunc(cands, func(a, b bastionCandidate) int {
			return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(b.depth, a.depth), cmp.Compare(a.group.Name, b.group.Name))
		})
		win := cands[0]
		for _, c := range cands[1:] {
			if c.depth == win.depth && c.bastion != win.bastion {
				r.warnings = append(r.warnings, Warning{Host: h.Name, Group: c.group.Name,
					Message: fmt.Sprintf("groups %s and %s set different jump hosts; %s wins", win.group.Name, c.group.Name, win.group.Name)})
			}
		}
		hops, err := sshconf.Chain(r.in.Bastions, win.bastion)
		return hops, "group:" + win.group.Name, err
	}
	if b, ok := r.in.ZoneBastions[h.ZoneID]; ok {
		hops, err := sshconf.Chain(r.in.Bastions, b)
		return hops, SourceZone, err
	}
	return nil, SourceNone, nil
}
```

```go
func rendererFixture(t *testing.T) (fixture, *inventory.Renderer, inventory.GroupView) {
	t.Helper()
	f := newFixture()
	g := webGroup(t, f)
	g, err := f.svc.SetVars(ctx, f.org, g.ID, g.Revision, map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": "group-secret"})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := f.svc.Assign(ctx, f.org, g.ID, f.web01, map[string]any{
		"vhosts": []any{map[string]any{"domain": "a.io", "password": "member-secret"}},
	}, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

		hostSSH:  []sshconf.HostSSH{{HostID: f.web01, OrgID: f.org, Port: 22, CredentialID: &key, BastionMode: sshconf.ModeInherit}},
		bastions: []sshconf.Bastion{{ID: f.bastionID, OrgID: f.org, Name: "bastion", Address: "b.example", Port: 22, Username: "ops"}},
		zones:    map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID{},
	}
	creds := fakeCreds{{ID: key, OrgID: f.org, Name: "key", Kind: credentials.KindSSHKey, Username: "ansible"}}
	r := inventory.NewRenderer(f.store, f.store.hosts, ssh, creds, testBox())
	return f, r, g
}

func TestPreviewMasksAndSnapshotReveals(t *testing.T) {
	f, r, g := rendererFixture(t)
	preview, err := r.Preview(ctx, f.org, render.Scope{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	web := preview.Doc["webserver"].(map[string]any)
	if web["vars"].(map[string]any)["db_password"] != render.MaskedSecret {
		t.Fatalf("preview group secret = %v", web["vars"])
	}
	hv := preview.Doc["_meta"].(map[string]any)["hostvars"].(map[string]any)["web-01"].(map[string]any)
	if hv["vhosts"].([]any)[0].(map[string]any)["password"] != render.MaskedSecret || hv["ansible_user"] != "ansible" {
		t.Fatalf("preview hostvars = %v", hv)
	}

	snap, err := r.Snapshot(ctx, f.org, render.Scope{GroupID: &g.ID})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if snap.Doc["webserver"].(map[string]any)["vars"].(map[string]any)["db_password"] != "group-secret" {
		t.Fatal("snapshot must reveal the group secret")
	}
	shv := snap.Doc["_meta"].(map[string]any)["hostvars"].(map[string]any)["web-01"].(map[string]any)
	if shv["vhosts"].([]any)[0].(map[string]any)["password"] != "member-secret" {
		t.Fatal("snapshot must reveal the membership secret")
	}

	// A ciphertext moved into another row does not open.
	other := f.group(t, "other", nil)
	stored := f.store.groups[other.ID]
	stored.Schema = schema.Schema{Fields: []schema.Field{{Name: "db_password", Type: schema.TypeSecret}}}
	stored.Vars = map[string]any{"db_password": f.store.groups[g.ID].Vars["db_password"]}
	f.store.groups[other.ID] = stored
	if _, err := r.Snapshot(ctx, f.org, render.Scope{}); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "open sealed") {
		t.Fatalf("a moved ciphertext must fail to open: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := r.Preview(ctx, f.org, render.Scope{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("preview never opens ciphertext: %v", err)
	}

	if _, err := r.Preview(ctx, f.org, render.Scope{GroupID: ptrUUID(uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7()))}); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown scope group: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestHostPreview(t *testing.T) {
	f, r, _ := rendererFixture(t)
	hp, err := r.HostPreview(ctx, f.org, f.web01)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if hp.Connection == nil || hp.Connection.BastionSource != render.SourceNone || hp.Vars["ansible_host"] != "web-01" {
		t.Fatalf("HostPreview = %+v", hp)
	}
	unmanaged, err := r.HostPreview(ctx, f.org, f.switch01)
	if err != nil || unmanaged.Connection != nil || len(unmanaged.Vars) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("an unmanaged host has no preview: %+v, %v", unmanaged, err)
	}
	if _, err := r.HostPreview(ctx, f.org, uuid.Must(uuid.NewV7())); !errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown host: %v", err)
	}
}

func ptrUUID(id uuid.UUID) *uuid.UUID { return &id }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the renderer service**

`internal/inventory/renderer.go`:

```go
package inventory

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/credentials"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/hosts"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/render"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/sshconf"
)

// HostSource is the slice of hosts.Service rendering needs.
type HostSource interface {
	ListAllHosts(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]hosts.Host, error)
}

// SSHSource is the slice of sshconf.Service rendering needs.
type SSHSource interface {
	ListHostSSH(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]sshconf.HostSSH, error)
	ListBastions(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]sshconf.Bastion, error)
	ZoneBastions(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) (map[uuid.UUID]uuid.UUID, error)
}

// CredentialSource is the slice of credentials.Service rendering needs: the
// default login user of each credential, never its secret.
type CredentialSource interface {
	List(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID) ([]credentials.Credential, error)
}

// Renderer loads an organization's inventory and renders it.
type Renderer struct {
	store Store
	hosts HostSource
	ssh   SSHSource
	creds CredentialSource
	box   Sealer
}

// NewRenderer wires the sources the renderer reads.
func NewRenderer(store Store, hostSource HostSource, ssh SSHSource, creds CredentialSource, box Sealer) *Renderer {
	return &Renderer{store: store, hosts: hostSource, ssh: ssh, creds: creds, box: box}
}

// HostPreview is one host's rendered variables and connection. Connection is
// nil when the host is not rendered (it is not managed).
type HostPreview struct {
	Vars       map[string]any
	Connection *render.Connection
	Warnings   []render.Warning
}

// Preview renders scope with every secret replaced by render.MaskedSecret.
// It never opens a ciphertext.
func (r *Renderer) Preview(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, scope render.Scope) (render.Inventory, error) {
	return r.render(ctx, orgID, scope, false)
}

// Snapshot renders scope with secrets decrypted, for the worker that runs a
// job (v0.2b). Its result must never be logged or returned by the public
// API.
func (r *Renderer) Snapshot(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, scope render.Scope) (render.Inventory, error) {
	return r.render(ctx, orgID, scope, true)
}

// HostPreview renders one host with secrets masked.
func (r *Renderer) HostPreview(ctx context.Context, orgID, hostID uuid.UUID) (HostPreview, error) {
	all, err := r.hosts.ListAllHosts(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return HostPreview{}, err
	}
	var name string
	for _, h := range all {
		if h.ID == hostID {
			name = h.Name
		}
	}
	if name == "" {
		return HostPreview{}, ErrNotFound
	}
	inv, err := r.render(ctx, orgID, render.Scope{HostIDs: []uuid.UUID{hostID}}, false)
	if err != nil {
		return HostPreview{}, err
	}
	out := HostPreview{Vars: map[string]any{}, Warnings: inv.Warnings}
	if hv, ok := inv.Doc["_meta"].(map[string]any)["hostvars"].(map[string]any)[name].(map[string]any); ok {
		out.Vars = hv
	}
	if c, ok := inv.Connections[name]; ok {
		out.Connection = &c
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (r *Renderer) render(ctx context.Context, orgID uuid.UUID, scope render.Scope, reveal bool) (render.Inventory, error) {
	groups, err := r.store.ListGroups(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}
	byID := make(map[uuid.UUID]Group, len(groups))
	for _, g := range groups {
		byID[g.ID] = g
	}
	if scope.GroupID != nil {
		if _, ok := byID[*scope.GroupID]; !ok {
			return render.Inventory{}, ErrNotFound
		}
	}
	memberships, err := r.store.ListMemberships(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}
	hostList, err := r.hosts.ListAllHosts(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}
	sshList, err := r.ssh.ListHostSSH(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}
	bastions, err := r.ssh.ListBastions(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}
	zoneBastions, err := r.ssh.ZoneBastions(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}
	creds, err := r.creds.List(ctx, orgID)
	if err != nil {
		return render.Inventory{}, err
	}

	in := render.Input{
		SSH: map[uuid.UUID]sshconf.HostSSH{}, Bastions: map[uuid.UUID]sshconf.Bastion{},
		ZoneBastions: zoneBastions, CredentialUsers: map[uuid.UUID]string{}, Scope: scope,
	}
	for _, g := range groups {
		vars, err := r.secrets(g.Schema.GroupFields(), g.Vars, GroupAAD(orgID, g.ID), reveal)
		if err != nil {
			return render.Inventory{}, err
		}
		in.Groups = append(in.Groups, render.Group{ID: g.ID, ParentID: g.ParentID, Name: g.Name, Schema: g.Schema, Vars: vars, BastionID: g.BastionID})
	}
	for _, m := range memberships {
		g := byID[m.GroupID]
		vars, err := r.secrets(g.Schema.MembershipFields(), m.Vars, MemberAAD(orgID, m.GroupID, m.HostID), reveal)
		if err != nil {
			return render.Inventory{}, err
		}
		in.Memberships = append(in.Memberships, render.Membership{GroupID: m.GroupID, HostID: m.HostID, Vars: vars})
	}
	for _, h := range hostList {
		in.Hosts = append(in.Hosts, render.Host{ID: h.ID, ZoneID: h.ZoneID, Name: h.Name, Address: h.Address, Managed: h.Managed, Facts: h.Facts})
	}
	for _, s := range sshList {
		in.SSH[s.HostID] = s
	}
	for _, b := range bastions {
		in.Bastions[b.ID] = b
	}
	for _, c := range creds {
		if c.Username != "" {
			in.CredentialUsers[c.ID] = c.Username
		}
	}
	return render.Render(in)
}

// secrets masks or opens every sealed value of a declared secret field.
// Undeclared (orphaned) values are left sealed; the renderer drops them.
func (r *Renderer) secrets(fields []schema.Field, vars map[string]any, aad []byte, reveal bool) (map[string]any, error) {
	return schema.MapSecrets(fields, vars, func(_ string, v any) (any, error) {
		ct, ok := schema.SealedBytes(v)
		if !ok {
			return v, nil
		}
		if !reveal {
			return render.MaskedSecret, nil
		}
		plain, err := r.box.OpenAAD(ct, aad)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("open sealed inventory value: %w", err)
		}
		return string(plain), nil
	})
}
```

### Task 6: API for credentials, jump hosts, zone defaults and host SSH

Transport for Tasks 2 and 3, wired into production (`internal/app`) and the API test harness. Handlers decode, call one service, encode. No response ever carries a private key, password or token.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/api/credential_handlers.go`, `internal/api/sshconf_handlers.go`, `internal/api/uuid_params.go`
- Modify: `internal/api/api.go` (`Deps.Credentials`, `Deps.SSH`, validation, registration)
- Modify: `internal/api/host_handlers.go` (`zoneDTO.BastionID`)
- Modify: `internal/api/api_test.go` (`testDeps`, `baseHealthzDeps`, the missing-dependency cases)
- Modify: `internal/app/app.go`, `internal/api/apitest/apitest.go`
- Modify: `internal/credentials/service.go` (`ExpiryState`)
- Test: `internal/api/credential_handlers_test.go`, `internal/api/sshconf_handlers_test.go`
- Regenerate: `web/src/api/schema.d.ts` (`cd web && npm run api:gen`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `credentials.Service` (`Create`, `Get`, `List`, `Update`, `Delete`, `Usage`, `AuthorizedKey`, `SnippetsFor`), `sshconf.Service` (all methods), `tenancy.PermCredentialsRead/Write`, `tenancy.PermInventoryRead/Write`, `requireOrg`, `withAudit`, `setAuditObject`, `okOutput`.
- Produces:
  - `api.Deps.Credentials *credentials.Service`, `api.Deps.SSH *sshconf.Service`
  - `(*credentials.Service).ExpiryState(c Credential) (expired, soon bool)`
  - `parseOptionalUUID(s *string, field string) (*uuid.UUID, error)` and `parseUUID(s, field string) (uuid.UUID, error)` in `internal/api/uuid_params.go` (400 on malformed ids; used again by Task 7)
  - Routes (all under `/api/v1/orgs/{org}`):

| Method | Path | Permission | Operation id / audit object |
|--------|------|------------|------------------------------|
| GET | `/credentials` | credentials.read | |
| POST | `/credentials` | credentials.write | `createCredential` / `credential` |
| GET | `/credentials/{id}` | credentials.read | |
| PATCH | `/credentials/{id}` | credentials.write | `updateCredential` / `credential` |
| DELETE | `/credentials/{id}` | credentials.write | `deleteCredential` / `credential` |
| GET | `/bastions` | inventory.read | |
| POST | `/bastions` | credentials.write | `createBastion` / `bastion` |
| GET | `/bastions/{id}` | inventory.read | |
| PATCH | `/bastions/{id}` | credentials.write | `updateBastion` / `bastion` |
| DELETE | `/bastions/{id}` | credentials.write | `deleteBastion` / `bastion` |
| PUT | `/zones/{id}/bastion` | inventory.write | `setZoneBastion` / `zone` |
| GET | `/hosts/{id}/ssh` | inventory.read | |
| PUT | `/hosts/{id}/ssh` | inventory.write | `setHostSSH` / `host` |

  - Wire shapes: `credential` = `{id, name, kind, username, public_key?, fingerprint?, key_type?, expires_at?, expired, expires_soon, used_by: {bastions, hosts}, created_at, updated_at}`; detail responses add `snippets?: {authorized_keys_command, cloud_init, terraform}` (SSH keys only). `bastion` = `{id, name, address, port, username, credential_id?, via_bastion_id?}`. `host_ssh` = `{host_id, username, port, credential_id?, bastion_mode, bastion_id?}`. `zone` gains `bastion_id?`.

```go
// ExpiryState reports whether c has expired or expires within ExpiryWarning,
// measured with the service's clock.
func (s *Service) ExpiryState(c Credential) (expired, soon bool) {
	now := s.now()
	return c.Expired(now), c.ExpiresSoon(now)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Shared id parsing**

`internal/api/uuid_params.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
	"github.com/google/uuid"
)

// parseUUID parses a required id from a path or body, answering 400 with
// the field's name when it is malformed.
func parseUUID(s, field string) (uuid.UUID, error) {
	id, err := uuid.Parse(s)
	if err != nil {
		return uuid.Nil, huma.Error400BadRequest("invalid " + field)
	}
	return id, nil
}

// parseOptionalUUID parses an optional id; nil or "" means none.
func parseOptionalUUID(s *string, field string) (*uuid.UUID, error) {
	if s == nil || *s == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	id, err := parseUUID(*s, field)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &id, nil
}

// optionalString renders an optional id for a response.
func optionalString(id *uuid.UUID) *string {
	if id == nil {
		return nil
	}
	s := id.String()
	return &s
}
```

```go
package api_test

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/api/apitest"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type credentialBody struct {
	Credential struct {
		ID          string `json:"id"`
		Name        string `json:"name"`
		Kind        string `json:"kind"`
		PublicKey   string `json:"public_key"`
		Fingerprint string `json:"fingerprint"`
		KeyType     string `json:"key_type"`
		UsedBy      struct {
			Bastions int `json:"bastions"`
			Hosts    int `json:"hosts"`
		} `json:"used_by"`
	} `json:"credential"`
	Snippets *struct {
		AuthorizedKeysCommand string `json:"authorized_keys_command"`
		CloudInit             string `json:"cloud_init"`
		Terraform             string `json:"terraform"`
	} `json:"snippets"`
}

func decodeInto(t *testing.T, resp *http.Response, v any) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(v); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestCredentialLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	s := apitest.New(t)
	owner := s.SeedUser("cred-owner@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	s.SeedOrg("cred-org", owner)
	c := s.LoginAs(owner)
	base := s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/cred-org/credentials"

	resp := postJSON(t, c, base, map[string]any{"name": "acme-deploy", "kind": "ssh_key", "username": "ansible", "generate": true})
	body := mustReadBody(t, resp)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("create: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}
	if strings.Contains(body, "PRIVATE KEY") {
		t.Fatal("a private key reached the response")
	}
	var created credentialBody
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &created); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cred := created.Credential
	if !strings.HasPrefix(cred.PublicKey, "ssh-ed25519 ") || !strings.HasSuffix(cred.PublicKey, " pulsix:cred-org:acme-deploy") || cred.KeyType != "ed25519" {
		t.Fatalf("credential = %+v", cred)
	}
	if created.Snippets == nil || !strings.Contains(created.Snippets.CloudInit, cred.PublicKey) {
		t.Fatalf("snippets = %+v", created.Snippets)
	}

	resp = requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodPatch, base+"/"+cred.ID, map[string]any{"name": "acme-deploy-2", "username": "ansible"})
	var renamed credentialBody
	decodeInto(t, resp, &renamed)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || !strings.HasSuffix(renamed.Credential.PublicKey, " pulsix:cred-org:acme-deploy-2") {
		t.Fatalf("rename: %d %+v", resp.StatusCode, renamed)
	}
	if renamed.Credential.Fingerprint != cred.Fingerprint {
		t.Fatal("a rename must keep the key")
	}

	resp = postJSON(t, c, base, map[string]any{"name": "office-fs-admin", "kind": "ssh_password", "username": "Administrator", "password": "pw-hunter2"})
	if body := mustReadBody(t, resp); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated || strings.Contains(body, "hunter2") {
		t.Fatalf("password credential: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}

	resp = requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, base, nil)
	if body := mustReadBody(t, resp); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || strings.Contains(body, "hunter2") || !strings.Contains(body, "office-fs-admin") {
		t.Fatalf("list: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, s.HTTP.URL+"/api/v1/orgs/cred-org/activity", nil)
	if body := mustReadBody(t, resp); strings.Contains(body, "hunter2") || !strings.Contains(body, "created credential office-fs-admin") {
		t.Fatalf("activity must name the credential and never its secret: %s", body)
	}

	resp = requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodDelete, base+"/"+cred.ID, nil)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("delete: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, base+"/"+cred.ID, nil)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("get deleted: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestCredentialValidationAndConflicts(t *testing.T) {
	s := apitest.New(t)
	owner := s.SeedUser("cred-val@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	s.SeedOrg("cred-val", owner)
	c := s.LoginAs(owner)
	base := s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/cred-val/credentials"

	cases := []struct {
		name string
		body map[string]any
		want int
	}{
		{"key without material", map[string]any{"name": "k", "kind": "ssh_key"}, http.StatusBadRequest},
		{"unknown kind", map[string]any{"name": "k", "kind": "ftp"}, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity},
		{"bad private key", map[string]any{"name": "k", "kind": "git_key", "private_key": "nope"}, http.StatusBadRequest},
		{"token without token", map[string]any{"name": "t", "kind": "git_token"}, http.StatusBadRequest},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			resp := postJSON(t, c, base, tc.body)
			if resp.StatusCode != tc.want {
				t.Fatalf("status %d, want %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, tc.want, mustReadBody(t, resp))
			}
		})
	}
	resp := postJSON(t, c, base, map[string]any{"name": "t", "kind": "git_token", "token": "x"})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, c, base, map[string]any{"name": "t", "kind": "git_token", "token": "y"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate name: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, base+"/not-a-uuid", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("malformed id: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestCredentialPermissions(t *testing.T) {
	s := apitest.New(t)
	owner := s.SeedUser("cred-perm-owner@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	org := s.SeedOrg("cred-perm", owner)
	operator := s.SeedUser("cred-perm-op@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	viewer := s.SeedUser("cred-perm-viewer@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	s.AddMember(org, operator, tenancy.RoleOperator)
	s.AddMember(org, viewer, tenancy.RoleViewer)
	base := s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/cred-perm/credentials"

	op := s.LoginAs(operator)
	if resp := requestJSON(t, op, http.MethodGet, base, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("operator list: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, op, base, map[string]any{"name": "k", "kind": "ssh_key", "generate": true}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("operator create: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, s.LoginAs(viewer), http.MethodGet, base, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer list: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

```go
package api_test

import (
	"net/http"

type bastionBody struct {

		BastionID    *string `json:"bastion_id"`

// sshFixture creates an org with an owner, the default zone's id, a managed

func TestBastionLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	f := newSSHFixture(t, "bastion-org")
	var a, b bastionBody
	resp := postJSON(t, f.c, f.org+"/bastions", map[string]any{"name": "a", "address": "a.example", "username": "ops", "credential_id": f.keyID})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("create a: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &a)
	if a.Bastion.Port != 22 {
		t.Fatalf("port default = %d", a.Bastion.Port)
	}
	resp = postJSON(t, f.c, f.org+"/bastions", map[string]any{"name": "b", "address": "b.example", "port": 2222, "username": "ops", "via_bastion_id": a.Bastion.ID})
	decodeInto(t, resp, &b)
	if b.Bastion.ViaBastionID == nil || *b.Bastion.ViaBastionID != a.Bastion.ID {
		t.Fatalf("b = %+v", b)
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, f.org+"/bastions/"+a.Bastion.ID, map[string]any{"name": "a", "address": "a.example", "username": "ops", "via_bastion_id": b.Bastion.ID})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("cycle: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, f.org+"/bastions/"+a.Bastion.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("delete a bastion another one goes through: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, f.org+"/credentials/"+f.keyID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("delete a credential a bastion uses: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, f.org+"/zones/"+f.zoneID+"/bastion", map[string]any{"bastion_id": b.Bastion.ID})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("zone default: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.org+"/zones", nil)
	var zones struct {
		Zones []struct {
			ID        string  `json:"id"`
			BastionID *string `json:"bastion_id"`
		} `json:"zones"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, resp, &zones)
	found := false
	for _, z := range zones.Zones {
		if z.ID == f.zoneID && z.BastionID != nil && *z.BastionID == b.Bastion.ID {
			found = true
		}
	}
	if !found {
		t.Fatalf("zone default not listed: %+v", zones)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, f.org+"/bastions/"+b.Bastion.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("delete a zone default: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, f.org+"/zones/"+f.zoneID+"/bastion", map[string]any{}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("clear zone default: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, f.org+"/bastions/"+b.Bastion.ID, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("delete b: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, f.org+"/zones/"+f.zoneID+"/bastion", map[string]any{"bastion_id": b.Bastion.ID}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("zone default to a deleted bastion: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestHostSSHSettings(t *testing.T) {
	f := newSSHFixture(t, "hostssh-org")
	url := f.org + "/hosts/" + f.hostID + "/ssh"
	var got hostSSHBody
	resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, url, nil)
	decodeInto(t, resp, &got)
	if got.SSH.Port != 22 || got.SSH.BastionMode != "inherit" || got.SSH.HostID != f.hostID {
		t.Fatalf("defaults = %+v", got)
	}
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		body map[string]any
		want int
	}{
		{"explicit without bastion", map[string]any{"bastion_mode": "explicit"}, http.StatusBadRequest},
		{"git credential", map[string]any{"credential_id": f.tokenID}, http.StatusBadRequest},
		{"unknown mode", map[string]any{"bastion_mode": "maybe"}, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity},
		{"malformed credential id", map[string]any{"credential_id": "nope"}, http.StatusBadRequest},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url, tc.body); resp.StatusCode != tc.want {
				t.Fatalf("status %d, want %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, tc.want, mustReadBody(t, resp))
			}
		})
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url, map[string]any{"username": "deploy", "port": 2222, "credential_id": f.keyID, "bastion_mode": "none"})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("put: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, url, nil), &got)
	if got.SSH.Username != "deploy" || got.SSH.Port != 2222 || got.SSH.BastionMode != "none" || got.SSH.CredentialID == nil {
		t.Fatalf("stored = %+v", got)
	}
	var cred credentialBody
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.org+"/credentials/"+f.keyID, nil), &cred)
	if cred.Credential.UsedBy.Hosts != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("used_by = %+v", cred.Credential.UsedBy)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.org+"/hosts/00000000-0000-7000-8000-000000000000/ssh", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("unknown host: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestSSHPermissionsAndIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	f := newSSHFixture(t, "sshperm-org")

	// A second organization on the same server, with an operator and a viewer.
	rolesOwner := f.s.SeedUser("sshperm-roles-owner@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	rolesOrg := f.s.SeedOrg("sshperm-roles", rolesOwner)
	operator := f.s.SeedUser("sshperm-op@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	viewer := f.s.SeedUser("sshperm-viewer@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	f.s.AddMember(rolesOrg, operator, tenancy.RoleOperator)
	f.s.AddMember(rolesOrg, viewer, tenancy.RoleViewer)
	roles := f.s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/sshperm-roles"

	if resp := postJSON(t, f.s.LoginAs(operator), roles+"/bastions", map[string]any{"name": "a", "address": "a.example", "username": "ops"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("operator creates a bastion: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.s.LoginAs(viewer), http.MethodGet, roles+"/bastions", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("viewer lists bastions: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	// A credential of the second organization cannot be bound to a host of the first.
	var foreign credentialBody
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, f.s.LoginAs(rolesOwner), roles+"/credentials", map[string]any{"name": "foreign", "kind": "ssh_key", "generate": true}), &foreign)
	resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, f.org+"/hosts/"+f.hostID+"/ssh", map[string]any{"credential_id": foreign.Credential.ID})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("cross-org credential: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the credential handlers**

`internal/api/credential_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/credentials"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type credentialUsageDTO struct {
	Bastions int `json:"bastions"`
	Hosts    int `json:"hosts"`
}

type credentialDTO struct {
	ID          string             `json:"id"`
	Name        string             `json:"name"`
	Kind        string             `json:"kind" enum:"ssh_key,ssh_password,git_key,git_token"`
	Username    string             `json:"username"`
	PublicKey   string             `json:"public_key,omitempty"`
	Fingerprint string             `json:"fingerprint,omitempty"`
	KeyType     string             `json:"key_type,omitempty"`
	ExpiresAt   *time.Time         `json:"expires_at,omitempty"`
	Expired     bool               `json:"expired"`
	ExpiresSoon bool               `json:"expires_soon"`
	UsedBy      credentialUsageDTO `json:"used_by"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time          `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time          `json:"updated_at"`
}

type credentialSnippetsDTO struct {
	AuthorizedKeysCommand string `json:"authorized_keys_command"`
	CloudInit             string `json:"cloud_init"`
	Terraform             string `json:"terraform"`
}

func toCredentialDTO(deps Deps, c credentials.Credential, usage credentials.Usage, orgSlug string) credentialDTO {
	expired, soon := deps.Credentials.ExpiryState(c)
	return credentialDTO{
		ID: c.ID.String(), Name: c.Name, Kind: string(c.Kind), Username: c.Username,
		PublicKey: credentials.AuthorizedKey(c, orgSlug), Fingerprint: c.Fingerprint, KeyType: c.KeyType,
		ExpiresAt: c.ExpiresAt, Expired: expired, ExpiresSoon: soon,
		UsedBy:    credentialUsageDTO{Bastions: usage.Bastions, Hosts: usage.Hosts},
		CreatedAt: c.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: c.UpdatedAt,
	}
}

func toSnippetsDTO(c credentials.Credential, orgSlug string) *credentialSnippetsDTO {
	s := credentials.SnippetsFor(c, orgSlug)
	if s == (credentials.Snippets{}) {
		return nil
	}
	return &credentialSnippetsDTO{AuthorizedKeysCommand: s.AuthorizedKeysCommand, CloudInit: s.CloudInit, Terraform: s.Terraform}
}

// credentialMaterial is the secret part of a create or update body. None of
// these fields is ever echoed back.
type credentialMaterial struct {
	Generate   bool   `json:"generate,omitempty" doc:"Key kinds: generate a new ed25519 key"`
	PrivateKey string `json:"private_key,omitempty" maxLength:"16384" doc:"Key kinds: import this private key"`
	Passphrase string `json:"passphrase,omitempty" maxLength:"1024" doc:"Decrypts private_key once; never stored"`
	Password   string `json:"password,omitempty" maxLength:"1024"`
	Token      string `json:"token,omitempty" maxLength:"4096"`
}

func (m credentialMaterial) toMaterial() credentials.Material {
	return credentials.Material{Generate: m.Generate, PrivateKey: m.PrivateKey, Passphrase: m.Passphrase, Password: m.Password, Token: m.Token}
}

type credentialListInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
}

type credentialListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Credentials []credentialDTO `json:"credentials"`
	}
}

type credentialGetInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type credentialDetailOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Credential credentialDTO          `json:"credential"`
		Snippets   *credentialSnippetsDTO `json:"snippets,omitempty"`
	}
}

type credentialCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body struct {
		Name      string     `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"63"`
		Kind      string     `json:"kind" enum:"ssh_key,ssh_password,git_key,git_token"`
		Username  string     `json:"username,omitempty" maxLength:"64"`
		ExpiresAt *time.Time `json:"expires_at,omitempty"`
		credentialMaterial
	}
}

type credentialUpdateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Body struct {
		Name      string     `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"63"`
		Username  string     `json:"username,omitempty" maxLength:"64"`
		ExpiresAt *time.Time `json:"expires_at,omitempty"`
		credentialMaterial
	}
}

// registerCredentialRoutes wires the credential store: reads need
// credentials.read (operator), writes credentials.write (admin). Responses
// carry metadata, the public key with its Pulsix comment and, for SSH keys,
// install snippets; never a private key, password or token.
func registerCredentialRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials", func(ctx context.Context, in *credentialListInput) (*credentialListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Credentials.List(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		usage, err := deps.Credentials.Usage(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &credentialListOutput{}
		out.Body.Credentials = make([]credentialDTO, len(list))
		for i, c := range list {
			out.Body.Credentials[i] = toCredentialDTO(deps, c, usage[c.ID], scope.Org.Slug)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials", func(ctx context.Context, in *credentialCreateInput) (*credentialDetailOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		creator := scope.UserID
		c, err := deps.Credentials.Create(ctx, scope.OrgID, credentials.CreateInput{
			Name: in.Body.Name, Kind: credentials.Kind(in.Body.Kind), Username: in.Body.Username,
			ExpiresAt: in.Body.ExpiresAt, CreatedBy: &creator, Material: in.Body.toMaterial(),
		})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, c.ID.String(), "created credential "+c.Name)
		return credentialDetail(deps, c, credentials.Usage{}, scope.Org.Slug), nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createCredential", "credential"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *credentialGetInput) (*credentialDetailOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "credential id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Credentials.Get(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		usage, err := deps.Credentials.Usage(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		return credentialDetail(deps, c, usage[c.ID], scope.Org.Slug), nil
	})

	huma.Patch(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *credentialUpdateInput) (*credentialDetailOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "credential id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c, err := deps.Credentials.Update(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, credentials.UpdateInput{
			Name: in.Body.Name, Username: in.Body.Username, ExpiresAt: in.Body.ExpiresAt, Material: in.Body.toMaterial(),
		})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		usage, err := deps.Credentials.Usage(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, c.ID.String(), "updated credential "+c.Name)
		return credentialDetail(deps, c, usage[c.ID], scope.Org.Slug), nil
	}, withAudit("updateCredential", "credential"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *credentialGetInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "credential id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Credentials.Delete(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapCredentialsErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted credential")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteCredential", "credential"))
}

func credentialDetail(deps Deps, c credentials.Credential, usage credentials.Usage, orgSlug string) *credentialDetailOutput {
	out := &credentialDetailOutput{}
	out.Body.Credential = toCredentialDTO(deps, c, usage, orgSlug)
	out.Body.Snippets = toSnippetsDTO(c, orgSlug)
	return out
}

func mapCredentialsErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, credentials.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, credentials.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, credentials.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, credentials.ErrInUse):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("the credential is still used by a jump host or a host")
	default:
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped credentials error", "err", err)
		return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
	}
}
```

The embedded `credentialMaterial` flattens into the body exactly as `encoding/json` does. If the huma version in use does not flatten embedded structs into the generated schema, copy the five fields into both bodies instead and keep `toMaterial` as a small function taking them; do not change the wire format.

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the jump host and host SSH handlers**

`internal/api/sshconf_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/sshconf"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type bastionDTO struct {
	ID           string  `json:"id"`
	Name         string  `json:"name"`
	Address      string  `json:"address"`
	Port         int     `json:"port"`
	Username     string  `json:"username"`
	CredentialID *string `json:"credential_id,omitempty"`
	ViaBastionID *string `json:"via_bastion_id,omitempty"`
}

func toBastionDTO(b sshconf.Bastion) bastionDTO {
	return bastionDTO{
		ID: b.ID.String(), Name: b.Name, Address: b.Address, Port: b.Port, Username: b.Username,
		CredentialID: optionalString(b.CredentialID), ViaBastionID: optionalString(b.ViaBastionID),
	}
}

type bastionBodyDTO struct {
	Name         string  `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"63"`
	Address      string  `json:"address" minLength:"1" maxLength:"253"`
	Port         int     `json:"port,omitempty" minimum:"0" maximum:"65535" doc:"0 or omitted means 22"`
	Username     string  `json:"username" minLength:"1" maxLength:"64"`
	CredentialID *string `json:"credential_id,omitempty"`
	ViaBastionID *string `json:"via_bastion_id,omitempty"`
}

func (b bastionBodyDTO) toInput() (sshconf.BastionInput, error) {
	cred, err := parseOptionalUUID(b.CredentialID, "credential_id")
	if err != nil {
		return sshconf.BastionInput{}, err
	}
	via, err := parseOptionalUUID(b.ViaBastionID, "via_bastion_id")
	if err != nil {
		return sshconf.BastionInput{}, err
	}
	return sshconf.BastionInput{Name: b.Name, Address: b.Address, Port: b.Port, Username: b.Username, CredentialID: cred, ViaBastionID: via}, nil
}

type bastionListInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
}

type bastionListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Bastions []bastionDTO `json:"bastions"`
	}
}

type bastionGetInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type bastionOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Bastion bastionDTO `json:"bastion"`
	}
}

type bastionCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body bastionBodyDTO
}

type bastionUpdateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Body bastionBodyDTO
}

type zoneBastionInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Body struct {
		BastionID *string `json:"bastion_id,omitempty" doc:"Omit or send an empty body to clear the zone default"`
	}
}

type hostSSHDTO struct {
	HostID       string  `json:"host_id"`
	Username     string  `json:"username"`
	Port         int     `json:"port"`
	CredentialID *string `json:"credential_id,omitempty"`
	BastionMode  string  `json:"bastion_mode" enum:"inherit,none,explicit"`
	BastionID    *string `json:"bastion_id,omitempty"`
}

func toHostSSHDTO(h sshconf.HostSSH) hostSSHDTO {
	return hostSSHDTO{
		HostID: h.HostID.String(), Username: h.Username, Port: h.Port, CredentialID: optionalString(h.CredentialID),
		BastionMode: string(h.BastionMode), BastionID: optionalString(h.BastionID),
	}
}

type hostSSHGetInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type hostSSHOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		SSH hostSSHDTO `json:"ssh"`
	}
}

type hostSSHPutInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Body struct {
		Username     string  `json:"username,omitempty" maxLength:"64"`
		Port         int     `json:"port,omitempty" minimum:"0" maximum:"65535" doc:"0 or omitted means 22"`
		CredentialID *string `json:"credential_id,omitempty"`
		BastionMode  string  `json:"bastion_mode,omitempty" enum:"inherit,none,explicit" doc:"Omitted means inherit"`
		BastionID    *string `json:"bastion_id,omitempty"`
	}
}

// registerSSHRoutes wires jump hosts (objects written with credentials.write,
// because they bind credentials), zone defaults and host SSH settings
// (inventory.write: assigning an existing jump host or credential reveals
// nothing).
func registerSSHRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions", func(ctx context.Context, in *bastionListInput) (*bastionListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.SSH.ListBastions(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &bastionListOutput{}
		out.Body.Bastions = make([]bastionDTO, len(list))
		for i, b := range list {
			out.Body.Bastions[i] = toBastionDTO(b)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions", func(ctx context.Context, in *bastionCreateInput) (*bastionOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		bi, err := in.Body.toInput()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		b, err := deps.SSH.CreateBastion(ctx, scope.OrgID, bi)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, b.ID.String(), "created jump host "+b.Name)
		out := &bastionOutput{}
		out.Body.Bastion = toBastionDTO(b)
		return out, nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createBastion", "bastion"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *bastionGetInput) (*bastionOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "jump host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		b, err := deps.SSH.GetBastion(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &bastionOutput{}
		out.Body.Bastion = toBastionDTO(b)
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Patch(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *bastionUpdateInput) (*bastionOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "jump host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		bi, err := in.Body.toInput()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		b, err := deps.SSH.UpdateBastion(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, bi)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, b.ID.String(), "updated jump host "+b.Name)
		out := &bastionOutput{}
		out.Body.Bastion = toBastionDTO(b)
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("updateBastion", "bastion"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *bastionGetInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermCredentialsWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "jump host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.SSH.DeleteBastion(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted jump host")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteBastion", "bastion"))

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/zones/{id}/bastion", func(ctx context.Context, in *zoneBastionInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		zoneID, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "zone id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		bastionID, err := parseOptionalUUID(in.Body.BastionID, "bastion_id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.SSH.SetZoneBastion(ctx, scope.OrgID, zoneID, bastionID); err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		summary := "cleared the zone's default jump host"
		if bastionID != nil {
			summary = "set the zone's default jump host"
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, zoneID.String(), summary)
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("setZoneBastion", "zone"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/ssh", func(ctx context.Context, in *hostSSHGetInput) (*hostSSHOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		h, err := deps.SSH.HostSSH(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		out := &hostSSHOutput{}
		out.Body.SSH = toHostSSHDTO(h)
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/ssh", func(ctx context.Context, in *hostSSHPutInput) (*hostSSHOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		cred, err := parseOptionalUUID(in.Body.CredentialID, "credential_id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		bastion, err := parseOptionalUUID(in.Body.BastionID, "bastion_id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		h, err := deps.SSH.SetHostSSH(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, sshconf.HostSSHInput{
			Username: in.Body.Username, Port: in.Body.Port, CredentialID: cred,
			BastionMode: sshconf.BastionMode(in.Body.BastionMode), BastionID: bastion,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapSSHErr(ctx, err)
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "updated SSH settings")
		out := &hostSSHOutput{}
		out.Body.SSH = toHostSSHDTO(h)
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("setHostSSH", "host"))
}

func mapSSHErr(ctx context.Context, err error) error {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, sshconf.ErrInUse):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("the jump host is still used by a zone, group, host or another jump host")
	default:
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped sshconf error", "err", err)
		return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
	}
}
```

| Method | Path | Permission | Operation id / audit object | Concurrency |
|--------|------|------------|------------------------------|-------------|
| GET | `/inventory/groups` | inventory.read | | |
| POST | `/inventory/groups` | inventory.write | `createInventoryGroup` / `inventory_group` | ETag out |
| GET | `/inventory/groups/{id}` | inventory.read | | ETag out |
| PATCH | `/inventory/groups/{id}` | inventory.write | `updateInventoryGroup` / `inventory_group` | If-Match required |
| DELETE | `/inventory/groups/{id}` | inventory.write | `deleteInventoryGroup` / `inventory_group` | |
| PUT | `/inventory/groups/{id}/schema` | inventory.write | `setInventoryGroupSchema` / `inventory_group` | If-Match required |
| PUT | `/inventory/groups/{id}/vars` | inventory.write | `setInventoryGroupVars` / `inventory_group` | If-Match required |
| GET | `/inventory/groups/{id}/members` | inventory.read | | |
| PUT | `/inventory/groups/{id}/members/{host}` | inventory.write | `assignInventoryHost` / `inventory_membership` | If-Match optional, ETag out |
| DELETE | `/inventory/groups/{id}/members/{host}` | inventory.write | `unassignInventoryHost` / `inventory_membership` | |
| GET | `/inventory/preview?group=` | inventory.read | | |
| GET | `/hosts/{id}/inventory/groups` | inventory.read | | |
| GET | `/hosts/{id}/inventory/preview` | inventory.read | | |

  - Wire shapes: `group_summary` = `{id, name, description, parent_id?, bastion_id?, members, group_fields, host_fields, complete, revision}`; `group` = `{id, name, description, parent_id?, bastion_id?, revision, schema, vars, problems[], orphaned[], created_at, updated_at}`; `member` = `{group_id, group_name, host_id, host_name, vars, complete, problems[], orphaned[], revision}`; `problem` = `{path, message}`; preview = `{inventory, connections, warnings}`; host preview = `{vars, connection?, warnings}`. A 422 carries one `errors[]` entry per problem with `location` = `body.schema.<path>` or `body.vars.<path>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: If-Match parsing with tests**

`internal/api/ifmatch_internal_test.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"testing"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
)

func statusOf(err error) int {
	var se huma.StatusError
	if errors.As(err, &se) {
		return se.GetStatus()
	}
	return 0
}

func TestIfMatch(t *testing.T) {
	if got := revisionETag(7); got != `"7"` {
		t.Fatalf("revisionETag = %s", got)
	}
	cases := []struct {
		header string
		rev    int64
		status int // 0 = ok
	}{
		{`"7"`, 7, 0},
		{`W/"7"`, 7, 0},
		{` "12" `, 12, 0},
		{`7`, 0, http.StatusPreconditionFailed},
		{`"abc"`, 0, http.StatusPreconditionFailed},
		{`"0"`, 0, http.StatusPreconditionFailed},
		{`"`, 0, http.StatusPreconditionFailed},
		{`*`, 0, http.StatusPreconditionFailed},
		{``, 0, http.StatusPreconditionRequired},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(c.header)
		if statusOf(err) != c.status || (c.status == 0 && rev != c.rev) {
			t.Errorf("requireIfMatch(%q) = %d, %v", c.header, rev, err)
		}
	}
	if rev, err := optionalIfMatch(""); rev != nil || err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("optionalIfMatch(\"\") = %v, %v", rev, err)
	}
	if rev, err := optionalIfMatch(`"3"`); err != nil || *rev != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("optionalIfMatch = %v, %v", rev, err)
	}
	if _, err := optionalIfMatch("junk"); statusOf(err) != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("optionalIfMatch(junk) = %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/api/ifmatch.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"
)

// revisionETag renders an object's revision as a strong ETag: "7".
func revisionETag(rev int64) string { return `"` + strconv.FormatInt(rev, 10) + `"` }

// requireIfMatch reads the revision a write is based on. Missing is 428,
// anything that is not an ETag this API issued is 412 (spec §15).
func requireIfMatch(header string) (int64, error) {
	rev, ok, err := parseIfMatch(header)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	if !ok {
		return 0, huma.Error428PreconditionRequired("send If-Match with the ETag of the version you edited")
	}
	return rev, nil
}

// optionalIfMatch reads If-Match when the client sent one.
func optionalIfMatch(header string) (*int64, error) {
	rev, ok, err := parseIfMatch(header)
	if err != nil || !ok {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &rev, nil
}

func parseIfMatch(header string) (int64, bool, error) {
	h := strings.TrimSpace(header)
	if h == "" {
		return 0, false, nil
	}
	v := strings.TrimPrefix(h, "W/")
	bad := huma.Error412PreconditionFailed("If-Match must be the quoted ETag of the version you edited")
	if len(v) < 2 || v[0] != '"' || v[len(v)-1] != '"' {
		return 0, false, bad
	}
	n, err := strconv.ParseInt(v[1:len(v)-1], 10, 64)
	if err != nil || n < 1 {
		return 0, false, bad
	}
	return n, true, nil
}
```

Run: `go test ./internal/api/ -run TestIfMatch` → PASS.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/google/uuid"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/api/apitest"

)

func requestWithHeaders(t *testing.T, c *http.Client, method, url string, body any, headers map[string]string) *http.Response {
	t.Helper()
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	if body != nil {
		if err := json.NewEncoder(&buf).Encode(body); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	req, err := http.NewRequest(method, url, &buf)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if body != nil {
		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	}
	for k, v := range headers {
		req.Header.Set(k, v)
	}
	resp, err := c.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = resp.Body.Close() })
	return resp
}

type groupBody struct {
	Group struct {
		ID       string           `json:"id"`
		Name     string           `json:"name"`
		ParentID *string          `json:"parent_id"`
		Revision int64            `json:"revision"`
		Vars     map[string]any   `json:"vars"`
		Problems []map[string]any `json:"problems"`
		Orphaned []string         `json:"orphaned"`
	} `json:"group"`
	IncompleteMembers int `json:"incomplete_members"`
}

type problemBody struct {
	Errors []struct {
		Location string `json:"location"`
		Message  string `json:"message"`
	} `json:"errors"`
}

func hasLocation(t *testing.T, resp *http.Response, location string) {
	t.Helper()
	var p problemBody
	decodeInto(t, resp, &p)
	for _, e := range p.Errors {
		if e.Location == location {
			return
		}
	}
	t.Fatalf("no error at %q in %+v", location, p.Errors)
}

func TestInventoryGroupsAndConcurrency(t *testing.T) {
	f := newInvFixture(t, "inv-groups")
	prod := f.group(t, "production", nil)
	web := f.group(t, "webserver", &prod.Group.ID)
	url := f.base + "/inventory/groups/" + web.Group.ID

	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, url, map[string]any{"name": "web"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionRequired {
		t.Fatalf("missing If-Match: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, url, map[string]any{"name": "web"}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"5"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("stale If-Match: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, url, map[string]any{"name": "web"}, map[string]string{"If-Match": "abc"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("malformed If-Match: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, url, map[string]any{"name": "web", "parent_id": prod.Group.ID}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"2"` {
		t.Fatalf("rename: %d %q %s", resp.StatusCode, resp.Header.Get("ETag"), mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	prodURL := f.base + "/inventory/groups/" + prod.Group.ID
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, prodURL, map[string]any{"name": "production", "parent_id": web.Group.ID}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("cycle: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, prodURL, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("delete a parent: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.c, f.base+"/inventory/groups", map[string]any{"name": "all"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("reserved name: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := postJSON(t, f.c, f.base+"/inventory/groups", map[string]any{"name": "production"}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("duplicate: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	var list struct {
		Groups []struct {
			Name    string `json:"name"`
			Members int    `json:"members"`
		} `json:"groups"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.base+"/inventory/groups", nil), &list)
	if len(list.Groups) != 2 || list.Groups[0].Name != "production" || list.Groups[1].Name != "web" {
		t.Fatalf("list = %+v", list)
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, url, nil)
	if resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"2"` {
		t.Fatalf("GET ETag = %q", resp.Header.Get("ETag"))
	}
}

func TestInventorySchemaAndValues(t *testing.T) {
	f := newInvFixture(t, "inv-schema")
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	url := f.base + "/inventory/groups/" + g.Group.ID
	v1 := map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`}

	bad := map[string]any{"schema": map[string]any{"fields": []any{map[string]any{"name": "ansible_x", "type": "string"}}}}
	resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/schema", bad, v1)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("invalid schema: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	hasLocation(t, resp, "body.schema.fields[0].name")
	unknownKey := map[string]any{"schema": map[string]any{"fields": []any{map[string]any{"name": "x", "type": "string", "colour": "red"}}}}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/schema", unknownKey, v1); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("unknown schema attribute: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/schema", webSchemaBody, v1)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"2"` {
		t.Fatalf("schema: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	var withSchema groupBody
	decodeInto(t, resp, &withSchema)
	if len(withSchema.Group.Problems) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the required group field env must show as a problem: %+v", withSchema.Group.Problems)
	}

	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/vars", map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": "grp-hunter2"}}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"2"`})
	body := mustReadBody(t, resp)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || strings.Contains(body, "hunter2") || !strings.Contains(body, `"$secret":"set"`) {
		t.Fatalf("vars: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}
	keep := map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"env": "staging", "db_password": map[string]any{"$secret": "keep", "from": "db_password"}}}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/vars", keep, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"3"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("keep: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	resp = requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/vars", map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"env": "prod", "extra": 1}}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"4"`})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("unknown value: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	hasLocation(t, resp, "body.vars.extra")
}

func TestInventoryMembers(t *testing.T) {
	f := newInvFixture(t, "inv-members")
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	url := f.base + "/inventory/groups/" + g.Group.ID
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/schema", webSchemaBody, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	managed, unmanaged := f.host(t, "web-01", true), f.host(t, "switch-01", false)

	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/members/"+unmanaged, map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{}}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
		t.Fatalf("unmanaged host: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/members/"+managed, map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{map[string]any{"domain": "Bad Domain"}}}})
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("invalid member values: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	hasLocation(t, resp, "body.vars.vhosts[0].domain")

	vars := map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"php_default": "8.4", "vhosts": []any{
		map[string]any{"domain": "acme.io", "basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u", "password": "mem-hunter2"}},
	}}}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/members/"+managed, vars)
	body := mustReadBody(t, resp)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || resp.Header.Get("ETag") != `"1"` || strings.Contains(body, "hunter2") {
		t.Fatalf("assign: %d %q %s", resp.StatusCode, resp.Header.Get("ETag"), body)
	}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, url+"/members/"+managed, vars, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"9"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusPreconditionFailed {
		t.Fatalf("stale member revision: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	var members struct {
		Members []struct {
			HostName string `json:"host_name"`
			Complete bool   `json:"complete"`
		} `json:"members"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, url+"/members", nil), &members)
	if len(members.Members) != 1 || members.Members[0].HostName != "web-01" || !members.Members[0].Complete {
		t.Fatalf("members = %+v", members)
	}
	var hostGroups struct {
		Groups []struct {
			GroupName string `json:"group_name"`
		} `json:"groups"`
	}
	decodeInto(t, requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.base+"/hosts/"+managed+"/inventory/groups", nil), &hostGroups)
	if len(hostGroups.Groups) != 1 || hostGroups.Groups[0].GroupName != "webserver" {
		t.Fatalf("host groups = %+v", hostGroups)
	}
	resp = requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.base+"/hosts/"+managed+"/inventory/preview", nil)
	body = mustReadBody(t, resp)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || !strings.Contains(body, `"password":"********"`) || !strings.Contains(body, `"ansible_host":"web-01"`) || strings.Contains(body, "hunter2") {
		t.Fatalf("host preview: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, url+"/members/"+managed, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("unassign: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodDelete, url+"/members/"+managed, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("second unassign: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}

func TestInventoryPermissionsAndIsolation(t *testing.T) {
	f := newInvFixture(t, "inv-perm")
	g := f.group(t, "webserver", nil)
	viewer := f.s.SeedUser("inv-perm-viewer@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	f.s.AddMember(f.orgID, viewer, tenancy.RoleViewer)
	view := f.s.LoginAs(viewer)
	url := f.base + "/inventory/groups/" + g.Group.ID
	if resp := requestJSON(t, view, http.MethodGet, url, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("viewer reads a group: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, view, http.MethodGet, f.base+"/inventory/preview", nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("viewer reads the preview: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, view, http.MethodPut, url+"/vars", map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{}}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("viewer writes values: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	// The group is invisible from another organization, not merely forbidden.
	otherOwner := f.s.SeedUser("inv-perm-other@example.test", "correct horse battery")
	f.s.SeedOrg("inv-perm-other", otherOwner)
	other := f.s.HTTP.URL + "/api/v1/orgs/inv-perm-other/inventory/groups/" + g.Group.ID
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.s.LoginAs(otherOwner), http.MethodGet, other, nil); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("a group of another organization: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
}
```

`internal/api/inventory_preview_test.go` pins the rendered inventory through the whole stack:

```go
package api_test

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"flag"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

var updateGolden = flag.Bool("update", false, "rewrite testdata/*.golden.json")

func TestInventoryPreviewGolden(t *testing.T) {
	f := newInvFixture(t, "inv-golden")

	var cred credentialBody
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, f.c, f.base+"/credentials", map[string]any{"name": "deploy", "kind": "ssh_key", "username": "ansible", "generate": true}), &cred)
	var jump bastionBody
	decodeInto(t, postJSON(t, f.c, f.base+"/bastions", map[string]any{"name": "jump", "address": "bastion.fra.example", "port": 2222, "username": "ops", "credential_id": cred.Credential.ID}), &jump)

	prod := f.group(t, "production", nil)
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPatch, f.base+"/inventory/groups/"+prod.Group.ID,
		map[string]any{"name": "production", "bastion_id": jump.Bastion.ID}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	web := f.group(t, "webserver", &prod.Group.ID)
	webURL := f.base + "/inventory/groups/" + web.Group.ID
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, webURL+"/schema", webSchemaBody, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"1"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestWithHeaders(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, webURL+"/vars", map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"env": "prod", "db_password": "golden-hunter2"}}, map[string]string{"If-Match": `"2"`}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	host := decodeHost(t, postJSON(t, f.c, f.base+"/hosts", map[string]any{"name": "web-01", "address": "10.0.0.11", "zone_id": f.zone, "managed": true})).ID
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, f.base+"/hosts/"+host+"/ssh", map[string]any{"port": 2200, "credential_id": cred.Credential.ID}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, webURL+"/members/"+host, map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{
		map[string]any{"domain": "acme.io", "basic_auth": map[string]any{"user": "u", "password": "golden-member"}},
		map[string]any{"domain": "shop.acme.io"},
	}}}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal(mustReadBody(t, resp))
	}
	// An invalid assignment is rejected and changes nothing.
	if resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodPut, webURL+"/members/"+host, map[string]any{"vars": map[string]any{"vhosts": []any{}}}); resp.StatusCode != http.StatusUnprocessableEntity {
		t.Fatalf("empty repeater must be rejected: %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}

	resp := requestJSON(t, f.c, http.MethodGet, f.base+"/inventory/preview?group="+prod.Group.ID, nil)
	body := mustReadBody(t, resp)
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || strings.Contains(body, "golden-hunter2") || strings.Contains(body, "golden-member") {
		t.Fatalf("preview: %d %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
	}
	var preview struct {
		Inventory json.RawMessage `json:"inventory"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(body), &preview); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var pretty bytes.Buffer
	if err := json.Indent(&pretty, preview.Inventory, "", "  "); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	pretty.WriteByte('\n')
	const path = "testdata/inventory_preview.golden.json"
	if *updateGolden {
		if err := os.WriteFile(path, pretty.Bytes(), 0o644); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	want, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("read golden file (run with -update once, then review it): %v", err)
	}
	if pretty.String() != string(want) {
		t.Errorf("preview differs from %s:\n%s", path, pretty.String())
	}
}
```

`json.Indent` keeps the key order the server sent; encoding/json writes map keys sorted, so the document is stable. The golden file must contain: `production` with `children: ["webserver"]`, `webserver` with `hosts: ["web-01"]` and `vars` `{db_password: "********", env: "prod", php_default: "8.3"}`, and `_meta.hostvars.web-01` with `ansible_host: "10.0.0.11"`, `ansible_port: 2200`, `ansible_user: "ansible"`, `ansible_ssh_common_args: "-o ProxyJump=ops@bastion.fra.example:2222"`, and both vhosts with `basic_auth.password` masked on the first. Review the generated file against this list before committing it.
