`internal/api/inventory_handlers.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/schema"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type problemDTO struct {
	Path    string `json:"path"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func toProblemDTOs(ps []schema.Problem) []problemDTO {
	out := make([]problemDTO, len(ps))
	for i, p := range ps {
		out[i] = problemDTO{Path: p.Path, Message: p.Message}
	}
	return out
}

func nonNilStrings(s []string) []string {
	if s == nil {
		return []string{}
	}
	return s
}

type groupSummaryDTO struct {
	ID          string  `json:"id"`
	Name        string  `json:"name"`
	Description string  `json:"description"`
	ParentID    *string `json:"parent_id,omitempty"`
	BastionID   *string `json:"bastion_id,omitempty"`
	Members     int     `json:"members"`
	GroupFields int     `json:"group_fields"`
	HostFields  int     `json:"host_fields"`
	Complete    bool    `json:"complete" doc:"The group's own values satisfy its schema"`
	Revision    int64   `json:"revision"`
}

// groupDTO's schema is schema.Schema itself: spec §6 defines that JSON as the
// contract the SPA renders forms from, so the API does not re-shape it.
type groupDTO struct {
	ID          string         `json:"id"`
	Name        string         `json:"name"`
	Description string         `json:"description"`
	ParentID    *string        `json:"parent_id,omitempty"`
	BastionID   *string        `json:"bastion_id,omitempty"`
	Revision    int64          `json:"revision"`
	Schema      schema.Schema  `json:"schema"`
	Vars        map[string]any `json:"vars" doc:"Group-scoped values; secrets read as {\"$secret\": \"set\"}"`
	Problems    []problemDTO   `json:"problems"`
	Orphaned    []string       `json:"orphaned" doc:"Stored keys the schema no longer declares; the next save drops them"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
	UpdatedAt   time.Time      `json:"updated_at"`
}

func toGroupDTO(v inventory.GroupView) groupDTO {
	return groupDTO{
		ID: v.ID.String(), Name: v.Name, Description: v.Description, ParentID: optionalString(v.ParentID),
		BastionID: optionalString(v.BastionID), Revision: v.Revision, Schema: v.Schema, Vars: v.Vars,
		Problems: toProblemDTOs(v.Problems), Orphaned: nonNilStrings(v.Orphaned), CreatedAt: v.CreatedAt, UpdatedAt: v.UpdatedAt,
	}
}

func toGroupSummaryDTO(s inventory.GroupSummary) groupSummaryDTO {
	return groupSummaryDTO{
		ID: s.ID.String(), Name: s.Name, Description: s.Description, ParentID: optionalString(s.ParentID),
		BastionID: optionalString(s.BastionID), Members: s.Members, GroupFields: len(s.Schema.GroupFields()),
		HostFields: len(s.Schema.HostFields()), Complete: len(s.Problems) == 0, Revision: s.Revision,
	}
}

type groupListInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
}

type groupListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Groups []groupSummaryDTO `json:"groups"`
	}
}

type groupOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Group groupDTO `json:"group"`
	}
}

func newGroupOutput(v inventory.GroupView) *groupOutput {
	out := &groupOutput{ETag: revisionETag(v.Revision)}
	out.Body.Group = toGroupDTO(v)
	return out
}

type groupCreateInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	Body struct {
		Name        string  `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"63"`
		Description string  `json:"description,omitempty" maxLength:"1024"`
		ParentID    *string `json:"parent_id,omitempty"`
	}
}

type groupGetInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type groupUpdateInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The group's ETag; required"`
	Body    struct {
		Name        string  `json:"name" minLength:"1" maxLength:"63"`
		Description string  `json:"description,omitempty" maxLength:"1024"`
		ParentID    *string `json:"parent_id,omitempty" doc:"Omit to make the group a root"`
		BastionID   *string `json:"bastion_id,omitempty" doc:"Omit to clear the group's jump host"`
	}
}

type groupSchemaInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The group's ETag; required"`
	Body    struct {
		Schema schema.Schema `json:"schema"`
	}
}

type groupSchemaOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Group             groupDTO `json:"group"`
		IncompleteMembers int      `json:"incomplete_members"`
	}
}

type groupVarsInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The group's ETag; required"`
	Body    struct {
		Vars map[string]any `json:"vars" doc:"Group-scoped values. Secrets: a string sets a new value, {\"$secret\": \"keep\", \"from\": \"<path>\"} keeps the stored one"`
	}
}

// registerInventoryRoutes wires inventory groups. Every write to a group
// needs If-Match with the group's ETag (spec §15).
func registerInventoryRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupListInput) (*groupListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Inventory.ListGroups(ctx, scope.OrgID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		out := &groupListOutput{}
		out.Body.Groups = make([]groupSummaryDTO, len(list))
		for i, g := range list {
			out.Body.Groups[i] = toGroupSummaryDTO(g)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Post(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupCreateInput) (*groupOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		parent, err := parseOptionalUUID(in.Body.ParentID, "parent_id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		g, err := deps.Inventory.CreateGroup(ctx, scope.OrgID, inventory.GroupInput{Name: in.Body.Name, Description: in.Body.Description, ParentID: parent})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, g.ID.String(), "created inventory group "+g.Name)
		return newGroupOutput(g), nil
	}, func(o *huma.Operation) { o.DefaultStatus = http.StatusCreated }, withAudit("createInventoryGroup", "inventory_group"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupGetInput) (*groupOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		g, err := deps.Inventory.GetGroup(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		return newGroupOutput(g), nil
	})

	huma.Patch(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupUpdateInput) (*groupOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		parent, err := parseOptionalUUID(in.Body.ParentID, "parent_id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		bastion, err := parseOptionalUUID(in.Body.BastionID, "bastion_id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		g, err := deps.Inventory.UpdateGroup(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rev, inventory.GroupPatch{
			Name: in.Body.Name, Description: in.Body.Description, ParentID: parent, BastionID: bastion,
		})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, g.ID.String(), "updated inventory group "+g.Name)
		return newGroupOutput(g), nil
	}, withAudit("updateInventoryGroup", "inventory_group"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupGetInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Inventory.DeleteGroup(ctx, scope.OrgID, id); err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, id.String(), "deleted inventory group")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("deleteInventoryGroup", "inventory_group"))

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/schema", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupSchemaInput) (*groupSchemaOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		g, incomplete, err := deps.Inventory.SetSchema(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rev, in.Body.Schema)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "body.schema")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, g.ID.String(), "changed the variable schema of inventory group "+g.Name)
		out := &groupSchemaOutput{ETag: revisionETag(g.Revision)}
		out.Body.Group, out.Body.IncompleteMembers = toGroupDTO(g), incomplete
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("setInventoryGroupSchema", "inventory_group"))

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/vars", func(ctx context.Context, in *groupVarsInput) (*groupOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := requireIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		g, err := deps.Inventory.SetVars(ctx, scope.OrgID, id, rev, in.Body.Vars)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "body.vars")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, g.ID.String(), "changed the values of inventory group "+g.Name)
		return newGroupOutput(g), nil
	}, withAudit("setInventoryGroupVars", "inventory_group"))
}

// mapInventoryErr converts inventory errors to HTTP. A *schema.ValidationError
// becomes 422 with one detail per problem, located under location (for
// example body.vars.vhosts[0].domain), so the SPA can show each message at
// its field.
func mapInventoryErr(ctx context.Context, err error, location string) error {
	var ve *schema.ValidationError
	if errors.As(err, &ve) {
		details := make([]error, len(ve.Problems))
		for i, p := range ve.Problems {
			loc := location
			if p.Path != "" {
				if loc != "" {
					loc += "."
				}
				loc += p.Path
			}
			details[i] = &huma.ErrorDetail{Location: loc, Message: p.Message}
		}
		return huma.Error422UnprocessableEntity("validation failed", details...)
	}
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrInvalidInput):
		return huma.Error400BadRequest(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotFound):
		return huma.Error404NotFound("not found")
	case errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNameTaken):
		return huma.Error409Conflict(err.Error())
	case errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrHasChildren):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("the group still has child groups; move or delete them first")
	case errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrNotManaged):
		return huma.Error409Conflict("only managed hosts can join inventory groups")
	case errors.Is(err, inventory.ErrRevisionMismatch):
		return huma.Error412PreconditionFailed("this changed since you loaded it; reload and apply your change again")
	default:
		slog.ErrorContext(ctx, "unmapped inventory error", "err", err)
		return huma.Error500InternalServerError("internal error")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the member, host and preview handlers**

`internal/api/inventory_member_handlers.go` (its types carry an `inventoryMember` prefix because `memberDTO`, `memberListInput` and `memberListOutput` already exist for organization members):

```go
package api

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/danielgtaylor/huma/v2"

	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/inventory/render"
	"github.com/noixdev/pulsix/internal/tenancy"
)

type inventoryMemberDTO struct {
	GroupID   string         `json:"group_id"`
	GroupName string         `json:"group_name"`
	HostID    string         `json:"host_id"`
	HostName  string         `json:"host_name"`
	Vars      map[string]any `json:"vars"`
	Complete  bool           `json:"complete"`
	Problems  []problemDTO   `json:"problems"`
	Orphaned  []string       `json:"orphaned"`
	Revision  int64          `json:"revision"`
}

func toInventoryMemberDTO(m inventory.MemberView) inventoryMemberDTO {
	return inventoryMemberDTO{
		GroupID: m.GroupID.String(), GroupName: m.GroupName, HostID: m.HostID.String(), HostName: m.HostName,
		Vars: m.Vars, Complete: m.Complete, Problems: toProblemDTOs(m.Problems), Orphaned: nonNilStrings(m.Orphaned), Revision: m.Revision,
	}
}

type inventoryMemberListInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type inventoryMemberListOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Members []inventoryMemberDTO `json:"members"`
	}
}

type inventoryMemberPutInput struct {
	Org     string `path:"org"`
	ID      string `path:"id"`
	Host    string `path:"host"`
	IfMatch string `header:"If-Match" doc:"The membership's ETag; optional"`
	Body    struct {
		Vars map[string]any `json:"vars" doc:"Host-scoped values and overrides. Secrets as for group values"`
	}
}

type inventoryMemberDeleteInput struct {
	Org  string `path:"org"`
	ID   string `path:"id"`
	Host string `path:"host"`
}

type inventoryMemberOutput struct {
	ETag string `header:"ETag"`
	Body struct {
		Member inventoryMemberDTO `json:"member"`
	}
}

type previewInput struct {
	Org   string `path:"org"`
	Group string `query:"group" doc:"Render only the members of this group and its descendants"`
}

type previewOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Inventory   map[string]any               `json:"inventory"`
		Connections map[string]render.Connection `json:"connections"`
		Warnings    []render.Warning             `json:"warnings"`
	}
}

type hostInventoryInput struct {
	Org string `path:"org"`
	ID  string `path:"id"`
}

type hostGroupsOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Groups []inventoryMemberDTO `json:"groups"`
	}
}

type hostPreviewOutput struct {
	Body struct {
		Vars       map[string]any     `json:"vars"`
		Connection *render.Connection `json:"connection,omitempty"`
		Warnings   []render.Warning   `json:"warnings"`
	}
}

func nonNilWarnings(w []render.Warning) []render.Warning {
	if w == nil {
		return []render.Warning{}
	}
	return w
}

// registerInventoryMemberRoutes wires memberships, a host's groups and the
// masked inventory previews.
func registerInventoryMemberRoutes(hapi huma.API, deps Deps) {
	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/members", func(ctx context.Context, in *inventoryMemberListInput) (*inventoryMemberListOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Inventory.Members(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		out := &inventoryMemberListOutput{}
		out.Body.Members = make([]inventoryMemberDTO, len(list))
		for i, m := range list {
			out.Body.Members[i] = toInventoryMemberDTO(m)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Put(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/members/{host}", func(ctx context.Context, in *inventoryMemberPutInput) (*inventoryMemberOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		groupID, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		hostID, err := parseUUID(in.Host, "host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		rev, err := optionalIfMatch(in.IfMatch)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		m, err := deps.Inventory.Assign(ctx, scope.OrgID, groupID, hostID, in.Body.Vars, rev)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "body.vars")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, groupID.String(), "assigned host "+m.HostName+" to inventory group "+m.GroupName)
		out := &inventoryMemberOutput{ETag: revisionETag(m.Revision)}
		out.Body.Member = toInventoryMemberDTO(m)
		return out, nil
	}, withAudit("assignInventoryHost", "inventory_membership"))

	huma.Delete(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/members/{host}", func(ctx context.Context, in *inventoryMemberDeleteInput) (*okOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryWrite)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		groupID, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "group id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		hostID, err := parseUUID(in.Host, "host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := deps.Inventory.Unassign(ctx, scope.OrgID, groupID, hostID); err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		setAuditObject(ctx, groupID.String(), "removed host "+hostID.String()+" from the inventory group")
		return &okOutput{}, nil
	}, withAudit("unassignInventoryHost", "inventory_membership"))

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/preview", func(ctx context.Context, in *previewInput) (*previewOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		gid, err := parseOptionalUUID(&in.Group, "group")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		inv, err := deps.Renderer.Preview(ctx, scope.OrgID, render.Scope{GroupID: gid})
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		out := &previewOutput{}
		out.Body.Inventory, out.Body.Connections, out.Body.Warnings = inv.Doc, inv.Connections, nonNilWarnings(inv.Warnings)
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/inventory/groups", func(ctx context.Context, in *hostInventoryInput) (*hostGroupsOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		list, err := deps.Inventory.HostGroups(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		out := &hostGroupsOutput{}
		out.Body.Groups = make([]inventoryMemberDTO, len(list))
		for i, m := range list {
			out.Body.Groups[i] = toInventoryMemberDTO(m)
		}
		return out, nil
	})

	huma.Get(hapi, "/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/inventory/preview", func(ctx context.Context, in *hostInventoryInput) (*hostPreviewOutput, error) {
		scope, err := requireOrg(ctx, deps, in.Org, tenancy.PermInventoryRead)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		id, err := parseUUID(in.ID, "host id")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		p, err := deps.Renderer.HostPreview(ctx, scope.OrgID, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, mapInventoryErr(ctx, err, "")
		}
		out := &hostPreviewOutput{}
		out.Body.Vars, out.Body.Connection, out.Body.Warnings = p.Vars, p.Connection, nonNilWarnings(p.Warnings)
		return out, nil
	})
}
```

A render error that is not `inventory.ErrNotFound` (a broken jump host chain) falls through `mapInventoryErr` to 500 and is logged: the data behind it was validated on write, so it means corruption, not a user mistake.

### Task 8: Schema-driven form renderer (SPA)

One recursive, controlled form component renders any group schema: the group values form, the host assignment form, and the builder's live preview all use it. It speaks the same paths as the server, so a 422 lands at the exact field. Pure helpers keep secrets masked end to end: a stored secret becomes a keep marker that remembers where it came from.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/features/inventory/schema/types.ts`
- Create: `web/src/features/inventory/schema/paths.ts`, test `paths.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/features/inventory/schema/secrets.ts`, test `secrets.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/features/inventory/schema/SchemaForm.tsx`, test `SchemaForm.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ApiError` (`web/src/api/client.ts`), `Button`, `Input`, `Label` (`web/src/ui`).
- Produces (all under `web/src/features/inventory/schema/`):
  - `types.ts`: `FieldType`, `FieldScope`, `SchemaField`, `VarSchema`, `Vars`, `Problem` (mirrors spec §6 JSON exactly)
  - `paths.ts`: `childPath(prefix, name)`, `indexPath(prefix, i)`, `problemAt(problems, path)`, `scopeOf(field)`, `groupFields(schema)`, `hostFields(schema)`, `membershipFields(schema)`, `problemsFromError(error, location)`
  - `secrets.ts`: `KeepMarker`, `isSecretSet(v)`, `isKeepMarker(v)`, `keepMarker(from)`, `toKeepMarkers(fields, vars, prefix?)`, `declaredOnly(fields, vars)`
  - `SchemaForm.tsx`: `SchemaForm({fields, value, onChange, problems?, disabled?, idPrefix?, pathPrefix?})`, `ScalarInput`, `inputId(idPrefix, path)`

- [ ] **Step 1: Types**

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/types.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The variable schema of an inventory group, exactly as spec §6 and
 * internal/inventory/schema define it. The API sends and accepts this JSON
 * unchanged; every form in the inventory UI renders from it.
 */
export type FieldType = 'string' | 'int' | 'float' | 'bool' | 'enum' | 'secret' | 'list' | 'repeater' | 'object';
export type FieldScope = 'group' | 'host';

export interface SchemaField {
  name: string;
  label?: string;
  description?: string;
  type: FieldType;
  scope?: FieldScope;
  required?: boolean;
  overridable?: boolean;
  default?: unknown;
  options?: string[];
  min?: number;
  max?: number;
  pattern?: string;
  min_items?: number;
  max_items?: number;
  item?: SchemaField;
  fields?: SchemaField[];
}

export interface VarSchema {
  fields: SchemaField[];
}

/** A vars document: group values or a membership's values. */
export type Vars = Record<string, unknown>;

/** One validation problem; path uses the server's format: vhosts[1].domain. */
export interface Problem {
  path: string;
  message: string;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError } from '../../../api/client.ts';
import { childPath, groupFields, hostFields, indexPath, membershipFields, problemAt, problemsFromError } from './paths.ts';
import type { VarSchema } from './types.ts';

const schema: VarSchema = {
  fields: [
    { name: 'env', type: 'string', required: true },
    { name: 'php_default', type: 'enum', options: ['8.3', '8.4'], default: '8.3', overridable: true, required: true },
    { name: 'vhosts', type: 'repeater', scope: 'host', fields: [{ name: 'domain', type: 'string' }] },
  ],
};

describe('paths', () => {
  it('builds paths in the server format', () => {
    expect(childPath('', 'vhosts')).toBe('vhosts');
    expect(childPath(indexPath('vhosts', 2), 'domain')).toBe('vhosts[2].domain');
    expect(problemAt([{ path: 'a', message: 'x' }], 'a')).toBe('x');
    expect(problemAt(undefined, 'a')).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('splits fields by scope like the server', () => {
    expect(groupFields(schema).map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['env', 'php_default']);
    expect(hostFields(schema).map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['vhosts']);
    const m = membershipFields(schema);
    expect(m.map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['php_default', 'vhosts']);
    expect(m[0].required).toBe(false);
    expect(m[0].default).toBeUndefined();
    expect(schema.fields[1].default).toBe('8.3');
  });

  it('maps 422 details under a location to problems', () => {
    const err = new ApiError(422, {
      errors: [
        { location: 'body.vars.vhosts[0].domain', message: 'is required' },
        { location: 'body.vars', message: 'root problem' },
        { location: 'body.schema.fields[0].name', message: 'elsewhere' },
        { message: 'no location' },
      ],
    });
    expect(problemsFromError(err, 'body.vars')).toEqual([
      { path: 'vhosts[0].domain', message: 'is required' },
      { path: '', message: 'root problem' },
    ]);
    expect(problemsFromError(new ApiError(400, {}), 'body.vars')).toEqual([]);
    expect(problemsFromError(new Error('x'), 'body.vars')).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { declaredOnly, isKeepMarker, isSecretSet, keepMarker, toKeepMarkers } from './secrets.ts';
import type { SchemaField } from './types.ts';

const fields: SchemaField[] = [
  { name: 'db_password', type: 'secret' },
  { name: 'auth', type: 'object', fields: [{ name: 'password', type: 'secret' }] },
  { name: 'vhosts', type: 'repeater', fields: [{ name: 'password', type: 'secret' }, { name: 'domain', type: 'string' }] },
];
const SET = { $secret: 'set' };

describe('secret helpers', () => {
  it('recognises masks and keep markers', () => {
    expect(isSecretSet(SET)).toBe(true);
    expect(isSecretSet('x')).toBe(false);
    expect(isKeepMarker(keepMarker('a[0].b'))).toBe(true);
    expect(isKeepMarker({ $secret: 'keep' })).toBe(false);
    expect(isKeepMarker(null)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('turns every mask into a keep marker that remembers its path', () => {
    const vars = {
      db_password: SET,
      auth: { password: SET },
      vhosts: [{ domain: 'a.io', password: SET }, { domain: 'b.io' }, 'odd'],
      untouched: SET,
    };
    expect(toKeepMarkers(fields, vars)).toEqual({
      db_password: keepMarker('db_password'),
      auth: { password: keepMarker('auth.password') },
      vhosts: [{ domain: 'a.io', password: keepMarker('vhosts[0].password') }, { domain: 'b.io' }, 'odd'],
      untouched: SET,
    });
    expect(vars.db_password).toBe(SET);
  });

  it('drops keys the schema no longer declares', () => {
    expect(declaredOnly(fields, { db_password: 'x', removed: 1 })).toEqual({ db_password: 'x' });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the helpers**

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/paths.ts`:

```ts
import { ApiError } from '../../../api/client.ts';
import type { FieldScope, Problem, SchemaField, VarSchema } from './types.ts';

export function childPath(prefix: string, name: string): string {
  return prefix ? `${prefix}.${name}` : name;
}

export function indexPath(prefix: string, index: number): string {
  return `${prefix}[${index}]`;
}

export function problemAt(problems: readonly Problem[] | undefined, path: string): string | undefined {
  return problems?.find((p) => p.path === path)?.message;
}

export function scopeOf(field: SchemaField): FieldScope {
  return field.scope ?? 'group';
}

export function groupFields(schema: VarSchema): SchemaField[] {
  return schema.fields.filter((f) => scopeOf(f) === 'group');
}

export function hostFields(schema: VarSchema): SchemaField[] {
  return schema.fields.filter((f) => scopeOf(f) === 'host');
}

/** What a membership may carry: host fields plus optional overrides of
 * overridable group fields (no schema default: an absent override means
 * "use the group value"), in schema order, exactly like the server. */
export function membershipFields(schema: VarSchema): SchemaField[] {
  return schema.fields.flatMap((f) => {
    if (scopeOf(f) === 'host') return [f];
    if (f.overridable) return [{ ...f, required: false, default: undefined }];
    return [];
  });
}

interface ErrorDetail {
  location?: string;
  message?: string;
}

/** problemsFromError turns a 422's details under location (for example
 * "body.vars") into problems with paths relative to that location. */
export function problemsFromError(error: unknown, location: string): Problem[] {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError) || error.status !== 422) return [];
  const body = error.body as { errors?: ErrorDetail[] } | undefined;
  const prefix = `${location}.`;
  return (body?.errors ?? []).flatMap((e) => {
    if (!e.location || !e.message) return [];
    if (e.location === location) return [{ path: '', message: e.message }];
    if (e.location.startsWith(prefix)) return [{ path: e.location.slice(prefix.length), message: e.message }];
    return [];
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/secrets.ts`:

```ts
import { childPath, indexPath } from './paths.ts';
import type { SchemaField, Vars } from './types.ts';

/** A write that keeps the stored secret found at `from` when the form was
 * loaded (internal/inventory/schema.KeepFrom). */
export interface KeepMarker {
  $secret: 'keep';
  from: string;
}

function isRecord(v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
}

/** isSecretSet recognises the mask a read returns for a stored secret. */
export function isSecretSet(v: unknown): boolean {
  return isRecord(v) && v.$secret === 'set';
}

export function isKeepMarker(v: unknown): v is KeepMarker {
  return isRecord(v) && v.$secret === 'keep' && typeof v.from === 'string';
}

export function keepMarker(from: string): KeepMarker {
  return { $secret: 'keep', from };
}

/** toKeepMarkers prepares loaded values for editing: every masked secret
 * of a declared secret field becomes a keep marker naming its original
 * path, so saving keeps it and a moved repeater entry keeps its own. */
export function toKeepMarkers(fields: readonly SchemaField[], vars: Vars, prefix = ''): Vars {
  const out: Vars = { ...vars };
  for (const f of fields) {
    const v = vars[f.name];
    const path = childPath(prefix, f.name);
    if (f.type === 'secret' && isSecretSet(v)) {
      out[f.name] = keepMarker(path);
    } else if (f.type === 'object' && isRecord(v)) {
      out[f.name] = toKeepMarkers(f.fields ?? [], v, path);
    } else if (f.type === 'repeater' && Array.isArray(v)) {
      out[f.name] = v.map((item, i) => (isRecord(item) ? toKeepMarkers(f.fields ?? [], item, indexPath(path, i)) : item));
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** declaredOnly keeps only the top-level keys fields declare, so saving a
 * form drops values of removed fields (spec §6: never silently, but on the
 * next save). */
export function declaredOnly(fields: readonly SchemaField[], vars: Vars): Vars {
  const names = new Set(fields.map((f) => f.name));
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(vars).filter(([k]) => names.has(k)));
}

export { isRecord };
```

```tsx
      <SchemaForm fields={fields} value={value} onChange={setValue} problems={problems} disabled={disabled} />
      <pre data-testid="value">{JSON.stringify(value)}</pre>
    </>
  );
}

const current = () => JSON.parse(screen.getByTestId('value').textContent ?? '{}') as Vars;

describe('SchemaForm', () => {

  it('adds, edits, reorders and removes repeater entries', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<Harness fields={[vhosts]} />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add Virtual hosts' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('domain'), 'a.io');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add Virtual hosts' }));
    await user.type(screen.getAllByLabelText('domain')[1], 'b.io');
    expect(current()).toEqual({ vhosts: [{ domain: 'a.io' }, { domain: 'b.io' }] });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Move Virtual hosts 2 up' }));
    expect(current()).toEqual({ vhosts: [{ domain: 'b.io' }, { domain: 'a.io' }] });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove Virtual hosts 1' }));
    expect(current()).toEqual({ vhosts: [{ domain: 'a.io' }] });
  });

  it('shows a stored secret as stored, replaces it, and can keep it again', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<Harness fields={[{ name: 'db_password', type: 'secret' }]} initial={{ db_password: keepMarker('db_password') }} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/stored/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Replace' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('db_password'), 'n3w');
    expect(current()).toEqual({ db_password: 'n3w' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep stored value' }));
    expect(current()).toEqual({ db_password: keepMarker('db_password') });
  });

  it('keeps a moved entry’s stored secret with that entry', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(
      <Harness
        fields={[vhosts]}
        initial={{ vhosts: [{ domain: 'a.io', password: keepMarker('vhosts[0].password') }, { domain: 'b.io' }] }}
      />,
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Move Virtual hosts 1 down' }));
    const second = screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Virtual hosts 2' });
    await user.click(within(second).getByRole('button', { name: 'Replace' }));
    await user.click(within(second).getByRole('button', { name: 'Keep stored value' }));
    expect(current()).toEqual({ vhosts: [{ domain: 'b.io' }, { domain: 'a.io', password: keepMarker('vhosts[0].password') }] });
  });

  it('edits list items and nested objects', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(
      <Harness
        fields={[
          { name: 'aliases', type: 'list', item: { name: '', type: 'string' } },
          { name: 'auth', type: 'object', fields: [{ name: 'user', type: 'string' }] },
        ]}
      />,
    );
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add aliases' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('aliases 1'), 'www.a.io');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('user'), 'admin');
    expect(current()).toEqual({ aliases: ['www.a.io'], auth: { user: 'admin' } });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove aliases 1' }));
    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('user'));
    expect(current()).toEqual({});
  });

  it('shows each server problem at its field', () => {
    render(
      <Harness
        fields={[vhosts]}
        initial={{ vhosts: [{ domain: 'x' }, {}] }}
        problems={[{ path: 'vhosts[1].domain', message: 'is required' }, { path: 'vhosts', message: 'needs more' }]}
      />,
    );
    const second = screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Virtual hosts 2' });
    expect(within(second).getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('is required');
    expect(within(second).getByLabelText('domain')).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByText('needs more')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders read-only when disabled', () => {
    render(<Harness fields={[{ name: 'env', type: 'string' }, vhosts]} initial={{ env: 'prod' }} disabled />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('env')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add Virtual hosts' })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it('says so when a schema has no fields', () => {
    render(<Harness fields={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No fields.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the form**

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/SchemaForm.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../../ui/label.tsx';
import { childPath, indexPath, problemAt } from './paths.ts';
import { isKeepMarker, isRecord, type KeepMarker } from './secrets.ts';
import type { FieldType, Problem, SchemaField, Vars } from './types.ts';

export interface SchemaFormProps {
  fields: readonly SchemaField[];
  value: Vars;
  onChange: (next: Vars) => void;
  /** Problems with server paths (vhosts[0].domain), shown at their field. */
  problems?: readonly Problem[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  /** Distinguishes input ids when two forms share a page. */
  idPrefix?: string;
  /** Path of this (nested) form inside the document. */
  pathPrefix?: string;
}

const SELECT =
  'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground focus-visible:border-primary focus-visible:outline-none disabled:opacity-50';

/**
 * SchemaForm renders a form for any variable schema (spec §6) as a
 * controlled component over a plain value tree. It never validates: the
 * server does, and its problems come back through `problems` at the same
 * paths this component uses for its inputs.
 */
export function SchemaForm({ fields, value, onChange, problems, disabled, idPrefix = 'sf', pathPrefix = '' }: SchemaFormProps) {
  if (fields.length === 0) return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No fields.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {fields.map((f) => (
        <FieldInput
          key={f.name}
          field={f}
          path={childPath(pathPrefix, f.name)}
          value={value[f.name]}
          onChange={(v) => onChange(withKey(value, f.name, v))}
          problems={problems}
          disabled={disabled}
          idPrefix={idPrefix}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function withKey(obj: Vars, key: string, v: unknown): Vars {
  const next = { ...obj };
  if (v === undefined) delete next[key];
  else next[key] = v;
  return next;
}

export function inputId(idPrefix: string, path: string): string {
  return `${idPrefix}-${path.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '-')}`;
}

interface FieldInputProps {
  field: SchemaField;
  path: string;
  value: unknown;
  onChange: (v: unknown) => void;
  problems?: readonly Problem[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  idPrefix: string;
}

function FieldInput(props: FieldInputProps) {
  const { field, path, value, onChange, problems, disabled, idPrefix } = props;
  const id = inputId(idPrefix, path);
  const error = problemAt(problems, path);
  const label = field.label || field.name;

  if (field.type === 'repeater') return <RepeaterInput {...props} label={label} error={error} />;
  if (field.type === 'list') return <ListInput {...props} id={id} label={label} error={error} />;
  if (field.type === 'object') {
    return (
      <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-md border border-border p-3" disabled={disabled}>
        <legend className="px-1 text-[11px] font-semibold">{label}</legend>
        <SchemaForm
          fields={field.fields ?? []}
          value={isRecord(value) ? value : {}}
          onChange={(v) => onChange(Object.keys(v).length === 0 ? undefined : v)}
          problems={problems}
          disabled={disabled}
          idPrefix={idPrefix}
          pathPrefix={path}
        />
        <FieldFooter field={field} error={error} />
      </fieldset>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        <Label htmlFor={id}>{label}</Label>
        {field.required && (
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-[11px] text-destructive">
            *
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      {field.type === 'secret' ? (
        <SecretInput id={id} value={value} onChange={onChange} disabled={disabled} invalid={!!error} />
      ) : (
        <ScalarInput id={id} field={field} value={value} onChange={onChange} disabled={disabled} invalid={!!error} />
      )}
      <FieldFooter field={field} error={error} />
    </div>
  );
}

function FieldFooter({ field, error }: { field: SchemaField; error?: string }) {
  return (
    <>
      {field.description && <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{field.description}</span>}
      {error && (
        <span role="alert" className="text-[11px] text-destructive">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

export interface ScalarInputProps {
  id: string;
  field: SchemaField;
  value: unknown;
  onChange: (v: unknown) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
  invalid?: boolean;
  ariaLabel?: string;
}

/** ScalarInput edits one string, int, float, bool or enum value. An
 * emptied input reports undefined so the key is dropped. */
interface CommonProps {
  id: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
  'aria-invalid'?: boolean;
  'aria-label'?: string;
}

export function ScalarInput({ id, field, value, onChange, disabled, invalid, ariaLabel }: ScalarInputProps) {
  const common: CommonProps = { id, disabled, 'aria-invalid': invalid ? true : undefined, 'aria-label': ariaLabel };
  switch (field.type) {
    case 'enum':
      return (
        <select
          {...common}
          className={SELECT}
          value={typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value === '' ? undefined : e.target.value)}
        >
          <option value="">{field.default !== undefined ? `default (${String(field.default)})` : '—'}</option>
          {(field.options ?? []).map((o) => (
            <option key={o} value={o}>
              {o}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      );
    case 'int':
    case 'float':
      return <NumberInput common={common} field={field} value={value} onChange={onChange} />;
    case 'bool':
      return (
        <input
          {...common}
          type="checkbox"
          className="h-4 w-4 accent-[var(--px-accent)]"
          checked={value === undefined ? field.default === true : value === true}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)}
        />
      );
    default:
      return (
        <Input
          {...common}
          type="text"
          className="font-mono"
          value={typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''}
          placeholder={typeof field.default === 'string' ? field.default : undefined}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value === '' ? undefined : e.target.value)}
        />
      );
  }
}

function parseNumber(raw: string): number | undefined {
  if (raw.trim() === '') return undefined;
  const n = Number(raw);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : undefined;
}

function formatNumber(v: unknown): string {
  return typeof v === 'number' ? String(v) : '';
}

/**
 * NumberInput is a text input with its own text state: "0." or "-" are
 * legitimate steps while typing a number, and a controlled <input
 * type="number"> would wipe them (the browser reports them as ""). The text
 * follows the value only when the value changes to something the text does
 * not already say (a reload, a reset), the "adjust state when a prop
 * changes" pattern from the React documentation.
 */
function NumberInput({ common, field, value, onChange }: { common: CommonProps; field: SchemaField; value: unknown; onChange: (v: unknown) => void }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(() => formatNumber(value));
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState(value);
  if (!Object.is(value, seen)) {
    setSeen(value);
    if (parseNumber(text) !== value) setText(formatNumber(value));
  }
  return (
    <Input
      {...common}
      type="text"
      inputMode={field.type === 'int' ? 'numeric' : 'decimal'}
      className="font-mono"
      value={text}
      placeholder={field.default !== undefined ? String(field.default) : undefined}
      onChange={(e) => {
        setText(e.target.value);
        onChange(parseNumber(e.target.value));
      }}
    />
  );
}

function SecretInput({ id, value, onChange, disabled, invalid }: { id: string; value: unknown; onChange: (v: unknown) => void; disabled?: boolean; invalid?: boolean }) {
  // The marker this input started with, so "Keep stored value" can undo a
  // replacement. The entry's component instance moves with the entry
  // (RepeaterInput keys entries stably), so this stays the right marker.
  const [original] = useState<KeepMarker | null>(() => (isKeepMarker(value) ? value : null));
  if (isKeepMarker(value)) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span id={id} className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          •••••••• stored
        </span>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" disabled={disabled} onClick={() => onChange(undefined)}>
          Replace
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <Input
        id={id}
        type="password"
        autoComplete="new-password"
        className="font-mono"
        disabled={disabled}
        aria-invalid={invalid ? true : undefined}
        value={typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value === '' ? undefined : e.target.value)}
      />
      {original && (
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" disabled={disabled} onClick={() => onChange(original)}>
          Keep stored value
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function emptyScalar(type: FieldType): unknown {
  if (type === 'bool') return false;
  if (type === 'int' || type === 'float') return null;
  return '';
}

function ListInput({ field, path, value, onChange, problems, disabled, idPrefix, id, label, error }: FieldInputProps & { id: string; label: string; error?: string }) {
  const items = Array.isArray(value) ? (value as unknown[]) : [];
  const item: SchemaField = field.item ?? { name: '', type: 'string' };
  const set = (next: unknown[]) => onChange(next.length === 0 && !field.required ? undefined : next);
  const full = field.max_items !== undefined && items.length >= field.max_items;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1" role="group" aria-labelledby={id}>
      <span id={id} className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">
        {label}
      </span>
      {items.map((it, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ScalarInput
            id={inputId(idPrefix, indexPath(path, i))}
            field={item}
            value={it}
            ariaLabel={`${label} ${i + 1}`}
            disabled={disabled}
            invalid={!!problemAt(problems, indexPath(path, i))}
            onChange={(v) => set(items.map((x, j) => (j === i ? (v ?? emptyScalar(item.type)) : x)))}
          />
          <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Remove ${label} ${i + 1}`} disabled={disabled} onClick={() => set(items.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}>
            <Trash2 size={14} />
          </Button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="self-start" disabled={disabled || full} onClick={() => set([...items, emptyScalar(item.type)])}>
        <Plus size={14} /> Add {label}
      </Button>
      <FieldFooter field={field} error={error} />
    </div>
  );
}

function newKey(): string {
  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
}

function RepeaterInput({ field, path, value, onChange, problems, disabled, idPrefix, label, error }: FieldInputProps & { label: string; error?: string }) {
  const items = Array.isArray(value) ? (value as unknown[]) : [];
  // Stable keys move with their entries, so an entry's inputs (and a
  // secret's remembered marker) follow it when entries are reordered. A
  // length change from outside (a reload) falls back to index keys.
  const [keys, setKeys] = useState<string[]>(() => items.map(newKey));
  const stable = keys.length === items.length ? keys : items.map((_, i) => `i${i}`);
  const set = (next: unknown[], nextKeys: string[]) => {
    setKeys(nextKeys);
    onChange(next.length === 0 && !field.required ? undefined : next);
  };
  const move = (from: number, to: number) => {
    const next = [...items];
    const k = [...stable];
    next.splice(to, 0, next.splice(from, 1)[0]);
    k.splice(to, 0, k.splice(from, 1)[0]);
    set(next, k);
  };
  const full = field.max_items !== undefined && items.length >= field.max_items;
  const headingId = inputId(idPrefix, path);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2" role="group" aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span id={headingId} className="text-[11px] font-semibold">
          {label}
          {field.required && <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-destructive"> *</span>}
        </span>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
          {items.length} {items.length === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries'}
        </span>
      </div>
      {items.map((it, i) => {
        const entryId = `${headingId}-${stable[i]}`;
        return (
          <div key={stable[i]} role="group" aria-labelledby={entryId} className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span id={entryId} className="text-[11px] font-semibold">
                {label} {i + 1}
              </span>
              <div className="flex gap-1">
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Move ${label} ${i + 1} up`} disabled={disabled || i === 0} onClick={() => move(i, i - 1)}>
                  <ArrowUp size={14} />
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Move ${label} ${i + 1} down`} disabled={disabled || i === items.length - 1} onClick={() => move(i, i + 1)}>
                  <ArrowDown size={14} />
                </Button>
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="ghost"
                  aria-label={`Remove ${label} ${i + 1}`}
                  disabled={disabled}
                  onClick={() => set(items.filter((_, j) => j !== i), stable.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}
                >
                  <Trash2 size={14} />
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <SchemaForm
              fields={field.fields ?? []}
              value={isRecord(it) ? it : {}}
              onChange={(v) => set(items.map((x, j) => (j === i ? v : x)), stable)}
              problems={problems}
              disabled={disabled}
              idPrefix={idPrefix}
              pathPrefix={indexPath(path, i)}
            />
          </div>
        );
      })}
      <Button type="button" variant="default" className="self-start" disabled={disabled || full} onClick={() => set([...items, {}], [...stable, newKey()])}>
        <Plus size={14} /> Add {label}
      </Button>
      <FieldFooter field={field} error={error} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

The group's accessible name for an entry comes from its heading span ("Virtual hosts 2"), which the tests use to scope queries to one entry. `getByLabelText('domain')` works because each entry's label is associated with its own input id.

Check `SchemaForm.test.tsx`'s first expectation against the code: typing `8` into `workers` reports `8` (a number); typing `0.5` into `ratio` passes through `0`, `0` and `0.5` while the text keeps showing what was typed; the bool checkbox starts unchecked (no default) and reports `true`.

### Task 9: Variable schema builder (SPA)

The editor from the SchemaBuilder mockup: field list, field editor, and the generated assignment form as a live preview. It is a pure, controlled component over a `VarSchema`; saving is the page's job (Task 10). Sub-schemas of repeater and object fields reuse the same builder recursively.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/features/inventory/schema/fieldEditor.ts`, test `fieldEditor.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/features/inventory/schema/SchemaBuilder.tsx`, test `SchemaBuilder.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SchemaForm`, `ScalarInput`, `membershipFields`, `scopeOf`, `problemAt` (Task 8), `Button`, `Input`, `Label`, `Tag`.
- Produces:
  - `fieldEditor.ts`: `FIELD_TYPES`, `SCALAR_TYPES`, `typeLabel(field)`, `newField(existing)`, `changeType(field, type)`, `moveField(fields, from, to)`, `replaceField(fields, i, field)`, `removeField(fields, i)`, `fieldPath(prefix, i)`, `hasProblemUnder(problems, path)`
  - `SchemaBuilder({value, onChange, problems?, pathPrefix?, nested?})`: problems use the server's schema paths (`fields[0].fields[2].name`).

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/fieldEditor.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { changeType, fieldPath, hasProblemUnder, moveField, newField, removeField, replaceField, typeLabel } from './fieldEditor.ts';
import type { SchemaField } from './types.ts';

describe('field editor helpers', () => {
  it('labels types like the mockup', () => {
    expect(typeLabel({ name: 'a', type: 'list', item: { name: '', type: 'int' } })).toBe('list<int>');
    expect(typeLabel({ name: 'a', type: 'object', fields: [] })).toBe('sub-schema');
    expect(typeLabel({ name: 'a', type: 'repeater' })).toBe('repeater');
  });

  it('names new fields uniquely', () => {
    expect(newField([])).toEqual({ name: 'field_1', type: 'string' });
    expect(newField([{ name: 'field_2', type: 'string' }]).name).toBe('field_3');
    expect(newField([{ name: 'a', type: 'string' }, { name: 'field_2', type: 'int' }]).name).toBe('field_3');
  });

  it('changes a type and keeps only attributes that still apply', () => {
    const enumField: SchemaField = { name: 'php', label: 'PHP', type: 'enum', options: ['8.3'], default: '8.3', scope: 'host', required: true };
    expect(changeType(enumField, 'string')).toEqual({ name: 'php', label: 'PHP', type: 'string', scope: 'host', required: true });
    expect(changeType({ name: 'x', type: 'string' }, 'enum').options).toEqual(['option_1']);
    expect(changeType({ name: 'x', type: 'string' }, 'list').item).toEqual({ name: '', type: 'string' });
    expect(changeType({ name: 'x', type: 'string' }, 'repeater').fields).toEqual([{ name: 'value', type: 'string' }]);
    const list: SchemaField = { name: 'l', type: 'list', item: { name: '', type: 'string' }, min_items: 1, max_items: 3 };
    expect(changeType(list, 'repeater')).toMatchObject({ min_items: 1, max_items: 3 });
    expect(changeType({ name: 'n', type: 'int', min: 1, overridable: true }, 'float')).toEqual({ name: 'n', type: 'float', overridable: true });
    const obj: SchemaField = { name: 'o', type: 'object', fields: [{ name: 'k', type: 'int' }] };
    expect(changeType(obj, 'repeater').fields).toEqual(obj.fields);
  });

  it('moves, replaces and removes without mutating', () => {
    const fields: SchemaField[] = [{ name: 'a', type: 'string' }, { name: 'b', type: 'string' }, { name: 'c', type: 'string' }];
    expect(moveField(fields, 0, 2).map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['b', 'c', 'a']);
    expect(replaceField(fields, 1, { name: 'z', type: 'int' })[1].name).toBe('z');
    expect(removeField(fields, 0).map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['b', 'c']);
    expect(fields.map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['a', 'b', 'c']);
  });

  it('builds schema paths and finds problems below them', () => {
    expect(fieldPath('', 2)).toBe('fields[2]');
    expect(fieldPath('fields[2]', 0)).toBe('fields[2].fields[0]');
    const problems = [{ path: 'fields[1].fields[0].name', message: 'x' }];
    expect(hasProblemUnder(problems, 'fields[1]')).toBe(true);
    expect(hasProblemUnder(problems, 'fields[10]')).toBe(false);
    expect(hasProblemUnder([{ path: 'fields[1]', message: 'x' }], 'fields[1]')).toBe(true);
    expect(hasProblemUnder(undefined, 'fields[0]')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the helpers**

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/fieldEditor.ts`:

```ts
import type { FieldType, Problem, SchemaField } from './types.ts';

export const FIELD_TYPES: readonly FieldType[] = ['string', 'int', 'float', 'bool', 'enum', 'secret', 'list', 'repeater', 'object'];
export const SCALAR_TYPES: readonly FieldType[] = ['string', 'int', 'float', 'bool', 'enum'];

export function typeLabel(f: SchemaField): string {
  if (f.type === 'list') return `list<${f.item?.type ?? 'string'}>`;
  if (f.type === 'object') return 'sub-schema';
  return f.type;
}

export function newField(existing: readonly SchemaField[]): SchemaField {
  const names = new Set(existing.map((f) => f.name));
  let n = existing.length + 1;
  while (names.has(`field_${n}`)) n++;
  return { name: `field_${n}`, type: 'string' };
}

const COLLECTIONS = new Set<FieldType>(['list', 'repeater']);
const WITH_SUBFIELDS = new Set<FieldType>(['repeater', 'object']);

/** changeType switches a field's type, keeping what still means the same
 * thing (name, label, description, scope, required, overridable, item
 * counts between collections, sub-fields between repeater and object) and
 * giving the new type the structure the server requires. */
export function changeType(f: SchemaField, type: FieldType): SchemaField {
  const next: SchemaField = { name: f.name, type };
  if (f.label) next.label = f.label;
  if (f.description) next.description = f.description;
  if (f.scope) next.scope = f.scope;
  if (f.required) next.required = true;
  if (f.overridable) next.overridable = true;
  if (type === 'enum') next.options = f.options?.length ? f.options : ['option_1'];
  if (type === 'list') next.item = f.item ?? { name: '', type: 'string' };
  if (WITH_SUBFIELDS.has(type)) next.fields = f.fields?.length ? f.fields : [{ name: 'value', type: 'string' }];
  if (COLLECTIONS.has(type) && COLLECTIONS.has(f.type)) {
    if (f.min_items !== undefined) next.min_items = f.min_items;
    if (f.max_items !== undefined) next.max_items = f.max_items;
  }
  return next;
}

export function moveField(fields: readonly SchemaField[], from: number, to: number): SchemaField[] {
  const next = [...fields];
  next.splice(to, 0, next.splice(from, 1)[0]);
  return next;
}

export function replaceField(fields: readonly SchemaField[], index: number, field: SchemaField): SchemaField[] {
  return fields.map((f, i) => (i === index ? field : f));
}

export function removeField(fields: readonly SchemaField[], index: number): SchemaField[] {
  return fields.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
}

/** fieldPath is the server's path of field index inside the list at prefix. */
export function fieldPath(prefix: string, index: number): string {
  return `${prefix ? `${prefix}.` : ''}fields[${index}]`;
}

export function hasProblemUnder(problems: readonly Problem[] | undefined, path: string): boolean {
  return (problems ?? []).some((p) => p.path === path || p.path.startsWith(`${path}.`) || p.path.startsWith(`${path}[`));
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { SchemaBuilder } from './SchemaBuilder.tsx';
import type { Problem, VarSchema } from './types.ts';

function Harness({ initial = { fields: [] }, problems }: { initial?: VarSchema; problems?: Problem[] }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<VarSchema>(initial);
  return (
    <>
      <SchemaBuilder value={value} onChange={setValue} problems={problems} />
      <pre data-testid="schema">{JSON.stringify(value)}</pre>
    </>
  );
}

const schemaNow = () => JSON.parse(screen.getByTestId('schema').textContent ?? '{}') as VarSchema;
const editor = (name: string) => screen.getByRole('group', { name: `Field ${name}` });

describe('SchemaBuilder', () => {
  it('adds a field and edits name, type, scope and required', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<Harness />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add field' }));
    const ed = editor('field_1');
    const name = within(ed).getByLabelText('Name');
    await user.clear(name);
    await user.type(name, 'vhosts');
    await user.selectOptions(within(editor('vhosts')).getByLabelText('Type'), 'repeater');
    await user.click(within(editor('vhosts')).getByLabelText(/Host \(asked/));
    // The sub-schema's own editor sits inside this one, so take the first "Required".
    await user.click(within(editor('vhosts')).getAllByLabelText('Required')[0]);
    expect(schemaNow()).toEqual({
      fields: [{ name: 'vhosts', type: 'repeater', scope: 'host', required: true, fields: [{ name: 'value', type: 'string' }] }],
    });
    expect(within(editor('vhosts')).queryByLabelText('Hosts may override')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('edits a sub-schema with the nested builder', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<Harness initial={{ fields: [{ name: 'vhosts', type: 'repeater', scope: 'host', fields: [{ name: 'value', type: 'string' }] }] }} />);
    const outer = editor('vhosts');
    await user.click(within(outer).getByRole('button', { name: 'Add sub-field' }));
    const inner = editor('field_2');
    await user.selectOptions(within(inner).getByLabelText('Type'), 'secret');
    expect(within(inner).queryByLabelText(/Host \(asked/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(schemaNow().fields[0].fields).toEqual([{ name: 'value', type: 'string' }, { name: 'field_2', type: 'secret' }]);
  });

  it('edits enum options, bounds, pattern and default', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<Harness initial={{ fields: [{ name: 'php', type: 'enum', options: ['8.3'] }, { name: 'domain', type: 'string' }] }} />);
    const options = within(editor('php')).getByLabelText('Options (one per line)');
    await user.clear(options);
    await user.type(options, '8.3{enter}8.4');
    await user.selectOptions(within(editor('php')).getByLabelText('Default'), '8.4');
    expect(schemaNow().fields[0]).toEqual({ name: 'php', type: 'enum', options: ['8.3', '8.4'], default: '8.4' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^domain/ }));
    // paste, not type: user-event reads "[" as the start of a key descriptor.
    await user.click(within(editor('domain')).getByLabelText('Pattern'));
    await user.paste('^[a-z.]+$');
    await user.type(within(editor('domain')).getByLabelText('Max length'), '64');
    expect(schemaNow().fields[1]).toEqual({ name: 'domain', type: 'string', pattern: '^[a-z.]+$', max: 64 });
  });

  it('moves and deletes fields', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<Harness initial={{ fields: [{ name: 'a', type: 'string' }, { name: 'b', type: 'string' }] }} />);
    await user.click(within(editor('a')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Move down' }));
    expect(schemaNow().fields.map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['b', 'a']);
    await user.click(within(editor('a')).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete field' }));
    expect(schemaNow().fields.map((f) => f.name)).toEqual(['b']);
  });

  it('marks fields with server problems and shows the message at the attribute', () => {
    render(
      <Harness
        initial={{ fields: [{ name: 'vhosts', type: 'repeater', fields: [{ name: 'Bad', type: 'string' }] }] }}
        problems={[{ path: 'fields[0].fields[0].name', message: 'must start with a lowercase letter' }]}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^vhosts.*has problems/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(editor('Bad')).getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('must start with a lowercase letter');
  });

  it('previews the assignment form from host fields and overrides', () => {
    render(
      <Harness
        initial={{
          fields: [
            { name: 'env', type: 'string' },
            { name: 'php_default', type: 'enum', options: ['8.3'], overridable: true },
            { name: 'domain', type: 'string', scope: 'host' },
          ],
        }}
      />,
    );
    const preview = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Assignment form preview' });
    expect(within(preview).getByLabelText('php_default')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(preview).getByLabelText('domain')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(preview).queryByLabelText('env')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the builder**

`web/src/features/inventory/schema/SchemaBuilder.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../../ui/label.tsx';
import { Tag } from '../../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { SchemaForm, ScalarInput } from './SchemaForm.tsx';
import { membershipFields, problemAt, scopeOf } from './paths.ts';
import { FIELD_TYPES, SCALAR_TYPES, changeType, fieldPath, hasProblemUnder, moveField, newField, removeField, replaceField, typeLabel } from './fieldEditor.ts';
import type { FieldType, Problem, SchemaField, VarSchema, Vars } from './types.ts';

export interface SchemaBuilderProps {
  value: VarSchema;
  onChange: (next: VarSchema) => void;
  /** Schema problems from the server (fields[0].fields[2].name). */
  problems?: readonly Problem[];
  /** Server path of this field list: "" at the top, "fields[2]" inside a sub-schema. */
  pathPrefix?: string;
  /** A sub-schema: no scope, no overridable, no preview. */
  nested?: boolean;
}

const SELECT =
  'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground focus-visible:border-primary focus-visible:outline-none';

/**
 * SchemaBuilder edits a group's variable schema (SchemaBuilder mockup):
 * the field list on the left, the selected field's editor in the middle,
 * and, at the top level, the generated assignment form as a live preview.
 */
export function SchemaBuilder({ value, onChange, problems, pathPrefix = '', nested = false }: SchemaBuilderProps) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(0);
  const fields = value.fields;
  const index = Math.min(selected, fields.length - 1);
  const field = index >= 0 ? fields[index] : undefined;
  const setFields = (next: SchemaField[]) => onChange({ fields: next });
  const idBase = `sb-${pathPrefix.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-') || 'top'}`;

  return (
    <div className={nested ? 'flex flex-col gap-3' : 'grid grid-cols-[240px_minmax(0,1fr)_300px] items-start gap-4'}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1" aria-label={nested ? 'Sub-fields' : 'Fields'}>
          {fields.map((f, i) => (
            <li key={i}>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={i === index}
                onClick={() => setSelected(i)}
                className={`flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left ${
                  i === index ? 'border-primary bg-accent' : 'border-border bg-card hover:bg-accent'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="flex-1 truncate font-mono text-xs">{f.name}</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{typeLabel(f)}</span>
                {!nested && scopeOf(f) === 'host' && <Tag>host</Tag>}
                {f.required && <span className="text-[11px] text-destructive" aria-label="required">*</span>}
                {hasProblemUnder(problems, fieldPath(pathPrefix, i)) && (
                  <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full" style={{ background: 'var(--px-crit)' }} aria-label="has problems" />
                )}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          className="self-start"
          onClick={() => {
            setFields([...fields, newField(fields)]);
            setSelected(fields.length);
          }}
        >
          <Plus size={14} /> {nested ? 'Add sub-field' : 'Add field'}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {field ? (
        <FieldEditor
          field={field}
          path={fieldPath(pathPrefix, index)}
          idBase={`${idBase}-${index}`}
          nested={nested}
          problems={problems}
          canMoveUp={index > 0}
          canMoveDown={index < fields.length - 1}
          onChange={(f) => setFields(replaceField(fields, index, f))}
          onMove={(dir) => {
            setFields(moveField(fields, index, index + dir));
            setSelected(index + dir);
          }}
          onRemove={() => {
            setFields(removeField(fields, index));
            setSelected(Math.max(0, index - 1));
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">No fields yet.</p>
      )}

      {!nested && <AssignmentPreview schema={value} />}
    </div>
  );
}

interface FieldEditorProps {
  field: SchemaField;
  path: string;
  idBase: string;
  nested: boolean;
  problems?: readonly Problem[];
  canMoveUp: boolean;
  canMoveDown: boolean;
  onChange: (f: SchemaField) => void;
  onMove: (dir: -1 | 1) => void;
  onRemove: () => void;
}

function FieldEditor({ field, path, idBase, nested, problems, canMoveUp, canMoveDown, onChange, onMove, onRemove }: FieldEditorProps) {
  const id = (k: string) => `${idBase}-${k}`;
  const problem = (k: string) => problemAt(problems, `${path}.${k}`) ?? (k === 'name' ? problemAt(problems, path) : undefined);
  const set = (patch: Partial<SchemaField>) => onChange({ ...field, ...patch });
  const isText = field.type === 'string';
  const hasBounds = isText || field.type === 'int' || field.type === 'float';
  const isCollection = field.type === 'list' || field.type === 'repeater';

  return (
    <div role="group" aria-label={`Field ${field.name}`} className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[10px] border border-border bg-card p-4">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <TextField id={id('name')} label="Name" mono value={field.name} error={problem('name')} onChange={(v) => set({ name: v })} />
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor={id('type')}>Type</Label>
          <select id={id('type')} className={SELECT} value={field.type} onChange={(e) => onChange(changeType(field, e.target.value as FieldType))}>
            {FIELD_TYPES.map((t) => (
              <option key={t} value={t}>
                {t === 'object' ? 'sub-schema (object)' : t}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <TextField id={id('label')} label="Label" value={field.label ?? ''} onChange={(v) => set({ label: v || undefined })} />
      <TextField id={id('description')} label="Description" value={field.description ?? ''} onChange={(v) => set({ description: v || undefined })} />

      {!nested && (
        <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <legend className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Scope</legend>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <input type="radio" name={id('scope')} checked={scopeOf(field) === 'group'} onChange={() => set({ scope: undefined })} />
            Group (set once on the group)
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <input type="radio" name={id('scope')} checked={scopeOf(field) === 'host'} onChange={() => set({ scope: 'host', overridable: undefined })} />
            Host (asked when a host is assigned)
          </label>
        </fieldset>
      )}
      <Checkbox id={id('required')} label="Required" checked={!!field.required} onChange={(c) => set({ required: c || undefined })} />
      {!nested && scopeOf(field) === 'group' && (
        <Checkbox id={id('overridable')} label="Hosts may override" checked={!!field.overridable} onChange={(c) => set({ overridable: c || undefined })} />
      )}

      {field.type === 'enum' && (
        <OptionsField id={id('options')} value={field.options ?? []} error={problem('options')} onChange={(options) => set({ options, default: undefined })} />
      )}
      {hasBounds && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <NumberField id={id('min')} label={isText ? 'Min length' : 'Min'} value={field.min} error={problem('min')} onChange={(min) => set({ min })} />
          <NumberField id={id('max')} label={isText ? 'Max length' : 'Max'} value={field.max} error={problem('max')} onChange={(max) => set({ max })} />
        </div>
      )}
      {isText && <TextField id={id('pattern')} label="Pattern" mono value={field.pattern ?? ''} error={problem('pattern')} onChange={(v) => set({ pattern: v || undefined })} />}
      {isCollection && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <NumberField id={id('min_items')} label="Min items" value={field.min_items} error={problem('min_items')} onChange={(min_items) => set({ min_items })} />
          <NumberField id={id('max_items')} label="Max items" value={field.max_items} error={problem('max_items')} onChange={(max_items) => set({ max_items })} />
        </div>
      )}
      {field.type === 'list' && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor={id('item')}>Item type</Label>
          <select
            id={id('item')}
            className={SELECT}
            value={field.item?.type ?? 'string'}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const t = e.target.value as FieldType;
              set({ item: t === 'enum' ? { name: '', type: t, options: field.item?.options ?? ['option_1'] } : { name: '', type: t } });
            }}
          >
            {SCALAR_TYPES.map((t) => (
              <option key={t} value={t}>
                {t}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      )}
      {field.type === 'list' && field.item?.type === 'enum' && (
        <OptionsField id={id('item-options')} value={field.item.options ?? []} error={problem('item.options')} onChange={(options) => set({ item: { ...(field.item ?? { name: '', type: 'enum' }), options } })} />
      )}
      {SCALAR_TYPES.includes(field.type) && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor={id('default')}>Default</Label>
          <ScalarInput id={id('default')} field={{ ...field, default: undefined, required: false }} value={field.default} onChange={(d) => set({ default: d })} />
          {problem('default') && (
            <span role="alert" className="text-[11px] text-destructive">
              {problem('default')}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {(field.type === 'repeater' || field.type === 'object') && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-border pt-3">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold">Sub-schema</span>
          <SchemaBuilder nested value={{ fields: field.fields ?? [] }} onChange={(v) => set({ fields: v.fields })} problems={problems} pathPrefix={path} />
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex gap-2 border-t border-border pt-3">
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" disabled={!canMoveUp} onClick={() => onMove(-1)}>
          Move up
        </Button>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" disabled={!canMoveDown} onClick={() => onMove(1)}>
          Move down
        </Button>
        <Button type="button" variant="destructive" className="ml-auto" onClick={onRemove}>
          Delete field
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function TextField({ id, label, value, onChange, error, mono }: { id: string; label: string; value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void; error?: string; mono?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <Label htmlFor={id}>{label}</Label>
      <Input id={id} value={value} className={mono ? 'font-mono' : undefined} aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />
      {error && (
        <span role="alert" className="text-[11px] text-destructive">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function NumberField({ id, label, value, onChange, error }: { id: string; label: string; value?: number; onChange: (v: number | undefined) => void; error?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <Label htmlFor={id}>{label}</Label>
      <Input
        id={id}
        type="text"
        inputMode="decimal"
        className="font-mono"
        value={value === undefined ? '' : String(value)}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const raw = e.target.value.trim();
          const n = Number(raw);
          onChange(raw === '' || !Number.isFinite(n) ? undefined : n);
        }}
      />
      {error && (
        <span role="alert" className="text-[11px] text-destructive">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function OptionsField({ id, value, onChange, error }: { id: string; value: string[]; onChange: (v: string[]) => void; error?: string }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(value.join('\n'));
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <Label htmlFor={id}>Options (one per line)</Label>
      <textarea
        id={id}
        rows={4}
        className="rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs text-foreground focus-visible:border-primary focus-visible:outline-none"
        value={text}
        onChange={(e) => {
          setText(e.target.value);
          onChange(e.target.value.split('\n').map((o) => o.trim()).filter((o) => o !== ''));
        }}
      />
      {error && (
        <span role="alert" className="text-[11px] text-destructive">
          {error}
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Checkbox({ id, label, checked, onChange }: { id: string; label: string; checked: boolean; onChange: (c: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <label htmlFor={id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
      <input id={id} type="checkbox" className="h-4 w-4 accent-[var(--px-accent)]" checked={checked} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)} />
      {label}
    </label>
  );
}

function AssignmentPreview({ schema }: { schema: VarSchema }) {
  const [vars, setVars] = useState<Vars>({});
  return (
    <section aria-label="Assignment form preview" className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[10px] border border-border bg-card p-4">
      <h3 className="text-[13px] font-semibold">Assignment form</h3>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">What an operator fills in when assigning a host to this group. Group-scoped values stay on the group.</p>
      <SchemaForm fields={membershipFields(schema)} value={vars} onChange={setVars} idPrefix="preview" />
    </section>
  );
}
```

Check the tests against this code:
- Name edits change the group's accessible name (`Field field_1` → `Field vhosts`), which is why the first test looks the editor up again by its new name after renaming.
- `OptionsField` keeps its own text so typing a newline does not collapse into the parsed list mid-edit; the stored options are the non-empty trimmed lines, and changing them clears the default so it can never point at a removed option.
- A field whose name is being edited keeps its list row selected: selection is by index, not by name.
- The enum default select comes from `ScalarInput` with `default: undefined`, so its empty option reads "—".
