`web/src/api/hooks/useInventory.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';
import type { paths } from '../schema.d.ts';
import type { VarSchema, Vars } from '../../features/inventory/schema/types.ts';

type SchemaBody = paths['/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/schema']['put']['requestBody']['content']['application/json'];

const ifMatch = (revision: number) => ({ 'If-Match': `"${revision}"` });

/** toVarSchema reads a schema from an API response; the generated type
 * mirrors VarSchema but may mark lists nullable. */
export function toVarSchema(raw: unknown): VarSchema {
  const fields = (raw as { fields?: VarSchema['fields'] | null } | undefined)?.fields;
  return { fields: fields ?? [] };
}

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['inventory', org] });
}

export function useGroups(org: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['inventory', org, 'groups'],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org,
  });
}

export function useGroup(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['inventory', org, 'group', id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}

export function useCreateGroup(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: { name: string; description?: string; parent_id?: string }) =>
      unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export interface GroupPatch {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  parent_id?: string;
  bastion_id?: string;
  revision: number;
}

export function useUpdateGroup(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ revision, ...body }: GroupPatch) =>
      unwrap(api.PATCH('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteGroup(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useSetGroupSchema(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ schema, revision }: { schema: VarSchema; revision: number }) =>
      unwrap(
        api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/schema', {
          params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) },
          body: { schema } as SchemaBody,
        }),
      ),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

/** gcTime: 0 — the variables carry plaintext secrets the user just typed;
 * the page also calls reset() after a successful save (the v0.1 token
 * lesson: nothing secret may linger in the mutation cache). */
export function useSetGroupVars(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ vars, revision }: { vars: Vars; revision: number }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/vars', { params: { path: { org, id }, header: ifMatch(revision) }, body: { vars } })),
    gcTime: 0,
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useGroupMembers(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['inventory', org, 'members', id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/members', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}

/** gcTime: 0 for the same reason as useSetGroupVars. */
export function useAssignHost(org: string, groupId: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ hostId, vars, revision }: { hostId: string; vars: Vars; revision?: number }) =>
      unwrap(
        api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/members/{host}', {
          params: { path: { org, id: groupId, host: hostId }, header: revision === undefined ? {} : ifMatch(revision) },
          body: { vars },
        }),
      ),
    gcTime: 0,
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useUnassignHost(org: string, groupId: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (hostId: string) =>
      unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/groups/{id}/members/{host}', { params: { path: { org, id: groupId, host: hostId } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useInventoryPreview(org: string, groupId?: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['inventory', org, 'preview', groupId ?? ''],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/inventory/preview', { params: { path: { org }, query: { group: groupId ?? '' } } })),
    enabled: !!org,
  });
}

export function useHostInventoryGroups(org: string, hostId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['inventory', org, 'host-groups', hostId],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/inventory/groups', { params: { path: { org, id: hostId } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!hostId,
  });
}

export function useHostInventoryPreview(org: string, hostId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['inventory', org, 'host-preview', hostId],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/inventory/preview', { params: { path: { org, id: hostId } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!hostId,
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tree.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { descendantIds, orderTree } from './tree.ts';

const groups = [
  { id: 'php', name: 'php', parent_id: 'web' },
  { id: 'web', name: 'webserver', parent_id: 'prod' },
  { id: 'prod', name: 'production', parent_id: null },
  { id: 'db', name: 'dbserver', parent_id: 'prod' },
  { id: 'lost', name: 'lost', parent_id: 'gone' },
];

describe('tree', () => {
  it('orders depth first, children by name, dangling parents as roots', () => {
    expect(orderTree(groups).map((r) => `${r.depth}:${r.group.name}`)).toEqual([
      '0:lost', '0:production', '1:dbserver', '1:webserver', '2:php',
    ]);
  });

  it('collects descendants', () => {
    expect([...descendantIds(groups, 'prod')].sort()).toEqual(['db', 'php', 'web']);
    expect(descendantIds(groups, 'php').size).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tree.ts`:

```ts
export interface TreeNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parent_id?: string | null;
}

export interface TreeRow<T> {
  group: T;
  depth: number;
}

/** orderTree lists groups depth first with children sorted by name; a
 * group whose parent is missing is shown as a root. */
export function orderTree<T extends TreeNode>(groups: readonly T[]): TreeRow<T>[] {
  const ids = new Set(groups.map((g) => g.id));
  const byParent = new Map<string, T[]>();
  for (const g of groups) {
    const key = g.parent_id && ids.has(g.parent_id) ? g.parent_id : '';
    byParent.set(key, [...(byParent.get(key) ?? []), g]);
  }
  for (const list of byParent.values()) list.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  const out: TreeRow<T>[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const walk = (key: string, depth: number) => {
    for (const g of byParent.get(key) ?? []) {
      if (seen.has(g.id)) continue;
      seen.add(g.id);
      out.push({ group: g, depth });
      walk(g.id, depth + 1);
    }
  };
  walk('', 0);
  return out;
}

/** descendantIds returns every group below id (a group cannot move there). */
export function descendantIds(groups: readonly TreeNode[], id: string): Set<string> {
  const out = new Set<string>();
  const visit = (parent: string) => {
    for (const g of groups) {
      if (g.parent_id === parent && !out.has(g.id)) {
        out.add(g.id);
        visit(g.id);
      }
    }
  };
  visit(id);
  return out;
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/CreateGroupDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useCreateGroup } from '../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { InventoryGroupSummary } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { orderTree } from './tree.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

export function CreateGroupDialog({
  org, open, onClose, groups, fixedParent, onCreated,
}: {
  org: string;
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  groups: readonly InventoryGroupSummary[];
  fixedParent?: string;
  onCreated: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const create = useCreateGroup(org);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [parent, setParent] = useState(fixedParent ?? '');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await create.mutateAsync({ name, description: description || undefined, parent_id: parent || undefined });
      toast.show(`Group ${res.group.name} created`);
      setName('');
      setDescription('');
      onCreated(res.group.id);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Creating the group failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onClose={onClose} title={fixedParent ? 'Add child group' : 'Add group'}>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="group-name">Name</Label>
          <Input id="group-name" className="font-mono" required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">Lowercase letters, digits and underscores; this becomes the Ansible group name.</span>
        </div>
        {!fixedParent && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="group-parent">Parent</Label>
            <select id="group-parent" className={SELECT} value={parent} onChange={(e) => setParent(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">— none (top level) —</option>
              {orderTree(groups).map(({ group, depth }) => (
                <option key={group.id} value={group.id}>
                  {'  '.repeat(depth)}
                  {group.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="group-description">Description</Label>
          <Input id="group-description" value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending}>
            Create group
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/GroupsPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useGroups } from '../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import { useBastions } from '../../api/hooks/useSsh.ts';
import { CreateGroupDialog } from './CreateGroupDialog.tsx';
import { orderTree } from './tree.ts';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 1fr) 90px 170px 120px 160px';

/** GroupsPage lists the organization's Ansible inventory groups as a tree. */
export function GroupsPage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const groups = useGroups(org);
  const bastions = useBastions(org);
  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(groups);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!groups.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = groups.data.groups ?? [];
  const bastionName = (id?: string | null) => bastions.data?.bastions?.find((b) => b.id === id)?.name ?? '—';

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Groups</h1>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setCreateOpen(true)}>
          <Layers size={15} /> Add group
        </Button>
      </div>
      <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">{list.length} inventory groups · each group owns a variable schema</span>
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Group</span>
          <span>Members</span>
          <span>Fields</span>
          <span>Values</span>
          <span>Jump host</span>
        </GridHeader>
        {orderTree(list).map(({ group, depth }) => (
          <GridRow key={group.id} columns={COLUMNS}>
            <Link
              to="/o/$org/groups/$id"
              params={{ org, id: group.id }}
              search={{ tab: undefined }}
              className="truncate font-mono text-[13px] text-foreground hover:text-primary"
              style={{ paddingLeft: depth * 16 }}
            >
              {group.name}
            </Link>
            <span className="text-[13px]">{group.members}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {group.group_fields} group · {group.host_fields} host
            </span>
            <span>{group.complete ? <Pill tone="ok">complete</Pill> : <Pill tone="warn">incomplete</Pill>}</span>
            <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">{bastionName(group.bastion_id)}</span>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No inventory groups yet.</p>}
      </Card>
      <CreateGroupDialog
        org={org}
        open={createOpen}
        onClose={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
        groups={list}
        onCreated={(id) => {
          setCreateOpen(false);
          void navigate({ to: '/o/$org/groups/$id', params: { org, id }, search: { tab: 'schema' } });
        }}
      />
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/GroupsPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { GroupsPage } from './GroupsPage.tsx';

const summary = (id: string, name: string, parent_id: string | null, extra = {}) => ({
  id, name, description: '', parent_id, members: 0, group_fields: 1, host_fields: 0, complete: true, revision: 1, ...extra,
});

function renderPage() {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const orgRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '/o/$org' });
  const listRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/groups', component: GroupsPage });
  const detailRoute = createRoute({
    getParentRoute: () => orgRoute,
    path: '/groups/$id',
    validateSearch: (s: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ tab: typeof s.tab === 'string' ? s.tab : undefined }),
    component: () => <div>GROUP DETAIL</div>,
  });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([listRoute, detailRoute])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/o/acme/groups'] }),
  });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('GroupsPage', () => {
  it('renders the tree and creates a group below a parent', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let posted: unknown;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups': {
        groups: [
          summary('w', 'webserver', 'p', { members: 14, host_fields: 1, complete: false, bastion_id: 'b1' }),
          summary('p', 'production', null),
        ],
      },
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions': { bastions: [{ id: 'b1', name: 'bastion-fra', address: 'b.example', port: 22, username: 'ops' }] },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups': (req: MockRequest) => {
        posted = req.body;
        return { group: { ...summary('n', 'dbserver', 'p'), schema: { fields: [] }, vars: {}, problems: [], orphaned: [], created_at: '', updated_at: '' } };
      },
    });
    renderPage();

    const web = await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'webserver' });
    expect(web).toHaveStyle({ paddingLeft: '16px' });
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'production' })).toHaveStyle({ paddingLeft: '0px' });
    expect(screen.getByText('incomplete')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('bastion-fra')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add group' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'dbserver');
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Parent'), 'p');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create group' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('GROUP DETAIL')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(posted).toEqual({ name: 'dbserver', parent_id: 'p' });
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';

/** StaleNotice answers a 412: someone else saved this group first. */
export function StaleNotice({ org }: { org: string }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-4 py-3 text-xs">
      <span>This group changed since you loaded it. Reload to see the current version, then apply your change again.</span>
      <Button onClick={() => void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['inventory', org] })}>Reload</Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tabs/SchemaTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { errorStatus } from '../../../lib/apiError.ts';
import { toVarSchema, useSetGroupSchema } from '../../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { InventoryGroup } from '../../../api/types.ts';
import { SchemaBuilder } from '../schema/SchemaBuilder.tsx';
import { problemsFromError } from '../schema/paths.ts';
import type { Problem, VarSchema } from '../schema/types.ts';
import { StaleNotice } from './StaleNotice.tsx';

export function SchemaTab({ org, group }: { org: string; group: InventoryGroup }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const save = useSetGroupSchema(org, group.id);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<VarSchema>(() => toVarSchema(group.schema));
  const [problems, setProblems] = useState<Problem[]>([]);
  const [stale, setStale] = useState(false);
  // The builder keeps per-field editor state keyed by field identity; a
  // wholesale reset of the draft (Discard) must remount it, or an editor
  // could keep showing text from before the reset.
  const [builderKey, setBuilderKey] = useState(0);

  async function onSave() {
    setProblems([]);
    try {
      const res = await save.mutateAsync({ schema: draft, revision: group.revision });
      toast.show(res.incomplete_members > 0 ? `Schema saved · ${res.incomplete_members} members need new values` : 'Schema saved');
    } catch (err) {
      if (errorStatus(err) === 412) return setStale(true);
      const ps = problemsFromError(err, 'body.schema');
      if (ps.length > 0) setProblems(ps);
      else toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving the schema failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {stale && <StaleNotice org={org} />}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {draft.fields.length} fields · {draft.fields.filter((f) => f.scope === 'host').length} host-scoped
        </span>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              setDraft(toVarSchema(group.schema));
              setProblems([]);
              setBuilderKey((k) => k + 1);
            }}
          >
            Discard
          </Button>
          <Button variant="primary" disabled={save.isPending} onClick={() => void onSave()}>
            Save schema
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {problems.length > 0 && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
          The schema has {problems.length} problem{problems.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}; fields with problems are marked.
        </p>
      )}
      <SchemaBuilder key={builderKey} value={draft} onChange={setDraft} problems={problems} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tabs/ValuesTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { errorStatus } from '../../../lib/apiError.ts';
import { toVarSchema, useSetGroupVars } from '../../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { InventoryGroup } from '../../../api/types.ts';
import { SchemaForm } from '../schema/SchemaForm.tsx';
import { groupFields, problemsFromError } from '../schema/paths.ts';
import { declaredOnly, toKeepMarkers } from '../schema/secrets.ts';
import type { Problem, Vars } from '../schema/types.ts';
import { StaleNotice } from './StaleNotice.tsx';

export function ValuesTab({ org, group }: { org: string; group: InventoryGroup }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const fields = groupFields(toVarSchema(group.schema));
  const save = useSetGroupVars(org, group.id);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Vars>(() => toKeepMarkers(fields, declaredOnly(fields, (group.vars ?? {}) as Vars)));
  const [problems, setProblems] = useState<Problem[]>(() => (group.problems ?? []).map((p) => ({ path: p.path, message: p.message })));
  const [stale, setStale] = useState(false);

  async function onSave() {
    setProblems([]);
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({ vars: declaredOnly(fields, draft), revision: group.revision });
      save.reset();
      toast.show('Group values saved');
    } catch (err) {
      if (errorStatus(err) === 412) return setStale(true);
      const ps = problemsFromError(err, 'body.vars');
      if (ps.length > 0) setProblems(ps);
      else toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving the values failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  if (fields.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">This group's schema has no group-scoped fields yet. Add them on the Variable schema tab.</p>;
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex max-w-[640px] flex-col gap-4">
      {stale && <StaleNotice org={org} />}
      {(group.orphaned ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <p className="rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-3 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          Values of removed fields are still stored: <span className="font-mono">{(group.orphaned ?? []).join(', ')}</span>. Saving this form drops them.
        </p>
      )}
      <SchemaForm fields={fields} value={draft} onChange={setDraft} problems={problems} idPrefix="values" />
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <Button variant="primary" disabled={save.isPending} onClick={() => void onSave()}>
          Save values
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tabs/AssignHostDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from '../../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { errorStatus } from '../../../lib/apiError.ts';
import { useHosts } from '../../../api/hooks/useHosts.ts';
import { toVarSchema, useAssignHost } from '../../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { InventoryGroup, InventoryMember } from '../../../api/types.ts';
import { SchemaForm } from '../schema/SchemaForm.tsx';
import { groupFields, membershipFields, problemsFromError } from '../schema/paths.ts';
import { declaredOnly, toKeepMarkers } from '../schema/secrets.ts';
import type { Problem, Vars } from '../schema/types.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

/** AssignHostDialog is the generated assignment form (SchemaBuilder mockup,
 * right column): a managed host, the group's host-scoped fields and
 * overrides, and the group's own values read-only. With a member it edits
 * that member's values instead. */
export function AssignHostDialog({
  org, group, member, existing, onClose,
}: {
  org: string;
  group: InventoryGroup;
  member?: InventoryMember;
  existing: readonly string[];
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const schema = toVarSchema(group.schema);
  const fields = membershipFields(schema);
  const ownFields = groupFields(schema);
  const hosts = useHosts(org, { managed: true });
  const assignable = (hosts.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.hosts ?? []) ?? []).filter((h) => !existing.includes(h.id));
  const assign = useAssignHost(org, group.id);
  const [hostId, setHostId] = useState(member?.host_id ?? '');
  const [vars, setVars] = useState<Vars>(() => (member ? toKeepMarkers(fields, declaredOnly(fields, (member.vars ?? {}) as Vars)) : {}));
  const [problems, setProblems] = useState<Problem[]>(() => (member?.problems ?? []).map((p) => ({ path: p.path, message: p.message })));
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setProblems([]);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await assign.mutateAsync({ hostId, vars: declaredOnly(fields, vars), revision: member?.revision });
      assign.reset();
      toast.show(member ? `Values of ${member.host_name} saved` : `Host assigned to ${group.name}`);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      if (errorStatus(err) === 412) return setError('These values changed since you opened them. Close the dialog, reload and try again.');
      const ps = problemsFromError(err, 'body.vars');
      if (ps.length > 0) setProblems(ps);
      else setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} size="wide" title={member ? `Values of ${member.host_name}` : `Assign host to ${group.name}`}>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex max-h-[70vh] flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto">
        {!member && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="assign-host">Host</Label>
            <select id="assign-host" className={SELECT} required value={hostId} onChange={(e) => setHostId(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">Choose a managed host…</option>
              {assignable.map((h) => (
                <option key={h.id} value={h.id}>
                  {h.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            {hosts.data && assignable.length === 0 && <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">Every managed host is already a member.</span>}
          </div>
        )}
        <SchemaForm fields={fields} value={vars} onChange={setVars} problems={problems} idPrefix="assign" />
        {ownFields.length > 0 && (
          <details className="rounded-md border border-border p-3">
            <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold">Group values (read-only)</summary>
            <div className="pt-3">
              <SchemaForm fields={ownFields} value={toKeepMarkers(ownFields, (group.vars ?? {}) as Vars)} onChange={() => undefined} disabled idPrefix="group-ro" />
            </div>
          </details>
        )}
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={!hostId || assign.isPending}>
            {member ? 'Save values' : `Assign to ${group.name}`}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tabs/MembersTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useGroupMembers, useUnassignHost } from '../../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { InventoryGroup, InventoryMember } from '../../../api/types.ts';
import { AssignHostDialog } from './AssignHostDialog.tsx';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 1fr) 200px 150px';

export function MembersTab({ org, group }: { org: string; group: InventoryGroup }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const members = useGroupMembers(org, group.id);
  const unassign = useUnassignHost(org, group.id);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<{ member?: InventoryMember } | null>(null);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(members);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!members.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = members.data.members ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {list.length} direct members · {list.filter((m) => !m.complete).length} need values
        </span>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setDialog({})}>
          Assign host
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Host</span>
          <span>Values</span>
          <span />
        </GridHeader>
        {list.map((m) => (
          <GridRow key={m.host_id} columns={COLUMNS}>
            <Link to="/o/$org/hosts/$id" params={{ org, id: m.host_id }} className="truncate font-mono text-[13px] hover:text-primary">
              {m.host_name}
            </Link>
            <span>{m.complete ? <Pill tone="ok">complete</Pill> : <Pill tone="warn">{(m.problems ?? []).length} to fill in</Pill>}</span>
            <span className="flex justify-end gap-1">
              <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDialog({ member: m })} aria-label={`Edit values of ${m.host_name}`}>
                Edit
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                aria-label={`Remove ${m.host_name}`}
                onClick={async () => {
                  if (!confirm(`Remove ${m.host_name} from ${group.name}? Its values for this group are deleted.`)) return;
                  await unassign.mutateAsync(m.host_id);
                  toast.show(`${m.host_name} removed from ${group.name}`);
                }}
              >
                Remove
              </Button>
            </span>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No hosts in this group yet.</p>}
      </Card>
      {dialog && (
        <AssignHostDialog org={org} group={group} member={dialog.member} existing={list.map((m) => m.host_id)} onClose={() => setDialog(null)} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tabs/ChildrenTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Card, GridRow } from '../../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import type { InventoryGroup, InventoryGroupSummary } from '../../../api/types.ts';
import { CreateGroupDialog } from '../CreateGroupDialog.tsx';

export function ChildrenTab({ org, group, groups }: { org: string; group: InventoryGroup; groups: readonly InventoryGroupSummary[] }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const children = groups.filter((g) => g.parent_id === group.id);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Child groups inherit this group's values through Ansible's group inheritance.</span>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
          Add child group
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Card>
        {children.map((c) => (
          <GridRow key={c.id} columns="minmax(0, 1fr) 120px">
            <Link to="/o/$org/groups/$id" params={{ org, id: c.id }} search={{ tab: undefined }} className="font-mono text-[13px] hover:text-primary">
              {c.name}
            </Link>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{c.members} members</span>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
        {children.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No child groups.</p>}
      </Card>
      <CreateGroupDialog
        org={org}
        open={open}
        onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        groups={groups}
        fixedParent={group.id}
        onCreated={(id) => {
          setOpen(false);
          void navigate({ to: '/o/$org/groups/$id', params: { org, id }, search: { tab: 'schema' } });
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useInventoryPreview } from '../../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { InventoryGroup } from '../../../api/types.ts';

export function PreviewTab({ org, group }: { org: string; group: InventoryGroup }) {
  const preview = useInventoryPreview(org, group.id);
  const guard = useQueryGuard(preview);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!preview.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const json = JSON.stringify(preview.data.inventory, null, 2);
  const warnings = preview.data.warnings ?? [];
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          The inventory a job targeting <span className="font-mono">{group.name}</span> receives. Secrets are masked here; a job run gets the real values.
        </p>
        <Button onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(json)}>Copy</Button>
      </div>
      {warnings.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5" aria-label="Warnings">
          {warnings.map((w, i) => (
            <li key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              <Pill tone="warn">warning</Pill>
              {w.host && <span className="font-mono">{w.host}</span>}
              <span>{w.message}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <pre aria-label="Inventory JSON" className="max-h-[560px] overflow-auto rounded-[10px] border border-border bg-background p-4 font-mono text-[12px] leading-relaxed">
        {json}
      </pre>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/tabs/SettingsTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { errorStatus } from '../../../lib/apiError.ts';
import { useDeleteGroup, useUpdateGroup } from '../../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import { useBastions } from '../../../api/hooks/useSsh.ts';
import type { InventoryGroup, InventoryGroupSummary } from '../../../api/types.ts';
import { descendantIds, orderTree } from '../tree.ts';
import { StaleNotice } from './StaleNotice.tsx';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

export function SettingsTab({ org, group, groups }: { org: string; group: InventoryGroup; groups: readonly InventoryGroupSummary[] }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const bastions = useBastions(org);
  const update = useUpdateGroup(org, group.id);
  const remove = useDeleteGroup(org);
  const [name, setName] = useState(group.name);
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(group.description);
  const [parent, setParent] = useState(group.parent_id ?? '');
  const [bastion, setBastion] = useState(group.bastion_id ?? '');
  const [stale, setStale] = useState(false);
  const excluded = descendantIds(groups, group.id).add(group.id);

  async function save(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      await update.mutateAsync({ name, description: description || undefined, parent_id: parent || undefined, bastion_id: bastion || undefined, revision: group.revision });
      toast.show('Group saved');
    } catch (err) {
      if (errorStatus(err) === 412) return setStale(true);
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  async function onDelete() {
    if (!confirm(`Delete group ${group.name}? Its members lose the values they have for this group.`)) return;
    try {
      await remove.mutateAsync(group.id);
      toast.show(`Group ${group.name} deleted`);
      await navigate({ to: '/o/$org/groups', params: { org } });
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deleting failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={save} className="flex max-w-[560px] flex-col gap-4">
      {stale && <StaleNotice org={org} />}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <Label htmlFor="settings-name">Name</Label>
        <Input id="settings-name" className="font-mono" required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <Label htmlFor="settings-description">Description</Label>
        <Input id="settings-description" value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} />
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <Label htmlFor="settings-parent">Parent</Label>
        <select id="settings-parent" className={SELECT} value={parent} onChange={(e) => setParent(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">— none (top level) —</option>
          {orderTree(groups)
            .filter(({ group: g }) => !excluded.has(g.id))
            .map(({ group: g, depth }) => (
              <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>
                {'  '.repeat(depth)}
                {g.name}
              </option>
            ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <Label htmlFor="settings-bastion">Jump host</Label>
        <select id="settings-bastion" className={SELECT} value={bastion} onChange={(e) => setBastion(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">— none (inherit from parent groups or the zone) —</option>
          {(bastions.data?.bastions ?? []).map((b) => (
            <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
              {b.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">Resolution: host → inventory group → zone default. Chains are rendered as ProxyJump.</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex justify-between">
        <Button type="button" variant="destructive" onClick={() => void onDelete()}>
          Delete group
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={update.isPending}>
          Save
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/inventory/GroupDetailPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useNavigate, useParams, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Tag } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { useGroup, useGroups } from '../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import { SchemaTab } from './tabs/SchemaTab.tsx';
import { ValuesTab } from './tabs/ValuesTab.tsx';
import { MembersTab } from './tabs/MembersTab.tsx';
import { ChildrenTab } from './tabs/ChildrenTab.tsx';
import { PreviewTab } from './tabs/PreviewTab.tsx';
import { SettingsTab } from './tabs/SettingsTab.tsx';

const TABS = [
  ['schema', 'Variable schema'],
  ['values', 'Group values'],
  ['members', 'Members'],
  ['children', 'Children'],
  ['preview', 'Preview inventory'],
  ['settings', 'Settings'],
] as const;

type TabKey = (typeof TABS)[number][0];

function isTab(v: unknown): v is TabKey {
  return TABS.some(([k]) => k === v);
}

/** GroupDetailPage follows the SchemaBuilder mockup's group page. Editing
 * tabs are keyed by the group's revision, so a successful save (which
 * bumps the revision) starts them over from what the server stored. */
export function GroupDetailPage() {
  const { org, id } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string; id: string };
  const search = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { tab?: string };
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const tab: TabKey = isTab(search.tab) ? search.tab : 'schema';
  const group = useGroup(org, id);
  const groups = useGroups(org);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(group);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!group.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const g = group.data.group;
  const all = groups.data?.groups ?? [];
  const parent = all.find((x) => x.id === g.parent_id);
  const own = all.find((x) => x.id === g.id);
  const children = all.filter((x) => x.parent_id === g.id);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <h1 className="font-mono text-xl font-bold">{g.name}</h1>
          <Tag>inventory group</Tag>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          parent: {parent?.name ?? 'none'} · {own?.members ?? 0} members · {children.length} children
          {g.description ? ` · ${g.description}` : ''}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div role="tablist" className="flex gap-6 border-b border-border">
        {TABS.map(([key, label]) => (
          <button
            key={key}
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={key === tab}
            className={`border-b-2 py-2.5 text-[13px] ${key === tab ? 'border-primary font-semibold text-foreground' : 'border-transparent font-medium text-muted-foreground'}`}
            onClick={() => void navigate({ to: '/o/$org/groups/$id', params: { org, id }, search: { tab: key } })}
          >
            {label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div role="tabpanel">
        {tab === 'schema' && <SchemaTab key={`schema-${g.revision}`} org={org} group={g} />}
        {tab === 'values' && <ValuesTab key={`values-${g.revision}`} org={org} group={g} />}
        {tab === 'members' && <MembersTab org={org} group={g} />}
        {tab === 'children' && <ChildrenTab org={org} group={g} groups={all} />}
        {tab === 'preview' && <PreviewTab org={org} group={g} />}
        {tab === 'settings' && <SettingsTab key={`settings-${g.revision}`} org={org} group={g} groups={all} />}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { GroupDetailPage } from './GroupDetailPage.tsx';

const SCHEMA = {
  fields: [
    { name: 'env', type: 'enum', options: ['prod', 'staging'], required: true },
    { name: 'db_password', type: 'secret' },
    { name: 'php_default', type: 'enum', options: ['8.3', '8.4'], default: '8.3', overridable: true },
    { name: 'domain', type: 'string', scope: 'host', required: true },
  ],
};

const GROUP = {
  group: {
    id: 'g1', name: 'webserver', description: '', parent_id: null, revision: 3, schema: SCHEMA,
    vars: { env: 'prod', db_password: { $secret: 'set' }, removed: 1 }, problems: [], orphaned: ['removed'],
    created_at: '', updated_at: '',
  },
};

const GROUPS = { groups: [{ id: 'g1', name: 'webserver', description: '', parent_id: null, members: 1, group_fields: 3, host_fields: 1, complete: true, revision: 3 }] };

function renderAt(path: string) {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const orgRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '/o/$org' });
  const detail = createRoute({
    getParentRoute: () => orgRoute,
    path: '/groups/$id',
    validateSearch: (s: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ tab: typeof s.tab === 'string' ? s.tab : undefined }),
    component: GroupDetailPage,
  });
  const list = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/groups', component: () => <div>GROUPS</div> });
  const host = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/hosts/$id', component: () => <div>HOST</div> });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([detail, list, host])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: [path] }),
  });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

const base = {
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1': GROUP,
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups': GROUPS,
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions': { bastions: [] },
};

describe('GroupDetailPage', () => {
  it('opens on the schema tab and switches tabs', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockApi(base);
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Fields' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Group values' }));
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('env')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Values of removed fields are still stored/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('saves group values with If-Match, a keep marker for the stored secret, and without orphans', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let seen: MockRequest | undefined;
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1/vars': (req: MockRequest) => {
        seen = req;
        return GROUP;
      },
    });
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1?tab=values');
    await user.selectOptions(await screen.findByLabelText('env'), 'staging');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save values' }));
    await screen.findByText('Group values saved');
    expect(seen?.headers.get('If-Match')).toBe('"3"');
    expect(seen?.body).toEqual({ vars: { env: 'staging', db_password: { $secret: 'keep', from: 'db_password' } } });
  });

  it('shows the server problems of a rejected save at their fields', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1/vars': () =>
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 422, errors: [{ location: 'body.vars.env', message: 'must be one of prod, staging' }] }), {
          status: 422, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        }),
    });
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1?tab=values');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Save values' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('must be one of prod, staging');
  });

  it('offers a reload when the schema changed underneath', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1/schema': () =>
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 412, detail: 'changed' }), { status: 412, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }),
    });
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Save schema' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/changed since you loaded it/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reload' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('assigns a managed host through the generated form', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let put: MockRequest | undefined;
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1/members': {
        members: [{ group_id: 'g1', group_name: 'webserver', host_id: 'h1', host_name: 'web-01', vars: {}, complete: false, problems: [{ path: 'domain', message: 'is required' }], orphaned: [], revision: 1 }],
      },
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/hosts': {
        hosts: [
          { id: 'h1', name: 'web-01', zone_id: 'z', address: '', managed: true, monitored: false, facts: {}, created_at: '' },
          { id: 'h2', name: 'web-02', zone_id: 'z', address: '', managed: true, monitored: false, facts: {}, created_at: '' },
        ],
        next_cursor: '',
      },
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1/members/h2': (req: MockRequest) => {
        put = req;
        return { member: { group_id: 'g1', group_name: 'webserver', host_id: 'h2', host_name: 'web-02', vars: {}, complete: true, problems: [], orphaned: [], revision: 1 } };
      },
    });
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1?tab=members');
    expect(await screen.findByText('1 to fill in')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Assign host' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog');
    const hostSelect = within(dialog).getByLabelText('Host');
    expect(within(hostSelect).queryByRole('option', { name: 'web-01' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.selectOptions(hostSelect, 'h2');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('domain'), 'shop.acme.io');
    // The read-only group values below the form also have a php_default; the override comes first.
    await user.selectOptions(within(dialog).getAllByLabelText('php_default')[0], '8.4');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Assign to webserver' }));
    await screen.findByText('Host assigned to webserver');
    expect(put?.body).toEqual({ vars: { domain: 'shop.acme.io', php_default: '8.4' } });
    expect(put?.headers.get('If-Match')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('renders the masked preview and its warnings', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup(); // installs a clipboard stub on navigator
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/preview': {
        inventory: { all: { children: ['webserver'] }, webserver: { vars: { db_password: '********' } } },
        connections: {},
        warnings: [{ host: 'web-03', message: 'groups dmz and edge set different jump hosts; dmz wins' }],
      },
    });
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1?tab=preview');
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Inventory JSON')).toHaveTextContent('"db_password": "********"');
    expect(screen.getByText(/dmz wins/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Copy' }));
    expect(await navigator.clipboard.readText()).toContain('"webserver"');
  });

  it('saves settings with the jump host and reports a refused delete', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let patched: MockRequest | undefined;
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions': { bastions: [{ id: 'b1', name: 'bastion-fra', address: 'b.example', port: 22, username: 'ops' }] },
      'PATCH /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1': (req: MockRequest) => {
        patched = req;
        return GROUP;
      },
      'DELETE /api/v1/orgs/acme/inventory/groups/g1': () =>
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 409, detail: 'the group still has child groups; move or delete them first' }), {
          status: 409, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        }),
    });
    renderAt('/o/acme/groups/g1?tab=settings');
    await user.selectOptions(await screen.findByLabelText('Jump host'), 'b1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    await screen.findByText('Group saved');
    expect(patched?.headers.get('If-Match')).toBe('"3"');
    expect(patched?.body).toEqual({ name: 'webserver', bastion_id: 'b1' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete group' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/still has child groups/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Card } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { useHostInventoryGroups } from '../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';

/** HostInventoryCard lists the inventory groups a managed host is a direct
 * member of, with whether its values are complete. */
export function HostInventoryCard({ org, hostId, managed }: { org: string; hostId: string; managed: boolean }) {
  const groups = useHostInventoryGroups(org, managed ? hostId : '');
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <h2 className="mb-3 text-[13px] font-semibold">Inventory groups</h2>
      {!managed && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Inventory groups need the Managed capability.</p>}
      {managed && groups.data && (groups.data.groups ?? []).length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Not in any inventory group yet.</p>}
      {managed && (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {(groups.data?.groups ?? []).map((m) => (
            <li key={m.group_id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <Link to="/o/$org/groups/$id" params={{ org, id: m.group_id }} search={{ tab: 'members' }} className="font-mono text-xs hover:text-primary">
                {m.group_name}
              </Link>
              {m.complete ? <Pill tone="ok">complete</Pill> : <Pill tone="warn">{(m.problems ?? []).length} to fill in</Pill>}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { HostInventoryCard } from './HostInventoryCard.tsx';

function renderCard(managed: boolean) {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute({ component: () => <HostInventoryCard org="acme" hostId="h1" managed={managed} /> });
  const groupRoute = createRoute({
    getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
    path: '/o/$org/groups/$id',
    validateSearch: (s: Record<string, unknown>) => ({ tab: typeof s.tab === 'string' ? s.tab : undefined }),
  });
  const router = createRouter({ routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([groupRoute]), history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/'] }) });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('HostInventoryCard', () => {

    renderCard(false);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Inventory groups need the Managed capability.')).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(await screen.findByText('Not in any inventory group yet.')).toBeInTheDocument();

      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/hosts/h1/inventory/groups': {
        groups: [{ group_id: 'g1', group_name: 'webserver', host_id: 'h1', host_name: 'web-01', vars: {}, complete: false, problems: [{ path: 'domain', message: 'is required' }], orphaned: [], revision: 1 }],
      },
    });
    renderCard(true);
    const link = await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'webserver' });
    expect(link.getAttribute('href')).toBe('/o/acme/groups/g1?tab=members');
    expect(screen.getByText('1 to fill in')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`web/src/api/hooks/useCredentials.ts`:

```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api, unwrap } from '../client.ts';

export interface CredentialInput {
  name: string;
  username?: string;
  expires_at?: string;
  generate?: boolean;
  private_key?: string;
  passphrase?: string;
  password?: string;
  token?: string;
}

function useInvalidate(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['credentials', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['ssh', org] });
  };
}

export function useCredentials(org: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['credentials', org],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials', { params: { path: { org } } })),
    enabled: !!org,
  });
}

export function useCredential(org: string, id: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['credentials', org, id],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!id,
  });
}

/** gcTime: 0 — the variables carry private keys, passwords or tokens. The
 * dialogs also call reset() once the result has been shown. */
export function useCreateCredential(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: CredentialInput & { kind: 'ssh_key' | 'ssh_password' | 'git_key' | 'git_token' }) =>
      unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    gcTime: 0,
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useUpdateCredential(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: CredentialInput) => unwrap(api.PATCH('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } }, body })),
    gcTime: 0,
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteCredential(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidate(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/credentials/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

Append to `web/src/api/hooks/useSsh.ts` (add `useMutation`, `useQueryClient` to its import):

```ts
export interface BastionInput {
  name: string;
  address: string;
  port?: number;
  username: string;
  credential_id?: string;
  via_bastion_id?: string;
}

export interface HostSSHInput {
  username?: string;
  port?: number;
  credential_id?: string;
  bastion_mode?: 'inherit' | 'none' | 'explicit';
  bastion_id?: string;
}

function useInvalidateSsh(org: string) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['ssh', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['zones', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['inventory', org] });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['credentials', org] });
  };
}

export function useCreateBastion(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidateSsh(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: BastionInput) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions', { params: { path: { org } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useUpdateBastion(org: string, id: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidateSsh(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: BastionInput) => unwrap(api.PATCH('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useDeleteBastion(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidateSsh(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/bastions/{id}', { params: { path: { org, id } } })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useSetZoneBastion(org: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidateSsh(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ zoneId, bastionId }: { zoneId: string; bastionId?: string }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/zones/{id}/bastion', { params: { path: { org, id: zoneId } }, body: bastionId ? { bastion_id: bastionId } : {} })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}

export function useHostSSH(org: string, hostId: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['ssh', org, 'host', hostId],
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/ssh', { params: { path: { org, id: hostId } } })),
    enabled: !!org && !!hostId,
  });
}

export function useSetHostSSH(org: string, hostId: string) {
  const invalidate = useInvalidateSsh(org);
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: HostSSHInput) => unwrap(api.PUT('/api/v1/orgs/{org}/hosts/{id}/ssh', { params: { path: { org, id: hostId } }, body })),
    onSuccess: invalidate,
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Labels with tests**

`web/src/features/credentials/labels.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { expiryState, kindLabel, routeLabel, usageLabel } from './labels.ts';

const base = { id: 'c', name: 'k', kind: 'ssh_key', username: '', expired: false, expires_soon: false, used_by: { bastions: 0, hosts: 0 }, created_at: '', updated_at: '' };

describe('credential labels', () => {
  it('names kinds with the key type', () => {
    expect(kindLabel({ ...base, key_type: 'ed25519' })).toBe('SSH key · ed25519');
    expect(kindLabel({ ...base, kind: 'git_token' })).toBe('Git token');
  });

  it('summarises usage', () => {
    expect(usageLabel(base)).toBe('unused');
    expect(usageLabel({ ...base, used_by: { bastions: 1, hosts: 41 } })).toBe('41 hosts · 1 jump host');
  });

  it('turns expiry into a state', () => {
    expect(expiryState(base)).toBeNull();
    expect(expiryState({ ...base, expires_soon: true, expires_at: '2026-10-04T00:00:00Z' }, new Date('2026-09-11T00:00:00Z'))).toEqual({ tone: 'warn', text: 'expires in 23 d' });
    expect(expiryState({ ...base, expired: true })).toEqual({ tone: 'crit', text: 'expired' });
  });

  it('explains where a jump host came from', () => {
    expect(routeLabel('group:production')).toBe('inherited from group production');
    expect(routeLabel('zone')).toBe('zone default');
    expect(routeLabel('host')).toBe('set on this host');
    expect(routeLabel('none')).toBe('direct connection');
  });
});
```

`web/src/features/credentials/labels.ts`:

```ts
import type { Credential } from '../../api/types.ts';
import type { Tone } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';

export const KIND_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  ssh_key: 'SSH key',
  ssh_password: 'SSH password',
  git_key: 'Git deploy key',
  git_token: 'Git token',
};

type CredentialLike = Pick<Credential, 'kind' | 'key_type' | 'used_by' | 'expired' | 'expires_soon' | 'expires_at'>;

export function kindLabel(c: CredentialLike): string {
  const kind = KIND_LABEL[c.kind] ?? c.kind;
  return c.key_type ? `${kind} · ${c.key_type}` : kind;
}

const plural = (n: number, word: string) => `${n} ${word}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;

export function usageLabel(c: CredentialLike): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (c.used_by.hosts) parts.push(plural(c.used_by.hosts, 'host'));
  if (c.used_by.bastions) parts.push(plural(c.used_by.bastions, 'jump host'));
  return parts.join(' · ') || 'unused';
}

/** expiryState is null for a credential that is fine; expiry is a state, so
 * it uses the state colours. */
export function expiryState(c: CredentialLike, now: Date = new Date()): { tone: Tone; text: string } | null {
  if (c.expired) return { tone: 'crit', text: 'expired' };
  if (c.expires_soon && c.expires_at) {
    const days = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(c.expires_at).getTime() - now.getTime()) / 86_400_000));
    return { tone: 'warn', text: `expires in ${days} d` };
  }
  return null;
}

export function routeLabel(source: string): string {
  if (source.startsWith('group:')) return `inherited from group ${source.slice('group:'.length)}`;
  if (source === 'zone') return 'zone default';
  if (source === 'host') return 'set on this host';
  return 'direct connection';
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Copy } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';

interface Snippets {
  authorized_keys_command: string;
  cloud_init: string;
  terraform: string;
}

const SNIPPETS: Array<[keyof Snippets, string]> = [
  ['authorized_keys_command', 'authorized_keys'],
  ['cloud_init', 'cloud-init'],
  ['terraform', 'Terraform'],
];

/** PublicKeyPanel shows the key an operator installs on a host, with the
 * spec §5 snippets. The private key never reaches the browser. */
export function PublicKeyPanel({ publicKey, snippets }: { publicKey: string; snippets?: Snippets | null }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<keyof Snippets>('authorized_keys_command');
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <CopyBlock label="Public key" text={publicKey} />
      {snippets && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div role="tablist" className="flex gap-4 border-b border-border">
            {SNIPPETS.map(([key, label]) => (
              <button
                key={key}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={tab === key}
                className={`border-b-2 py-1.5 text-xs ${tab === key ? 'border-primary font-semibold' : 'border-transparent text-muted-foreground'}`}
                onClick={() => setTab(key)}
              >
                {label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <CopyBlock label={SNIPPETS.find(([k]) => k === tab)?.[1] ?? ''} text={snippets[tab]} />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function CopyBlock({ label, text }: { label: string; text: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>
        <Button type="button" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Copy ${label}`} onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)}>
          <Copy size={14} /> Copy
        </Button>
      </div>
      <pre aria-label={label} className="overflow-x-auto rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-3 font-mono text-[11px] whitespace-pre-wrap break-all">
        {text}
      </pre>
    </div>
  );
}
```
