`web/src/features/credentials/AddCredentialDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useCreateCredential } from '../../api/hooks/useCredentials.ts';
import { KIND_LABEL } from './labels.ts';
import { PublicKeyPanel } from './PublicKeyPanel.tsx';

type Kind = 'ssh_key' | 'ssh_password' | 'git_key' | 'git_token';
const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

interface Created {
  publicKey: string;
  snippets?: { authorized_keys_command: string; cloud_init: string; terraform: string } | null;
}

export function AddCredentialDialog({ org, open, onClose }: { org: string; open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const create = useCreateCredential(org);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<Kind>('ssh_key');
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'generate' | 'import'>('generate');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [expires, setExpires] = useState('');
  const [privateKey, setPrivateKey] = useState('');
  const [passphrase, setPassphrase] = useState('');
  const [secret, setSecret] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [created, setCreated] = useState<Created | null>(null);
  const isKey = kind === 'ssh_key' || kind === 'git_key';

  const clearSecrets = () => {
    setPrivateKey('');
    setPassphrase('');
    setSecret('');
  };

  function close() {
    create.reset();
    clearSecrets();
    setName('');
    setUsername('');
    setExpires('');
    setError(null);
    setCreated(null);
    onClose();
  }

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    const material = isKey
      ? mode === 'generate'
        ? { generate: true }
        : { private_key: privateKey, passphrase: passphrase || undefined }
      : kind === 'ssh_password'
        ? { password: secret }
        : { token: secret };
    try {
      const res = await create.mutateAsync({
        name, kind, username: username || undefined, expires_at: expires ? new Date(`${expires}T00:00:00Z`).toISOString() : undefined, ...material,
      });
      clearSecrets();
      create.reset();
      toast.show(`Credential ${res.credential.name} created`);
      if (res.credential.public_key) setCreated({ publicKey: res.credential.public_key, snippets: res.snippets });
      else close();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Creating the credential failed');
    }
  }

  if (created) {
    return (
      <Dialog open={open} onClose={close} size="wide" title="Install the public key">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">The private key stays sealed in Pulsix and is never shown. Install this public key where the credential logs in.</p>
          <PublicKeyPanel publicKey={created.publicKey} snippets={created.snippets} />
          <div className="flex justify-end">
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={close}>
              Done
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Dialog>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onClose={close} title="Add credential">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="cred-kind">Kind</Label>
          <select id="cred-kind" className={SELECT} value={kind} onChange={(e) => setKind(e.target.value as Kind)}>
            {Object.entries(KIND_LABEL).map(([k, label]) => (
              <option key={k} value={k}>
                {label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="cred-name">Name</Label>
          <Input id="cred-name" className="font-mono" required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="cred-username">Username</Label>
          <Input id="cred-username" className="font-mono" value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} />
          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            {kind.startsWith('ssh') ? 'The login user; a host can name another.' : 'Optional, for example oauth2 for GitLab tokens.'}
          </span>
        </div>
        {isKey && (
          <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <legend className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Key</legend>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              <input type="radio" name="cred-mode" checked={mode === 'generate'} onChange={() => setMode('generate')} /> Generate a new ed25519 key
            </label>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              <input type="radio" name="cred-mode" checked={mode === 'import'} onChange={() => setMode('import')} /> Import a private key
            </label>
            {mode === 'import' && (
              <>
                <Label htmlFor="cred-private-key">Private key</Label>
                <textarea id="cred-private-key" rows={6} required className="rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2 font-mono text-[11px]" value={privateKey} onChange={(e) => setPrivateKey(e.target.value)} />
                <Label htmlFor="cred-passphrase">Passphrase (if the key has one; used once, never stored)</Label>
                <Input id="cred-passphrase" type="password" autoComplete="off" value={passphrase} onChange={(e) => setPassphrase(e.target.value)} />
              </>
            )}
          </fieldset>
        )}
        {!isKey && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="cred-secret">{kind === 'ssh_password' ? 'Password' : 'Token'}</Label>
            <Input id="cred-secret" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={secret} onChange={(e) => setSecret(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="cred-expires">Expires (optional)</Label>
          <Input id="cred-expires" type="date" value={expires} onChange={(e) => setExpires(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" onClick={close}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={create.isPending}>
            Create credential
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/credentials/CredentialDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useCredential, useDeleteCredential, useUpdateCredential } from '../../api/hooks/useCredentials.ts';
import { kindLabel, usageLabel } from './labels.ts';
import { PublicKeyPanel } from './PublicKeyPanel.tsx';

/** CredentialDialog shows one credential: its public key and snippets, its
 * metadata for editing, a way to replace its secret, and delete. */
export function CredentialDialog({ org, id, onClose }: { org: string; id: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const detail = useCredential(org, id);
  const update = useUpdateCredential(org, id);
  const remove = useDeleteCredential(org);
  const c = detail.data?.credential;
  const [name, setName] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [replacement, setReplacement] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!c) {
    return (
      <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title="Credential">
        <QueryLoading />
      </Dialog>
    );
  }
  const isKey = c.kind === 'ssh_key' || c.kind === 'git_key';

  async function save(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    const material = replacement === '' ? {} : isKey ? { private_key: replacement } : c!.kind === 'ssh_password' ? { password: replacement } : { token: replacement };
    try {
      await update.mutateAsync({ name: name ?? c!.name, username: (username ?? c!.username) || undefined, expires_at: c!.expires_at ?? undefined, ...material });
      setReplacement('');
      update.reset();
      toast.show('Credential saved');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
    }
  }

  async function rotateKey() {
    try {
      await update.mutateAsync({ name: c!.name, username: c!.username || undefined, expires_at: c!.expires_at ?? undefined, generate: true });
      update.reset();
      toast.show('New key generated; install the new public key before hosts use it');
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Generating failed');
    }
  }

  async function onDelete() {
    if (!confirm(`Delete credential ${c!.name}?`)) return;
    try {
      await remove.mutateAsync(c!.id);
      toast.show(`Credential ${c!.name} deleted`);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deleting failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} size="wide" title={c.name}>
      <form onSubmit={save} className="flex max-h-[70vh] flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto">
        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          {kindLabel(c)} · {usageLabel(c)}
          {c.fingerprint ? ` · ${c.fingerprint}` : ''}
        </span>
        {c.public_key && <PublicKeyPanel publicKey={c.public_key} snippets={detail.data?.snippets} />}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="cred-edit-name">Name</Label>
            <Input id="cred-edit-name" className="font-mono" value={name ?? c.name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="cred-edit-username">Username</Label>
            <Input id="cred-edit-username" className="font-mono" value={username ?? c.username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="cred-replace">{isKey ? 'Replace with an imported private key' : c.kind === 'ssh_password' ? 'New password' : 'New token'}</Label>
          {isKey ? (
            <textarea id="cred-replace" rows={4} className="rounded-md border border-border bg-background p-2 font-mono text-[11px]" value={replacement} onChange={(e) => setReplacement(e.target.value)} />
          ) : (
            <Input id="cred-replace" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" value={replacement} onChange={(e) => setReplacement(e.target.value)} />
          )}
          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">Leave empty to keep the stored secret.</span>
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <Button type="button" variant="destructive" onClick={() => void onDelete()}>
            Delete
          </Button>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {isKey && (
              <Button type="button" onClick={() => void rotateKey()}>
                Generate new key
              </Button>
            )}
            <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={update.isPending}>
              Save
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/credentials/CredentialsPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { KeyRound } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Pill } from '../../ui/badge.tsx';
import { SettingsTabs } from '../../ui/SettingsTabs.tsx';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useCredentials } from '../../api/hooks/useCredentials.ts';
import { AddCredentialDialog } from './AddCredentialDialog.tsx';
import { CredentialDialog } from './CredentialDialog.tsx';
import { expiryState, kindLabel, usageLabel } from './labels.ts';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 1fr) 190px minmax(0, 1fr) 130px 110px';

export function CredentialsPage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const credentials = useCredentials(org);
  const [addOpen, setAddOpen] = useState(false);
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(credentials);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!credentials.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = credentials.data.credentials ?? [];

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Credentials &amp; secrets</h1>
          <SettingsTabs org={org} />
        </div>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setAddOpen(true)}>
          <KeyRound size={15} /> Add credential
        </Button>
      </div>
      <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">{list.length} credentials · encrypted with the instance master key · values are never shown again</span>
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Name</span>
          <span>Kind</span>
          <span>Used by</span>
          <span>Expiry</span>
          <span>Created</span>
        </GridHeader>
        {list.map((c) => {
          const expiry = expiryState(c);
          return (
            <GridRow key={c.id} columns={COLUMNS}>
              <button type="button" className="truncate text-left font-mono text-[13px] hover:text-primary" onClick={() => setOpenId(c.id)}>
                {c.name}
              </button>
              <span className="text-xs">{kindLabel(c)}</span>
              <span className="truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {c.username ? `${c.username}@ · ` : ''}
                {usageLabel(c)}
              </span>
              <span>{expiry ? <Pill tone={expiry.tone}>{expiry.text}</Pill> : <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">—</span>}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
            </GridRow>
          );
        })}
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No credentials yet.</p>}
      </Card>
      <AddCredentialDialog org={org} open={addOpen} onClose={() => setAddOpen(false)} />
      {openId && <CredentialDialog org={org} id={openId} onClose={() => setOpenId(null)} />}
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/credentials/CredentialsPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { CredentialsPage } from './CredentialsPage.tsx';

const cred = (over: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
  id: 'c1', name: 'acme-deploy', kind: 'ssh_key', username: 'ansible', public_key: 'ssh-ed25519 AAAA pulsix:acme:acme-deploy',
  fingerprint: 'SHA256:abc', key_type: 'ed25519', expired: false, expires_soon: false, used_by: { bastions: 1, hosts: 41 },
  created_at: '2026-06-01T00:00:00Z', updated_at: '2026-06-01T00:00:00Z', ...over,
});
// SettingsTabs asks who is signed in (to decide on the instance-admin tab).
const ME = { id: 'u', email: 'a@b', display_name: 'A', instance_admin: false };
const SNIPPETS = { authorized_keys_command: "echo 'ssh-ed25519 AAAA pulsix:acme:acme-deploy' >> ~/.ssh/authorized_keys", cloud_init: '#cloud-config', terraform: 'locals {}' };

function renderPage() {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const orgRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '/o/$org' });
  const settings = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/settings' });
  const page = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => settings, path: '/credentials', component: CredentialsPage });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([settings.addChildren([page])])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/o/acme/settings/credentials'] }),
  });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

describe('CredentialsPage', () => {
  it('lists credentials with kind, usage and expiry', async () => {
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': ME,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': {
        credentials: [cred({}), cred({ id: 'c2', name: 'gitlab-ops', kind: 'git_token', public_key: undefined, key_type: undefined, expires_soon: true, expires_at: '2099-01-01T00:00:00Z', used_by: { bastions: 0, hosts: 0 } })],
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    expect(await screen.findByText('SSH key · ed25519')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/41 hosts · 1 jump host/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/expires in/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('generates a key and shows its public key and snippets once', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let posted: unknown;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': ME,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': { credentials: [] },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': (req: MockRequest) => {
        posted = req.body;
        return { credential: cred({ used_by: { bastions: 0, hosts: 0 } }), snippets: SNIPPETS };
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Add credential' }));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'acme-deploy');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Username'), 'ansible');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create credential' }));
    expect(posted).toEqual({ name: 'acme-deploy', kind: 'ssh_key', username: 'ansible', generate: true });
    const panel = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Install the public key' });
    expect(within(panel).getByLabelText('Public key')).toHaveTextContent('pulsix:acme:acme-deploy');
    expect(within(panel).getByLabelText('authorized_keys')).toHaveTextContent('authorized_keys');
    await user.click(within(panel).getByRole('tab', { name: 'cloud-init' }));
    expect(within(panel).getByLabelText('cloud-init')).toHaveTextContent('#cloud-config');
    await user.click(within(panel).getByRole('button', { name: 'Done' }));
  });

  it('creates a password credential and sends the password only once', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let posted: unknown;
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': ME,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': { credentials: [] },
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': (req: MockRequest) => {
        posted = req.body;
        return { credential: cred({ kind: 'ssh_password', public_key: undefined, key_type: undefined, name: 'fs-admin' }) };
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Add credential' }));
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Kind'), 'ssh_password');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'fs-admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Password'), 'hunter2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create credential' }));
    expect(posted).toEqual({ name: 'fs-admin', kind: 'ssh_password', password: 'hunter2' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Credential fs-admin created')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByDisplayValue('hunter2')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renames a credential and reports a refused delete', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let patched: unknown;
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    mockApi({
      'GET /api/v1/auth/me': ME,
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': { credentials: [cred({})] },
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials/c1': { credential: cred({}), snippets: SNIPPETS },
      'PATCH /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials/c1': (req: MockRequest) => {
        patched = req.body;
        return { credential: cred({ name: 'acme-deploy-2' }), snippets: SNIPPETS };
      },
      'DELETE /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials/c1': () =>
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 409, detail: 'the credential is still used by a jump host or a host' }), { status: 409, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }),
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'acme-deploy' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'acme-deploy' });
    const name = within(dialog).getByLabelText('Name');
    await user.clear(name);
    await user.type(name, 'acme-deploy-2');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Credential saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(patched).toEqual({ name: 'acme-deploy-2', username: 'ansible' });
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete' }));
    expect(await within(dialog).findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('still used');
  });
});
```

`SettingsTabs` calls `useMe` (`GET /api/v1/auth/me`); if the `ME` shape above lacks a field `useMe` requires, copy the shape `TokensPage.test.tsx` mocks.

`web/src/features/credentials/BastionDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Dialog } from '../../ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useCreateBastion, useUpdateBastion } from '../../api/hooks/useSsh.ts';
import type { Bastion, Credential } from '../../api/types.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

export function BastionDialog({
  org, bastion, bastions, credentials, onClose,
}: {
  org: string;
  bastion?: Bastion;
  bastions: readonly Bastion[];
  credentials: readonly Credential[];
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const create = useCreateBastion(org);
  const update = useUpdateBastion(org, bastion?.id ?? '');
  const [name, setName] = useState(bastion?.name ?? '');
  const [address, setAddress] = useState(bastion?.address ?? '');
  const [port, setPort] = useState(String(bastion?.port ?? 22));
  const [username, setUsername] = useState(bastion?.username ?? '');
  const [credentialId, setCredentialId] = useState(bastion?.credential_id ?? '');
  const [via, setVia] = useState(bastion?.via_bastion_id ?? '');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    const body = { name, address, port: Number(port) || 22, username, credential_id: credentialId || undefined, via_bastion_id: via || undefined };
    try {
      if (bastion) await update.mutateAsync(body);
      else await create.mutateAsync(body);
      toast.show(`Jump host ${name} saved`);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onClose={onClose} title={bastion ? `Jump host ${bastion.name}` : 'Add jump host'}>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="bastion-name">Name</Label>
          <Input id="bastion-name" className="font-mono" required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_90px] gap-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="bastion-address">Address</Label>
            <Input id="bastion-address" className="font-mono" required value={address} onChange={(e) => setAddress(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="bastion-port">Port</Label>
            <Input id="bastion-port" className="font-mono" inputMode="numeric" value={port} onChange={(e) => setPort(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="bastion-username">Username</Label>
          <Input id="bastion-username" className="font-mono" required value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="bastion-credential">Credential</Label>
          <select id="bastion-credential" className={SELECT} value={credentialId} onChange={(e) => setCredentialId(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">— none —</option>
            {credentials.filter((c) => c.kind === 'ssh_key' || c.kind === 'ssh_password').map((c) => (
              <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                {c.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="bastion-via">Reached through</Label>
          <select id="bastion-via" className={SELECT} value={via} onChange={(e) => setVia(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">— directly —</option>
            {bastions.filter((b) => b.id !== bastion?.id).map((b) => (
              <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                {b.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        {error && (
          <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button type="button" onClick={onClose}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
            Save jump host
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/credentials/JumpHostsPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { Server } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, GridHeader, GridRow } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { SettingsTabs } from '../../ui/SettingsTabs.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useQueryGuard } from '../../ui/useQueryGuard.tsx';
import { QueryLoading } from '../../ui/QueryState.tsx';
import { useBastions, useDeleteBastion, useSetZoneBastion } from '../../api/hooks/useSsh.ts';
import { useCredentials } from '../../api/hooks/useCredentials.ts';
import { useZones } from '../../api/hooks/useHosts.ts';
import type { Bastion } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { BastionDialog } from './BastionDialog.tsx';

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1.4fr) 160px 160px 120px';
const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

export function JumpHostsPage() {
  const { org } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { org: string };
  const toast = useToast();
  const bastions = useBastions(org);
  const credentials = useCredentials(org);
  const zones = useZones(org);
  const remove = useDeleteBastion(org);
  const setZone = useSetZoneBastion(org);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<{ bastion?: Bastion } | null>(null);

  const guard = useQueryGuard(bastions);
  if (guard) return guard;
  if (!bastions.data) return <QueryLoading />;
  const list = bastions.data.bastions ?? [];
  // A viewer may read jump hosts but not credentials; names fall back to "—".
  const creds = credentials.data?.credentials ?? [];
  const bastionName = (id?: string | null) => list.find((b) => b.id === id)?.name ?? 'directly';

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Credentials &amp; secrets</h1>
          <SettingsTabs org={org} />
        </div>
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => setDialog({})}>
          <Server size={15} /> Add jump host
        </Button>
      </div>
      <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">Resolution: host → inventory group → zone default. Chains are rendered as ProxyJump.</span>
      <Card>
        <GridHeader columns={COLUMNS}>
          <span>Name</span>
          <span>Login</span>
          <span>Reached</span>
          <span>Credential</span>
          <span />
        </GridHeader>
        {list.map((b) => (
          <GridRow key={b.id} columns={COLUMNS}>
            <span className="font-mono text-[13px]">{b.name}</span>
            <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{`${b.username}@${b.address}:${b.port}`}</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{b.via_bastion_id ? `via ${bastionName(b.via_bastion_id)}` : 'directly'}</span>
            <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">{creds.find((c) => c.id === b.credential_id)?.name ?? '—'}</span>
            <span className="flex justify-end gap-1">
              <Button variant="ghost" aria-label={`Edit ${b.name}`} onClick={() => setDialog({ bastion: b })}>
                Edit
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                aria-label={`Delete ${b.name}`}
                onClick={async () => {
                  if (!confirm(`Delete jump host ${b.name}?`)) return;
                  try {
                    await remove.mutateAsync(b.id);
                    toast.show(`Jump host ${b.name} deleted`);
                  } catch (err) {
                    toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Deleting failed', 'destructive');
                  }
                }}
              >
                Delete
              </Button>
            </span>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
        {list.length === 0 && <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">No jump hosts yet.</p>}
      </Card>

      <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold">Zone defaults</h2>
      <Card>
        {(zones.data?.zones ?? []).map((z) => (
          <GridRow key={z.id} columns="minmax(0, 1fr) 260px">
            <label htmlFor={`zone-${z.id}`} className="text-[13px]">
              {z.name} <span className="font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground">{z.slug}</span>
            </label>
            <select
              id={`zone-${z.id}`}
              className={SELECT}
              value={z.bastion_id ?? ''}
              onChange={async (e) => {
                await setZone.mutateAsync({ zoneId: z.id, bastionId: e.target.value || undefined });
                toast.show(`Default jump host of ${z.name} saved`);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">— direct connection —</option>
              {list.map((b) => (
                <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                  {b.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </GridRow>
        ))}
      </Card>
      {dialog && <BastionDialog org={org} bastion={dialog.bastion} bastions={list} credentials={creds} onClose={() => setDialog(null)} />}
    </>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/credentials/JumpHostsPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createMemoryHistory, createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter, RouterProvider } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { JumpHostsPage } from './JumpHostsPage.tsx';

const ME = { id: 'u', email: 'a@b', display_name: 'A', instance_admin: false };
const BASTIONS = {
  bastions: [
    { id: 'b1', name: 'bastion-a', address: 'bastion-a.example', port: 22, username: 'ops', credential_id: 'c1' },
    { id: 'b2', name: 'bastion-b', address: 'bastion-b.example', port: 2222, username: 'ops', via_bastion_id: 'b1' },
  ],
};
const CREDS = { credentials: [{ id: 'c1', name: 'inv-deploy', kind: 'ssh_key', username: 'ansible', expired: false, expires_soon: false, used_by: { bastions: 1, hosts: 0 }, created_at: '', updated_at: '' }] };
const ZONES = { zones: [{ id: 'z1', slug: 'primary', name: 'Primary' }] };

function renderPage() {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  const rootRoute = createRootRoute();
  const orgRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '/o/$org' });
  const settings = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => orgRoute, path: '/settings' });
  const page = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => settings, path: '/jump-hosts', component: JumpHostsPage });
  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree: rootRoute.addChildren([orgRoute.addChildren([settings.addChildren([page])])]),
    history: createMemoryHistory({ initialEntries: ['/o/acme/settings/jump-hosts'] }),
  });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <RouterProvider router={router} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

const base = {
  'GET /api/v1/auth/me': ME,
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions': BASTIONS,
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': CREDS,
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/zones': ZONES,
};

describe('JumpHostsPage', () => {
  it('lists jump hosts with their login, chain and credential', async () => {
    mockApi(base);
    renderPage();
    expect(await screen.findByText('ops@bastion-a.example:22')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('via bastion-a')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('inv-deploy')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('adds a chained jump host', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let posted: unknown;
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'POST /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions': (req: MockRequest) => {
        posted = req.body;
        return { bastion: { id: 'b3', name: 'edge', address: 'edge.example', port: 2222, username: 'ops' } };
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Add jump host' }));
    const dialog = screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add jump host' });
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Name'), 'edge');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Address'), 'edge.example');
    await user.clear(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Port'));
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Port'), '2222');
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Username'), 'ops');
    await user.selectOptions(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Credential'), 'c1');
    await user.selectOptions(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Reached through'), 'b1');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Save jump host' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Jump host edge saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(posted).toEqual({ name: 'edge', address: 'edge.example', port: 2222, username: 'ops', credential_id: 'c1', via_bastion_id: 'b1' });
  });

  it('sets and clears a zone default', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const bodies: unknown[] = [];
    let zoneBastion: string | undefined;
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      // The select shows what the server stores, so the mock stores it too.
      'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/zones': () => ({ zones: [{ ...ZONES.zones[0], bastion_id: zoneBastion }] }),
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/zones/z1/bastion': (req: MockRequest) => {
        bodies.push(req.body);
        zoneBastion = (req.body as { bastion_id?: string }).bastion_id;
        return {};
      },
    });
    renderPage();
    const select = await screen.findByLabelText(/Primary/);
    await user.selectOptions(select, 'b1');
    await waitFor(() => expect(select).toHaveValue('b1'));
    await user.selectOptions(select, '');
    await waitFor(() => expect(select).toHaveValue(''));
    expect(bodies).toEqual([{ bastion_id: 'b1' }, {}]);
  });

  it('shows why a jump host cannot be deleted', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    vi.spyOn(window, 'confirm').mockReturnValue(true);
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'DELETE /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions/b1': () =>
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 409, detail: 'the jump host is still used by a zone, group, host or another jump host' }), {
          status: 409, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        }),
    });
    renderPage();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Delete bastion-a' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/still used by a zone/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`web/src/features/hosts/HostSshCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Card } from '../../ui/table.tsx';
import { Button } from '../../ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '../../ui/input.tsx';
import { Label } from '../../ui/label.tsx';
import { useToast } from '../../ui/toastContext.ts';
import { useBastions, useHostSSH, useSetHostSSH } from '../../api/hooks/useSsh.ts';
import { useCredential, useCredentials } from '../../api/hooks/useCredentials.ts';
import { useHostInventoryPreview } from '../../api/hooks/useInventory.ts';
import type { Bastion, Credential, HostSSH } from '../../api/types.ts';
import { PublicKeyPanel } from '../credentials/PublicKeyPanel.tsx';
import { routeLabel } from '../credentials/labels.ts';

const SELECT = 'h-8 w-full rounded-md border border-border bg-background px-2 text-xs text-foreground';

/** HostSshCard is the SSH access step of the enrollment mockup for a
 * managed host: how Pulsix logs in, which jump host it goes through (and
 * why), and the public key to install. */
export function HostSshCard({ org, hostId, managed }: { org: string; hostId: string; managed: boolean }) {
  const ssh = useHostSSH(org, managed ? hostId : '');
  const bastions = useBastions(org);
  const credentials = useCredentials(org);
  if (!managed) return null;
  if (!ssh.data) return null;
  return (
    <HostSshForm
      key={JSON.stringify(ssh.data.ssh)}
      org={org}
      hostId={hostId}
      current={ssh.data.ssh}
      bastions={bastions.data?.bastions ?? []}
      credentials={(credentials.data?.credentials ?? []).filter((c) => c.kind === 'ssh_key' || c.kind === 'ssh_password')}
    />
  );
}

function HostSshForm({ org, hostId, current, bastions, credentials }: { org: string; hostId: string; current: HostSSH; bastions: readonly Bastion[]; credentials: readonly Credential[] }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const save = useSetHostSSH(org, hostId);
  const preview = useHostInventoryPreview(org, hostId);
  const [username, setUsername] = useState(current.username);
  const [port, setPort] = useState(String(current.port));
  const [credentialId, setCredentialId] = useState(current.credential_id ?? '');
  const [mode, setMode] = useState(current.bastion_mode);
  const [bastionId, setBastionId] = useState(current.bastion_id ?? '');
  const selected = credentials.find((c) => c.id === credentialId);
  const keyDetail = useCredential(org, selected?.kind === 'ssh_key' ? selected.id : '');
  const connection = preview.data?.connection;
  const names = (connection?.bastions ?? []).map((id) => bastions.find((b) => b.id === id)?.name ?? id.slice(0, 8));

  async function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({
        username: username || undefined, port: Number(port) || 22, credential_id: credentialId || undefined,
        bastion_mode: mode, bastion_id: mode === 'explicit' ? bastionId || undefined : undefined,
      });
      toast.show('SSH settings saved');
    } catch (err) {
      toast.show(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Saving failed', 'destructive');
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <h2 className="mb-3 text-[13px] font-semibold">SSH access</h2>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_90px] gap-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="ssh-user">User</Label>
            <Input id="ssh-user" className="font-mono" placeholder={selected?.username || 'from the credential'} value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <Label htmlFor="ssh-port">Port</Label>
            <Input id="ssh-port" className="font-mono" inputMode="numeric" value={port} onChange={(e) => setPort(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <Label htmlFor="ssh-credential">Credential</Label>
          <select id="ssh-credential" className={SELECT} value={credentialId} onChange={(e) => setCredentialId(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">— none —</option>
            {credentials.map((c) => (
              <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                {c.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        <fieldset className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <legend className="text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground">Jump host</legend>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <input type="radio" name="ssh-mode" checked={mode === 'inherit'} onChange={() => setMode('inherit')} /> Inherit (inventory group, then zone)
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <input type="radio" name="ssh-mode" checked={mode === 'none'} onChange={() => setMode('none')} /> Direct connection
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            <input type="radio" name="ssh-mode" checked={mode === 'explicit'} onChange={() => setMode('explicit')} /> This jump host
          </label>
          {mode === 'explicit' && (
            <select aria-label="Jump host for this host" className={SELECT} value={bastionId} onChange={(e) => setBastionId(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="">Choose…</option>
              {bastions.map((b) => (
                <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>
                  {b.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </fieldset>
        {connection && (
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" aria-label="Effective route">
            Route: {routeLabel(connection.bastion_source)}
            {names.length > 0 && <span className="font-mono"> · {names.join(' → ')} → host</span>}
          </p>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end">
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={save.isPending}>
            Save SSH settings
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
      {keyDetail.data?.credential.public_key && (
        <div className="mt-4 border-t border-border pt-4">
          <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold">Install this key on the host</h3>
          <PublicKeyPanel publicKey={keyDetail.data.credential.public_key} snippets={keyDetail.data.snippets} />
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

Render `<HostSshCard org={org} hostId={h.id} managed={h.managed} />` in `HostDetailPage.tsx` next to the inventory card, and add the new GETs (`/hosts/h1/ssh`, `/bastions`, `/credentials`, `/hosts/h1/inventory/preview`) to its test mocks where the host is managed.

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ToastProvider } from '../../ui/toast.tsx';
import { mockApi, type MockRequest } from '../../test/mockApi.ts';
import { HostSshCard } from './HostSshCard.tsx';

const KEY = { id: 'c1', name: 'inv-deploy', kind: 'ssh_key', username: 'ansible', public_key: 'ssh-ed25519 AAAA pulsix:acme:inv-deploy', expired: false, expires_soon: false, used_by: { bastions: 0, hosts: 0 }, created_at: '', updated_at: '' };

function renderCard(managed: boolean) {
  const qc = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={qc}>
      <ToastProvider>
        <HostSshCard org="acme" hostId="h1" managed={managed} />
      </ToastProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

const base = {
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/hosts/h1/ssh': { ssh: { host_id: 'h1', username: '', port: 22, bastion_mode: 'inherit' } },
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/bastions': {
    bastions: [
      { id: 'b1', name: 'bastion-a', address: 'a.example', port: 22, username: 'ops' },
      { id: 'b2', name: 'bastion-b', address: 'b.example', port: 2222, username: 'ops', via_bastion_id: 'b1' },
    ],
  },
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials': { credentials: [KEY] },
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/credentials/c1': { credential: KEY, snippets: { authorized_keys_command: 'echo', cloud_init: '#cloud-config', terraform: 'locals {}' } },
  'GET /api/v1/orgs/acme/hosts/h1/inventory/preview': {
    vars: { ansible_host: 'web-01' },
    connection: { host_id: 'h1', bastion_source: 'group:production', bastions: ['b1', 'b2'] },
    warnings: [],
  },
};

describe('HostSshCard', () => {
  it('renders nothing for a host that is not managed', () => {
    mockApi(base);
    renderCard(false);
    expect(screen.queryByText('SSH access')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the resolved route, saves an explicit jump host, and offers the key to install', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    let put: unknown;
    mockApi({
      ...base,
      'PUT /api/v1/orgs/acme/hosts/h1/ssh': (req: MockRequest) => {
        put = req.body;
        return { ssh: { host_id: 'h1', username: '', port: 22, credential_id: 'c1', bastion_mode: 'explicit', bastion_id: 'b1' } };
      },
    });
    renderCard(true);
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Effective route')).toHaveTextContent('Route: inherited from group production · bastion-a → bastion-b → host');

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Credential'), 'c1');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Install this key on the host')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Public key')).toHaveTextContent('pulsix:acme:inv-deploy');

    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText(/This jump host/));
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Jump host for this host'), 'b1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save SSH settings' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('SSH settings saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(put).toEqual({ port: 22, credential_id: 'c1', bastion_mode: 'explicit', bastion_id: 'b1' });
  });
});
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `web/playwright.config.ts` (project `inventory`, after `tenancy`)
- Modify (docs repo `~/_dev/noix-docs`, branch `pulsix`, commit locally, **do not push**): `src/content/docs/pulsix/user/inventory-and-variables.md`, `src/content/docs/pulsix/user/hosts.md`, `src/content/docs/pulsix/user/team-and-roles.md`, `src/content/docs/pulsix/admin/security.md`, `src/content/docs/pulsix/reference/changelog.md`; create `src/content/docs/pulsix/user/credentials-and-jump-hosts.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: The end-to-end journey**

In `web/playwright.config.ts` add `{ name: 'inventory', testMatch: /inventory\.spec\.ts/, dependencies: ['tenancy'] }`.

`web/e2e/inventory.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { test, expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { ADMIN_STORAGE_STATE, ORG } from './env.ts';

test.use({ storageState: ADMIN_STORAGE_STATE });

const base = `/o/${ORG.slug}`;

async function toast(page: Page, text: string | RegExp) {
  await expect(page.getByText(text).first()).toBeVisible();
}

test('build an inventory with a schema, a jump host and a generated key, assign a host, and preview what Ansible receives', async ({ page }) => {
  // A zone and a managed host of this journey's own.
  await page.goto(`${base}/hosts`);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add zone' }).click();
  let dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add zone' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Name').fill('Inventory');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Slug').fill('inv');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create zone' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Zone Inventory created');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add host' }).click();
  dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add host' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Name').fill('inv-web-01');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Zone').selectOption({ label: 'Inventory' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Managed (Ansible via SSH)').check();
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create host' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Host inv-web-01 created');

  // A generated SSH key: the public key is shown once with snippets; no private key ever.
  await page.goto(`${base}/settings/credentials`);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add credential' }).click();
  dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add credential' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Name').fill('inv-deploy');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Username').fill('ansible');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create credential' }).click();
  const keyDialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Install the public key' });
  await expect(keyDialog.getByLabel('Public key')).toContainText(`pulsix:${ORG.slug}:inv-deploy`);
  await expect(page.locator('body')).not.toContainText('PRIVATE KEY');
  await keyDialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Done' }).click();

  // A jump host that uses the key.
  await page.goto(`${base}/settings/jump-hosts`);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add jump host' }).click();
  dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add jump host' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Name').fill('inv-bastion');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Address').fill('bastion.inv.example');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Port').fill('2222');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Username').fill('ops');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Credential').selectOption({ label: 'inv-deploy' });
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save jump host' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Jump host inv-bastion saved');

  // Parent group with a required group field and the jump host.
  await page.goto(`${base}/groups`);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add group' }).click();
  dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add group' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Name').fill('inv_production');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create group' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add field' }).click();
  let editor = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field field_1' });
  await editor.getByLabel('Name').fill('env');
  editor = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field env' });
  await editor.getByLabel('Required').first().check();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save schema' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Schema saved');
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Group values' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('env').fill('prod');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save values' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Group values saved');
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Settings' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Jump host').selectOption({ label: 'inv-bastion' });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save', exact: true }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Group saved');

  // Child group with a host-scoped repeater holding a secret.
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Children' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add child group' }).click();
  dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Add child group' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Name').fill('inv_web');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create group' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add field' }).click();
  editor = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field field_1' });
  await editor.getByLabel('Name').fill('vhosts');
  editor = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field vhosts' });
  await editor.getByLabel('Type').first().selectOption('repeater');
  await editor.getByLabel(/Host \(asked/).check();
  await editor.getByLabel('Required').first().check();
  const sub = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field value' });
  await sub.getByLabel('Name').fill('domain');
  await page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field domain' }).getByLabel('Required').check();
  await editor.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add sub-field' }).click();
  const pw = page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field field_2' });
  await pw.getByLabel('Name').fill('password');
  await page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Field password' }).getByLabel('Type').selectOption('secret');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save schema' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Schema saved');

  // Assign the host: a missing required value is rejected at its field.
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Members' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Assign host' }).click();
  dialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Assign host to inv_web' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Host').selectOption({ label: 'inv-web-01' });
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Assign to inv_web' }).click();
  await expect(dialog.getByRole('alert').first()).toContainText('is required');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Add vhosts' }).click();
  await dialog.getByLabel('domain').fill('shop.acme.io');
  await dialog.getByLabel('password').fill('e2e-hunter2');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Assign to inv_web' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'Host assigned to inv_web');
  await expect(page.getByText('complete')).toBeVisible();

  // The preview renders the jump host chain and masks the secret.
  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Preview inventory' }).click();
  const json = page.getByLabel('Inventory JSON');
  await expect(json).toContainText('-o ProxyJump=ops@bastion.inv.example:2222');
  await expect(json).toContainText('"password": "********"');
  await expect(json).toContainText('"env": "prod"');
  await expect(page.locator('body')).not.toContainText('e2e-hunter2');

  // The host page shows its groups, the resolved route, and the key to install.
  await page.goto(`${base}/hosts`);
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'inv-web-01', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: 'inv_web' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByLabel('Credential').selectOption({ label: 'inv-deploy' });
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save SSH settings' }).click();
  await toast(page, 'SSH settings saved');
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Effective route')).toContainText('inherited from group inv_production');
  await expect(page.getByText('Install this key on the host')).toBeVisible();
});
```

Run the suite exactly as CI does (see `web/playwright.config.ts` and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` for the Compose test stack it starts):

Run: `cd web && npm run e2e`
Expected: every project passes, including `inventory`. On failure, open the trace (`npx playwright show-trace test-results/…/trace.zip`) and fix the product or the selector, not the assertion. Selectors that depend on the order of nested editors (`.first()`) are deliberate; if a selector turns out ambiguous, scope it to the right group instead of adding `.first()` elsewhere.

- [ ] **Step 2: End-user documentation (docs repo, local only)**

Work in `~/_dev/noix-docs` on branch `pulsix`. Public text rules apply: no tech-stack names (Ansible is the product's subject and stays). Make each page describe what v0.2a actually ships, and say plainly what arrives with the job runner.

- `user/inventory-and-variables.md`: keep the structure; correct these points to match the implementation:
  - group names use lowercase letters, digits and underscores, and `all`/`ungrouped` are reserved;
  - field names must not start with `ansible_` or `pulsix_`;
  - collections use *min items*/*max items*;
  - host-scoped fields are asked only for hosts assigned directly to that group; child groups inherit the group values through Ansible's group inheritance;
  - what Ansible receives: each group's values as that group's vars, each host's assignment values and overrides as its host vars, merged across groups by depth and then name with a preview warning when two groups disagree, facts under `pulsix_facts`;
  - secret values show as stored and are never displayed again; a moved list entry keeps its secret;
  - concurrent edits: saving after someone else saved asks you to reload;
  - replace the availability note: groups, schemas, values and the preview ship now; running playbooks arrives with the job runner.
- `user/credentials-and-jump-hosts.md` (new, `sidebar.order` right after hosts): credential kinds; generating or importing a key (passphrase used once, never stored); the public key with its `pulsix:<org>:<name>` comment and the three snippets; expiry warnings; "used by" and why a used credential cannot be deleted; jump hosts, chaining through another jump host (up to four hops), the resolution order host → group → zone → direct, and where each is set.
- `user/hosts.md` › *SSH access and jump hosts*: the SSH access card (user, port, credential, jump host mode, effective route, key to install); state that the connection test, host-key pinning and the one-time bootstrap arrive with the job runner.
- `user/team-and-roles.md`: add the new abilities to the role table (viewers read inventory; operators edit inventory and read credentials; admins manage credentials and jump hosts).
- `admin/security.md` › *Secrets*: credentials and secret inventory values are encrypted with the instance master key and bound to the record they belong to; they are never returned by the interface or the API.
- `reference/changelog.md` › *Unreleased*: one line per user-visible feature of this plan.

Build the docs site locally (`npm run build` in the docs repo) to catch broken links, then:

```bash
git commit -m "docs(pulsix): inventory groups, credentials, jump hosts and host SSH access

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```
